| [0122](0122-predrawn-occlusion-derives-from-canonical-raised-geometry.md) | Pre-drawn occlusion derives per-depth alpha masks from canonical props, fences, posts, walls, and wall art; strictly nearer masks erase isolated additive scene draws to reveal the unchanged plate, with owner-visible before/after and magenta seed proofs and no pixel inference or persisted seed | superseded by [0158](0158-immutable-predrawn-background-versions-own-derived-raster-and-occlusion.md) | 2026-07-14 |
| [0123](0123-accepted-predrawn-scenes-keep-their-pixels-and-saved-alignment.md) | An accepted pre-drawn scene keeps the exact approved bytes at their actual dimensions; the Level stores its stable media slot plus the exact versioned whole-image alignment payload, including a pinned boundary that remains display-only, while preview identity and browser state stay outside canonical content; camera room is reviewed from the composition rather than enforced through a 3840x2160 frame | superseded by [0158](0158-immutable-predrawn-background-versions-own-derived-raster-and-occlusion.md); had superseded [0118](0118-predrawn-scenes-generate-a-camera-overscan-frame.md) and partially superseded the production-registration clauses of ADR-0076, ADR-0134, ADR-0135, ADR-0110, and ADR-0111 | 2026-07-14 |
| [0124](0124-edge-barriers-close-diagonals-only-when-both-routes-are-blocked.md) | Edge barriers use open-versus-closed corner geometry: a direct orthogonal crossing is blocked by its shared edge, a diagonal is blocked only when both orthogonal routes around the corner are blocked, and non-adjacent jumps such as knights continue to hop | accepted | 2026-07-14 |
| [0125](0125-predrawn-preparation-self-validates-before-generation.md) | Pre-drawn preparation derives and fail-closed validates the canonical definition, top-only reference, prompt, packet, and hashes, then reports `ready-for-generation` without an owner checkpoint; mandatory owner judgment begins only when an actual generated candidate is mounted on the game-owned review surface | accepted; per-run canonical recapture partially superseded by [0165](0165-ai-artwork-separates-sources-attempts-and-background-mode.md); refines ADR-0071, ADR-0109, and ADR-0120 | 2026-07-14 |
| [0126](0126-scenic-terrain-preserves-boundary-topology-in-one-depth-pass.md) | Scenic terrain projects exact clamped playable-boundary occupancy so edge voids remain open through every added row or column; explicit scenic cells override only their own coordinates; rendering, selection, and generation share that topology; playable and scenic terrain render in one frame-zero depth pass without changing gameplay projection | accepted; extent-growth non-propagation clause partially superseded by ADR-0127; partially supersedes ADR-0096 nearest-available synthesis and separate-canvas clauses | 2026-07-16 |
| [0127](0127-scenic-terrain-extent-growth-copies-the-authored-canvas-edge.md) | Each cardinal Scenic terrain rectangle increase preserves existing destinations, otherwise copies explicit terrain from the exact aligned old whole-canvas edge into the new band, and leaves destinations unpainted when that exact source is unpainted; repeated one-cell growth chains through copied authored cells without changing runtime synthesis or gameplay projection | accepted; single-strategy requirement partially superseded by ADR-0129; partially supersedes ADR-0126 explicit scenic-cell non-propagation during extent growth | 2026-07-16 |
| [0128](0128-level-editor-secondary-drag-is-pan-only.md) | Level Editor primary-button gestures perform the active tool action, secondary-button gestures are pan-only everywhere in the board viewport, and erasure requires the explicit Erase tool; no movement threshold or target-local right-click erase may make navigation destructive | accepted | 2026-07-18 |
| [0129](0129-level-editor-terrain-authoring-is-explicit-and-area-scoped.md) | Scenic terrain growth explicitly chooses exact aligned reference matching or canonical grass fill, while Tile shares the resolved connected-area selector and atomically stamps the exact selected tile across playable and scenic destinations without creating Generate records | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0127 single-strategy requirement | 2026-07-18 |
| [0130](0130-visible-scenic-terrain-fill-is-a-sparse-projected-surface.md) | Scenic terrain remains an optional cardinal rectangle plus valid explicit decorative terrain coordinates; Fill visible area uses the live shared viewport and exact isometric tile-diamond intersection to author only visible non-playable cells in the current Match-reference or Grass mode, with stable camera anchoring and one atomic Undo | superseded by [0131](0131-sparse-scenic-terrain-separates-footprint-from-material.md) | 2026-07-18 |
| [0131](0131-sparse-scenic-terrain-separates-footprint-from-material.md) | Scenic terrain activity is the union of its optional cardinal rectangle and a compact persisted decorative footprint, while decorativeCells remains the sole material store; Fill visible area atomically authors only exact viewport-intersecting non-playable coordinates without reactivating hidden shrink-preserved material | accepted; supersedes ADR-0130 and extends ADR-0126/0129 | 2026-07-18 |
| [0132](0132-admins-may-direct-read-editor-documents.md) | An authenticated allowlisted administrator may open an existing Level Editor document by its exact opaque ID, without gaining cross-owner listing, resolve, create, autosave, Save, Discard, or Delete access; missing IDs remain not found and the normal document URL is the only review handoff | accepted | 2026-07-19 |
| [0133](0133-pre-drawn-boards-use-one-registered-live-media-plate.md) | A pre-drawn board renders one complete live-media plate registered to the canonical board frame; authored level geometry remains gameplay-authoritative, baked-art editing is locked, and the live grid, units, doodads, cover, and tactical overlays remain above it | superseded by [0134](0134-predrawn-candidate-review-uses-exact-board-plane-registration.md) | 2026-07-13 |
| [0134](0134-predrawn-candidate-review-uses-exact-board-plane-registration.md) | Pre-drawn candidate review registers the one complete plate with an exact four-corner homography; an evidence-reporting top-plane line detector derives obscured corners and fails closed | accepted; automatic corner authority superseded by [0135](0135-predrawn-registration-is-owner-picked-source-geometry.md), four-corner-only correction by [0110](0110-owner-fitted-grid-defines-predrawn-review-rectification.md), production registration now materializes the raster child required by [0158](0158-immutable-predrawn-background-versions-own-derived-raster-and-occlusion.md), and shared-intersection correction prohibition partially superseded by [0171](0171-local-predrawn-grid-correction-uses-a-shared-vertex-mesh.md) | 2026-07-13 |
| [0135](0135-predrawn-registration-is-owner-picked-source-geometry.md) | Pre-drawn candidate registration is an owner-operated in-app instrument over the untouched source: place and nudge N/E/S/W handles, then apply the stable source-pixel payload to the temporary URL for immediate grid comparison; automatic detection may seed but never outrank owner-picked points | accepted; separate Apply transaction superseded by [0107](0107-predrawn-registration-has-no-unsaved-dialog-state.md), corner-only instrument by [0110](0110-owner-fitted-grid-defines-predrawn-review-rectification.md), production registration now materializes the raster child required by [0158](0158-immutable-predrawn-background-versions-own-derived-raster-and-occlusion.md), and shared-intersection correction prohibition partially superseded by [0171](0171-local-predrawn-grid-correction-uses-a-shared-vertex-mesh.md) | 2026-07-13 |
| [0136](0136-loading-is-manifest-driven-and-frame-acknowledged.md) | Loading uses shell-critical, surface-critical, and opportunistic manifests; readiness means the actual compositor acknowledged its first complete painted frame, never merely fetch/decode/React commit; one Studio Loading Lab records the shared lifecycle and network evidence; list thumbnails become immutable mutation-time derivatives | accepted; refines ADR-0046/0051 readiness clauses | 2026-07-19 |
| [0137](0137-subterrain-follows-the-visual-terrain-surface.md) | Explicit Subterrain may occupy exposed faces on any active visual terrain coordinate, including rectangular and sparse scenic terrain; playability never determines visual-face eligibility | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0105 playable-occupancy eligibility | 2026-07-19 |
| [0138](0138-codex-environments-use-browser-approved-dev-auth.md) | Every Codex environment obtains a fresh browser-approved auth.romaine.life device grant; the ignored short-lived token supplies verified loopback-only development identity so authenticated browser verification is available from session start | accepted | 2026-07-19 |
| [0139](0139-persistence-failures-interrupt-editing-and-recovery-conflicts-resolve.md) | Level Editor cloud autosave errors and conflicts interrupt every editing layer; an explicitly chosen browser recovery may resume CAS autosave only when its scoped revision and cloud signature still match the document on screen, while Discard remains a separate confirmed choice of the canonical saved position | accepted; browser-recovery conflict workflow partially superseded by [0304](0304-level-editor-documents-are-live-shared-working-copies.md) | 2026-07-19 |
| [0140](0140-working-copy-revisions-are-retained-and-owner-restorable.md) | Every acknowledged Level Editor working-copy mutation records a retained server checkpoint; the owner may export both sides and CAS-restore history only as a new private working revision, while canonical Save and ADR-0132 admin-review boundaries remain unchanged | accepted | 2026-07-19 |
| [0141](0141-predrawn-generation-references-preserve-explicit-subterrain.md) | The canonical pre-drawn generation reference preserves terrain tops plus only explicitly persisted Subterrain resolved onto exposed active visual-terrain faces; absent faces stay empty, and generation may not invent skirts, cliffs, attached strips, addresses, or gameplay height | accepted; all-active-surface inclusion and capture-edge clause partially superseded by [0142](0142-owner-authored-frame-defines-predrawn-generation-reference.md); partially supersedes ADR-0120's top-surfaces-only exclusion and refines ADR-0105/0137 | 2026-07-19 |
| [0142](0142-owner-authored-frame-defines-predrawn-generation-reference.md) | A saved owner-authored 16:9 frame in canonical projected-board coordinates defines the pre-drawn generation-reference crop; required gameplay-authoritative reference geometry must fit, scenic-only art may clip or be excluded, and preparation fails closed without a valid frame | accepted; single-current-reference authority partially superseded by [0165](0165-ai-artwork-separates-sources-attempts-and-background-mode.md); refines ADR-0071/0120/0123/0125 and partially supersedes ADR-0141's unqualified all-active-surface inclusion and capture-edge clause | 2026-07-19 |
| [0143](0143-level-editor-sessions-are-attributable-single-writer-and-owner-takeoverable.md) | Each Level Editor document has one PostgreSQL-fenced, attributable owner writer session; the owner may explicitly take over after preserving the displaced branch, while session-scoped browser recovery and baseline/revision conflicts remain separate and administrators remain cross-owner read-only | superseded by [0304](0304-level-editor-documents-are-live-shared-working-copies.md) | 2026-07-20 |
| [0144](0144-level-editor-events-use-the-shell-workspace.md) | Level Editor Events visually replace the board inside the shell-owned left workspace while the title bar and right controls remain fixed; the surface fills its parent, owns only content and internal scrolling, reuses the generated fill-only path, and has no separate outer frame, dialog semantics, or viewport math | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0064 Events presentation and ADR-0082 Events outer-panel classification | 2026-07-19 |
| [0145](0145-scenic-artwork-is-free-transform-generation-input.md) | Installed structure source art may be placed directly as gameplay-inert, scalable, true-eight-way pre-drawn generation input, independent of prop/doodad definitions and suppressed once baked into an accepted plate | accepted; editor nudge clause superseded by [0146](0146-scenic-artwork-reuses-object-selection-and-axis-sliders.md), board-coordinate/contact-depth clauses by [0147](0147-floating-artwork-uses-projected-scene-pixels.md), and Level Editor destination/shelf naming by [0176](0176-placed-art-and-level-artwork-are-separate-editor-destinations.md) | 2026-07-22 |
| [0146](0146-scenic-artwork-reuses-object-selection-and-axis-sliders.md) | Scenic artwork introduces no visible placement marker, reuses an invisible object selection target, and exposes X, Y, and Scale as consistent slider-plus-number rows | accepted; seated-target and board-bounded-axis clauses superseded by [0147](0147-floating-artwork-uses-projected-scene-pixels.md), and Artwork-layer naming by [0176](0176-placed-art-and-level-artwork-are-separate-editor-destinations.md) | 2026-07-23 |
| [0147](0147-floating-artwork-uses-projected-scene-pixels.md) | Direct source art floats at an exact projected-scene pixel, drags without grid projection or snapping, renders as an ordered overlay, retains exact pixel controls, and has no tile, contact, footprint, depth seat, or gameplay semantics | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0145 and ADR-0146 placement clauses; Level Editor placement/interaction clauses superseded by [0148](0148-floating-artwork-uses-dedicated-placement-and-explicit-selection.md) | 2026-07-23 |
| [0148](0148-floating-artwork-uses-dedicated-placement-and-explicit-selection.md) | Floating artwork uses a dedicated viewport free-placement brush, an explicit growing instance dropdown with None, non-overlapping Select/Move/Delete behavior, and a dotted current-instance outline without invoking tile authoring | accepted; growing Selected dropdown superseded by [0500](0500-scene-art-select-is-local-alpha-aware-and-stack-cycling.md); supersedes ADR-0147 Level Editor placement and interaction clauses; Select activation/current-outline visibility clauses previously superseded by [0149](0149-artwork-select-toggles-candidate-discovery.md), and Artwork-layer naming by [0176](0176-placed-art-and-level-artwork-are-separate-editor-destinations.md) | 2026-07-23 |
| [0149](0149-artwork-select-toggles-candidate-discovery.md) | Artwork Select toggles an explicit discovery mode that highlights every selectable image-bounds target; its second click exits the mode, clears the current selection, and removes all artwork selection outlines | superseded by [0500](0500-scene-art-select-is-local-alpha-aware-and-stack-cycling.md); partially superseded ADR-0148 Select activation/current-outline visibility clauses; Artwork-layer naming previously superseded by [0176](0176-placed-art-and-level-artwork-are-separate-editor-destinations.md) | 2026-07-24 |
| [0150](0150-structure-source-art-turntables-are-complete-source-only-live-groups.md) | Structure source artwork installs only as a complete atomic eight-view Blender turntable; source-only landmarks appear in Artwork without fake prop/doodad gameplay policy, and Studio owns native-1× group review, acceptance, and drawable installation | accepted; isolated native-frame review clause superseded by [0151](0151-source-art-review-requires-interactive-board-placement.md); refines ADR-0145, ADR-0071, and ADR-0085 | 2026-07-24 |
| [0151](0151-source-art-review-requires-interactive-board-placement.md) | Source-art candidates must be freely placed, dragged, scaled, and rotated through all eight exact directions on the shared game board before review can be recorded; native decode remains a prerequisite rather than the review surface | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0150 isolated native-frame review clause; refines ADR-0071, ADR-0147, and ADR-0148 | 2026-07-24 |
| [0152](0152-level-editor-session-attention-lives-in-title-bar-and-status.md) | Level Editor session and recovery details live only in Status; one conditional title-bar attention control opens Status and focuses the relevant information without occupying every authoring layer | superseded by [0304](0304-level-editor-documents-are-live-shared-working-copies.md) | 2026-07-25 |
| [0153](0153-bulk-recovery-cleanup-is-snapshot-exact-and-atomic.md) | Status may delete the exact listed set of server recoveries through one current-writer-fenced database transaction; missing or foreign ids delete none, later recoveries survive, and destructive confirmation defaults to Cancel | superseded by [0304](0304-level-editor-documents-are-live-shared-working-copies.md) | 2026-07-25 |
| [0154](0154-level-editor-viewing-does-not-acquire-the-writer-lease.md) | Opening an owner Level Editor registers a lease-free viewer; the first persisted authoring change is browser-recovered, generation-fenced, and acquires before autosave, while a racing loser cannot implicitly take over the winner | superseded by [0304](0304-level-editor-documents-are-live-shared-working-copies.md) | 2026-07-25 |
| [0155](0155-source-asset-packs-yield-isolated-provenance-linked-artwork.md) | One owner source pack may yield independently placeable eight-way Artwork through exact mesh-object allowlists while all outputs share one archived source version; authored pose/yaw and separate opacity maps remain deterministic provenance, and malformed sources are excluded rather than presented for approval | accepted; refines ADR-0150 and ADR-0151 | 2026-07-25 |
| [0156](0156-named-predrawn-candidate-refinements-are-separate-non-isolated-branches.md) | After owner review finds a localized miss, a named pre-drawn comparative refinement may add that exact candidate as subordinate Image 2 while canonical Image 1 and the unchanged semantic packet retain authority; fail-closed parent lineage, hashes, a separate branch, and `isolatedPipelineEvidence: false` prevent the edit from masquerading as an isolated result or inheriting acceptance | accepted; refines ADR-0109, ADR-0120, and ADR-0125 | 2026-07-20 |
| [0157](0157-recovery-snapshots-browse-one-at-a-time-and-clear-atomically.md) | Status browses server recovery snapshots one at a time newest-first; bulk cleanup deletes the exact confirmed ids atomically under the revalidated current writer fence, preserves later recoveries, and cannot affect working, canonical, history, or browser-backup content | superseded by [0304](0304-level-editor-documents-are-live-shared-working-copies.md) | 2026-07-20 |
| [0158](0158-immutable-predrawn-background-versions-own-derived-raster-and-occlusion.md) | Pre-drawn backgrounds are owner-operated immutable live-storage lineages: uploads are settable raw roots, grid adjustment emits a deterministic raster child, occlusion emits an exact depth-aware mask child, Set changes only the fenced working copy, Save/Publish crosses the canonical boundary, and runtime suppresses every ordinary environment pixel while retaining units, tactical overlays, and UI | accepted; supersedes ADR-0122/0123 and partially supersedes ADR-0076/0085/0110/0111/0115/0116/0121/0134/0135 as detailed in the record | 2026-07-20 |
| [0159](0159-predrawn-background-authoring-storage-is-bounded.md) | Pre-drawn lineage authoring is server-bounded to 256 permanent version rows per document, 1 GiB of distinct retained Blob bytes per owner, and one in-flight raw upload body per document; Archive retains and therefore does not evade either quota | accepted; artifact accounting and attempt bound extended by [0165](0165-ai-artwork-separates-sources-attempts-and-background-mode.md); refines ADR-0158 | 2026-07-20 |
| [0160](0160-automated-editor-verification-is-observation-only.md) | Authenticated automated Level Editor verification registers an attributable observing session that never acquires or extends the writer lease, advances the fencing generation, resolves expiry, creates recovery, or mutates content | accepted; refines ADR-0143 | 2026-07-20 |
| [0161](0161-predrawn-artwork-is-a-linear-shell-workspace.md) | AI board artwork uses a dedicated shell-owned Level Editor workspace and presents generated, warped, and occlusion-ready outputs as distinct selectable board versions; the attached depth data is internal to the final artifact rather than a second owner-facing selection axis | superseded by [0165](0165-ai-artwork-separates-sources-attempts-and-background-mode.md) | 2026-07-20 |
| [0162](0162-predrawn-backgrounds-retain-live-ground-cover.md) | Pre-drawn backgrounds suppress baked environment art but retain explicitly authored ground cover as a live editable layer across playable and scenic visual terrain; cover keeps its canonical unit depth, animation, and persisted-mask clipping, remains absent from generation references and background geometry fingerprints, and cover-only edits do not stale raster/mask lineage | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0158’s ground-cover suppression, authoring-lock, and geometry-lineage clauses | 2026-07-20 |
| [0163](0163-legacy-predrawn-geometry-fingerprints-bind-to-cover-independent-v2.md) | Migration 30 preserves immutable v1 pre-drawn geometry provenance in a one-row-per-version external binding to the cover-independent v2 digest, established only from an exact server-held Level proof at a fenced autosave, direct derivative creation, or Save/Publish fallback; new operations require v2 and reads never mutate | accepted; refines ADR-0158 and ADR-0162; incoming-autosave validation and first baked-edit rejection clauses partially superseded by [0164](0164-predrawn-geometry-staleness-does-not-block-draft-persistence.md) | 2026-07-20 |
| [0164](0164-predrawn-geometry-staleness-does-not-block-draft-persistence.md) | Pre-drawn geometry mismatch is explicit stale draft state, not an autosave or recovery rejection: the first fenced autosave binds legacy v1 from the server-held pre-mutation Level and still preserves the incoming body, artwork marks stale selections and disables Set/derivation, while Save and Publish remain fail-closed | accepted; dormant-selection Save rejection partially superseded by [0165](0165-ai-artwork-separates-sources-attempts-and-background-mode.md); partially supersedes ADR-0163's incoming-autosave validation and first baked-edit rejection clauses | 2026-07-20 |
| [0165](0165-ai-artwork-separates-sources-attempts-and-background-mode.md) | AI artwork separates immutable saved Source Artworks, server-owned linear Creation Attempts, and the Level's persistent Legacy/AI background mode; side controls keep the board visible, dedicated URL-addressable workspaces manage sources and attempts, and each attempt permits one committed generated, warped, and occlusion-ready result | accepted; source terminology, pipeline workspace name, and generated-stage ingress partially superseded by [0166](0166-manual-ai-handoff-separates-generation-references-from-raw-pipeline-sources.md); creation-attempt generated-stage ownership partially superseded by [0168](0168-creation-slots-begin-with-reusable-raw-pipeline-sources.md); AI Artwork destination and route namespace naming partially superseded by [0176](0176-placed-art-and-level-artwork-are-separate-editor-destinations.md); supersedes ADR-0161 and partially supersedes ADR-0120/0125/0142/0159/0164 as detailed in the record | 2026-07-22 |
| [0166](0166-manual-ai-handoff-separates-generation-references-from-raw-pipeline-sources.md) | Board Art Pipeline distinguishes the level-derived Generation Reference copied at full resolution from the AI-painted Raw Pipeline Source: clipboard paste or exact-PNG file selection stages a local preview, Use this board explicitly commits it, and an editor-mounted preexisting Codex result has a direct raw-import path; the app persists canonical semantic-request provenance without claiming the external conversation's model, prompt, or parameters | accepted; combined Generation-Reference handoff and deterministic-processing attempt model partially superseded by [0168](0168-creation-slots-begin-with-reusable-raw-pipeline-sources.md); partially supersedes ADR-0165's Source Artwork terminology, AI Generation Pipeline workspace name, and unspecified generated-stage ingress; refines ADR-0109/0125/0158 | 2026-07-23 |
| [0167](0167-raw-pipeline-sources-can-seed-new-attempts.md) | An exact retained Raw Pipeline Source, including one owned by a migrated historical slot, may seed a separate non-isolated attempt by stored version/Blob reference: Start new attempt from this image binds a discriminated raw-pipeline-source input and the current canonical semantic request without reclassification, media copy/upload, or mutation; warped and occlusion-ready artifacts remain ineligible | superseded by [0168](0168-creation-slots-begin-with-reusable-raw-pipeline-sources.md) | 2026-07-24 |
| [0168](0168-creation-slots-begin-with-reusable-raw-pipeline-sources.md) | Generation Reference handoff produces an immutable Raw Pipeline Source, while every Board Art creation slot begins from one reusable raw input and owns only its optional warped and occlusion-ready results; workspace-level New attempt chooses an eligible retained raw and starts directly at grid fitting without another model handoff, upload, or raw output | accepted; different-warp-requires-another-slot clause partially superseded by [0175](0175-rejected-warp-retries-stay-in-the-same-pipeline-slot.md); supersedes ADR-0167, partially supersedes ADR-0165's generated-stage attempt ownership and ADR-0166's combined generation/processing attempt, and refines ADR-0071/0158/0166 | 2026-07-24 |
| [0169](0169-historical-raw-contracts-bind-only-from-saved-level-proof.md) | Historical Raw Pipeline Sources missing later coordinate-basis/viewing-pane metadata remain immutable; only fenced processing-attempt creation may establish an external one-row raw-contract binding after exact server-held saved-Level proof of frame/bounds, legacy geometry, bytes, and provenance, while the backend owns picker eligibility and the chooser shows failures beside the affected source | accepted; refines ADR-0158/0163/0168 | 2026-07-24 |
| [0170](0170-derived-board-inspection-is-a-full-workspace-revision-gate.md) | Warped and occlusion-ready boards use a focused full-pipeline-workspace grid/cyan inspection instrument; Tweak grid creates a new immutable attempt from the exact same Raw Pipeline Source and preloads the inspected warp's direct registration without mutating or copying prior art | accepted; same-slot rejected-warp workflow partially superseded by [0175](0175-rejected-warp-retries-stay-in-the-same-pipeline-slot.md), and non-persistent cyan-proof clause partially superseded by [0179](0179-predrawn-cyan-move-highlights-use-per-cell-visual-footprints.md); refines ADR-0071/0158/0165/0168 | 2026-07-25 |
| [0171](0171-local-predrawn-grid-correction-uses-a-shared-vertex-mesh.md) | Pre-drawn fitting keeps coarse corners and row/column guides, then permits sparse local correction through one continuous shared-vertex mesh: selecting a cell exposes its shared intersections, boundary nodes remain coarse, every affected cell remains non-folded, and one deterministic complete-raster transform preserves shared edges and surrounding scenery | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0110's arbitrary per-intersection prohibition and row/column-only correction scope plus ADR-0134/0135's shared-intersection prohibition; refines ADR-0071/0111/0113/0114/0158/0170 | 2026-07-25 |
| [0172](0172-archiving-a-board-art-slot-forgets-only-dormant-legacy-selection.md) | The existing Archive slot action atomically forgets that slot's dormant remembered selection from Legacy working and canonical Levels and archives the slot under the editor and document fences, while any matching AI-mode use or published output remains protected; immutable versions and bytes stay retained | accepted; refines ADR-0143/0158/0165/0168 | 2026-07-25 |
| [0173](0173-structure-source-art-turntables-are-complete-source-only-live-groups.md) | Structure source artwork remains a complete atomic eight-view Blender turntable; source-only landmarks remain outside prop/doodad gameplay projections and Prop Seat creation, while Studio owns interactive board-context group review, acceptance, and drawable installation | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0150's source-only projection and installation clauses; refines ADR-0071/0085/0106/0150/0151 | 2026-07-25 |
| [0174](0174-database-migrations-are-append-only-checksummed-and-explicit.md) | Database migrations are contiguous append-only identities: CI and runtime reject changed history, migration 37 seals legacy numeric rows and registers working-copy revision reasons, migration 38 closes the legacy bridge with non-null identity, readiness proves live schema topology, local startup stays check-only, explicit migration runs report exact applied/skipped/pending work, and disposable upgrade-path QA executes the real authenticated mutation | accepted; sparse legacy-history premise partially superseded by ADR-0186; refines ADR-0086 | 2026-07-25 |
| [0175](0175-rejected-warp-retries-stay-in-the-same-pipeline-slot.md) | Rejecting a warped board archives that immutable artifact, clears only the exact current warp from the same slot, preserves and reloads its registration over the same Raw Pipeline Source, and permits another deterministic warp without creating a slot | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0168's different-warp-requires-another-slot clause and ADR-0170's Tweak-grid-in-new-attempt workflow; refines ADR-0158/0165/0174 | 2026-07-25 |
| [0176](0176-placed-art-and-level-artwork-are-separate-editor-destinations.md) | Level Artwork exclusively owns the AI background workflow, while Placed Art groups Scene Art, Doodads, and Props behind one subtype selector; only Scene Art accepts new off-board positions, and existing off-board doodads/props remain visible and removable without a destructive migration | accepted; Props placement boundary partially superseded by ADR-0365; partially supersedes ADR-0098/0145/0146/0148/0149/0165 as detailed in the record; refines ADR-0147/0168 | 2026-07-25 |
| [0177](0177-level-editor-recovery-is-a-separate-side-control-destination.md) | Level Editor Recovery is a distinct URL-addressable side-control destination for browser/cloud copies, server recovery snapshots, and working-copy history; Status retains session, validation, autosave, and Save/Publish controls while the global persistence interruption remains cross-layer | superseded by [0304](0304-level-editor-documents-are-live-shared-working-copies.md) | 2026-07-25 |
| [0178](0178-predrawn-grid-fitting-uses-one-reversible-edit-history.md) | Pre-drawn grid fitting uses one bounded session-local Undo/Redo history across every pending coarse, boundary, dimension, spacing, snap, local-mesh, and opening-restore mutation; compound actions and completed drags are atomic while view-only operations do not enter history | accepted; refines ADR-0071/0110/0113/0114/0171 | 2026-07-25 |
| [0179](0179-predrawn-cyan-move-highlights-use-per-cell-visual-footprints.md) | After warp, each Board Art attempt owns one CAS-updated latest sparse per-cell cyan footprint profile; setting art embeds an exact schema-v3 Level snapshot, new occlusion requires a saved profile, and the full reversible fitting workspace clips only cyan move paint while movement and hit geometry remain canonical | accepted; cyan-only rendering scope partially superseded by [0185](0185-predrawn-fitted-cell-footprints-shape-every-square-local-visual-highlight.md); partially supersedes ADR-0170's non-persistent cyan-proof clause; refines ADR-0071/0158/0165 | 2026-07-26 |
| [0180](0180-predrawn-occlusion-selects-final-raster-pixels.md) | Pre-drawn occlusion is owner-authored over the exact warped raster: a pinned off-thread browser-local SlimSAM proposes three point-guided candidates, complete manual alpha editing remains available, and explicit creation deterministically assigns per-component ground-contact depth and persists one provenance-complete immutable child | accepted; refines ADR-0071/0158/0165/0179 | 2026-07-26 |
| [0181](0181-occlusion-mask-retries-stay-in-the-same-pipeline-slot.md) | The final owner-facing stage is Board with occlusion mask; rejecting its current immutable child detaches only that mask under writer, attempt, and document fences, preserves the same slot, warp, grid, and cyan profile, falls a matching working selection back to warp-only, retains canonical history, and reopens mask authoring for an in-place retry | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0165/0168 one-commit-requires-new-slot and owner-facing stage-name clauses; refines ADR-0158/0175/0179/0180 | 2026-07-26 |
| [0182](0182-cyan-footprint-editing-has-image-axis-locks-and-native-pixel-nudges.md) | Cyan footprint fitting exposes Free/X-only/Y-only image-axis movement and visible one-native-artwork-pixel nudges; constrained moves preserve the locked coordinate exactly, use the existing normalized profile, and participate in the same reversible session history | accepted; refines ADR-0071/0179 | 2026-07-26 |
| [0183](0183-cyan-footprint-fitting-is-viewport-level-and-edits-points-or-edges.md) | Cyan fitting uses the same viewport-level precision workspace as grid fitting; a selected edge moves as one supporting line with jointly derived endpoints, exact native-pixel scaling, canonical validation, and one atomic history step while point editing remains available | accepted; single-cell target clause partially superseded by [0184](0184-cyan-footprint-fitting-supports-additive-tile-selections-and-outer-border-bars.md); partially supersedes ADR-0179's center-workspace placement and ADR-0182's point-only precision target; refines ADR-0071/0178 | 2026-07-26 |
| [0184](0184-cyan-footprint-fitting-supports-additive-tile-selections-and-outer-border-bars.md) | Cyan fitting supports plain-click replacement and Shift-click additive tile selection, keeps one primary tile for point handles, exposes only maximal contiguous same-edge selection-boundary bars, and applies bar nudges or selected resets atomically through the existing history and profile schema | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0183's single-cell target clause; refines ADR-0071/0178 | 2026-07-26 |
| [0185](0185-predrawn-fitted-cell-footprints-shape-every-square-local-visual-highlight.md) | On pre-drawn boards, the fitted cell footprint shapes every square-local runtime and Level Editor visual highlight while canonical cells remain authoritative for interaction, movement, pathfinding, occupancy, zone membership, topology, and solver logic; compatibility profile and storage names remain unchanged with no migration | accepted; partially supersedes only ADR-0179's cyan-only rendering scope | 2026-07-26 |
| [0186](0186-legacy-migration-36-is-an-explicit-sparse-history-bridge.md) | The exact numeric-only legacy ledger 1–27,36 may bridge its one known sparse row while 28–35 are applied, after which migration 37 seals a contiguous identity history; every other gap and any identified sparse row still fail closed, and upgrade QA starts from the real sparse baseline | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0174's contiguous legacy-history premise | 2026-07-26 |
| [0187](0187-required-schema-repair-installs-final-state.md) | Generation-attempt relation, retry, and profile repair uses append-only migration 43's complete forward-compatible topology instead of replaying transitional migrations that reject valid pipeline-source rows or move-highlight audit events | accepted; refines ADR-0174 | 2026-07-26 |
| [0188](0188-committed-movers-release-client-selection.md) | A committed mover releases client-local selection and focus so its legal-move overlay returns only after an explicit selection; pending network intents retain recoverable context, different explicitly chosen units survive asynchronous work, and captured selections clear without an arbitrary fallback | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0077's relay selection-retention clause | 2026-07-27 |
| [0189](0189-board-facing-views-open-on-playable-geometry.md) | Board-facing live and static views contain the projected playable-board presentation plus a five-percent per-side margin, while accepted raster bounds independently own the zoom-out/pan safety boundary; generated overscan supplies exploration room, compact previews use 3:2, exact-art instruments retain full-art fit, and a renderer revision regenerates disposable derivatives without regenerating AI artwork | accepted; tile-relief/headroom framing clause partially superseded by [0191](0191-board-opening-frame-uses-the-playable-contact-surface.md), selected-preview/gameplay viewport-shape clauses partially superseded by [0192](0192-interactive-board-viewports-share-a-four-by-three-shape.md), all board-pane shape exceptions partially superseded by [0204](0204-all-board-viewing-panes-match-play.md), and accepted-raster-only camera-boundary clause partially superseded by [0301](0301-levels-own-an-authored-camera-coverage-boundary.md); partially supersedes ADR-0067's dense-solid crop/static-only clauses and refines ADR-0121/0136/0158 | 2026-07-27 |
| [0190](0190-accepted-art-zoom-floor-uses-the-full-feasible-pan-region.md) | The board-relative opening camera remains independent from maximum zoom-out; the accepted-art floor is the smallest zoom whose viewport fits anywhere in the transformed art polygon, and zoom/resize reclamp to the nearest feasible pan without revealing black | accepted; no-additional-level-relative-limit clause partially superseded by [0301](0301-levels-own-an-authored-camera-coverage-boundary.md); partially supersedes ADR-0121's centered-floor clause and refines ADR-0189 | 2026-07-27 |
| [0191](0191-board-opening-frame-uses-the-playable-contact-surface.md) | The board-owned opening frame contains the projected playable contact-diamond surface plus five percent per side, excluding fixed tile sprite relief/headroom; accepted-art zoom-out remains independent and derivative framing revisions advance | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0189's tile-relief/headroom clause and refines ADR-0190 | 2026-07-27 |
| [0192](0192-interactive-board-viewports-share-a-four-by-three-shape.md) | Gameplay and the Campaign/Campaign Editor selected-level live preview share one literal 4:3 drawable viewport; gameplay fits the largest centred instance inside its responsive seat, the fixed-width preview derives height, and compact static derivatives remain 3:2 | accepted; gameplay 4:3 clause partially superseded by [0201](0201-board-cameras-fit-the-actual-owning-viewport.md), and all remaining board-pane shape clauses partially superseded by [0204](0204-all-board-viewing-panes-match-play.md) | 2026-07-28 |
| [0193](0193-runs-are-persistent-wars-around-unchanged-chess-battles.md) | Run adds a seeded persistent army and economy around canonical chess Battles; separately authored Wars own exclusive ordered Levels, every non-final win has a shop, Loot reveals never-repeat lipsana, signed-in Runs persist cross-device, and no lipsanon may change chess-piece behavior | accepted; opening draft and free-Pawn baseline partially superseded by [0319](0319-run-openings-spend-nine-gold-on-one-of-three-seeded-cards.md)/[0320](0320-run-openings-begin-with-only-the-permanent-king.md)/[0321](0321-run-opening-is-the-normal-shop-and-draft-is-retired.md)/[0322](0322-run-openings-use-two-pawns-eight-gold-and-card-native-purchase-feedback.md), lipsanon display identities by [0264](0264-run-lipsanon-identities-carry-anti-story-residue.md), and unit-placement lipsanon effect ownership by [0274](0274-lipsana-grant-unit-owned-deployment-abilities.md); supersedes the roguelike/continuity deferral in the game concept and refines ADR-0038/0050/0060/0070/0071/0074 | 2026-07-28 |
| [0194](0194-admin-playtest-controls-are-a-hidden-settings-destination.md) | Allowlisted admins gain one hidden Settings destination for one-shot Free Move, Kill Unit, and Win Battle interventions plus active-Run Gold and Lipsanon grants; every action is backend-authorized, normal Battle/Run lifecycles remain authoritative, and restart/progression/removal/history/Undo duplicates are excluded | superseded by [0195](0195-admin-playtest-controls-open-in-place-from-the-battle-hud.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0195](0195-admin-playtest-controls-open-in-place-from-the-battle-hud.md) | Admin playtest controls open as a hidden in-HUD destination from the active Battle's Controls panel, keeping the board mounted and visible while preserving the approved interventions and backend authorization | accepted; supersedes [0194](0194-admin-playtest-controls-are-a-hidden-settings-destination.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0196](0196-codex-environments-have-owner-named-localhost-identities.md) | Every Windows Codex environment receives one owner-named `<environment>.chess-tactics.localhost` identity shared by auth approval, devctl/Caddy routing, agent context, browser verification, and handoff while dynamic ports remain internal | superseded by [0197](0197-codex-agents-discover-named-environments-from-setup-state.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0197](0197-codex-agents-discover-named-environments-from-setup-state.md) | Windows Codex setup remains the sole lifecycle mechanism for named environments: it writes and prints the stable identity, while agents read the worktree-local record or inspect devctl instead of requiring a project SessionStart hook | accepted; terminal environment-name prompt superseded by [0199](0199-codex-environment-names-are-chosen-during-auth-approval.md); supersedes ADR-0196 and refines ADR-0138 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0198](0198-run-lipsanon-icons-are-installed-live-art-and-persistently-visible.md) | Native 64×64 Run lipsanon icons are accepted live media installed through per-lipsanon drawable records; offers render them and acquired lipsana remain visible between Battles and in the Battle HUD, while missing approved pixels fail explicitly without fabricated fallback art | accepted; installed-art authority for renamed identities partially superseded by [0264](0264-run-lipsanon-identities-carry-anti-story-residue.md), native-source clause for eight exact outputs by [0332](0332-eight-run-lipsanon-icons-ship-the-approved-resized-pixels.md); refines ADR-0085 and ADR-0193 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0199](0199-codex-environment-names-are-chosen-during-auth-approval.md) | Fresh Windows Codex setup requests one auth-owned non-secret environment-name input during browser approval, persists the returned canonical value as setup state, and leaves existing or explicitly named environments unchanged | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0197's terminal prompt and refines ADR-0138 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0200](0200-bgm-is-private-storage-behind-app-owned-capability-routes.md) | Anonymous BGM uses a private Blob container behind app-owned discovery and stable opaque playback routes: the backend validates the current catalog id, mints a fresh Blob-specific read-only HTTPS user-delegation SAS, and returns a no-store 302 while Azure serves bytes and ranges; full IDs, bounded coalesced delegation-key caching, account-scoped Blob Delegator plus container-scoped Reader, no frontend storage base, and no public/static-index compatibility path | accepted; refines ADR-0085's BGM Blob-index/range-streaming exception and preserves ADR-0044's single persistent control | 2026-07-29 |
| [0201](0201-board-cameras-fit-the-actual-owning-viewport.md) | Gameplay measures, clips, and renders against the complete visible pane beside its HUD; the shared camera contains the playable contact surface plus five-percent padding in that exact rectangle, and its natural fit may raise the gameplay zoom ceiling instead of being stopped by a fixed cap | accepted; responsive Play-seat clause partially superseded by [0202](0202-play-uses-one-fixed-design-resolution.md), unchanged-other-surfaces clause partially superseded by [0204](0204-all-board-viewing-panes-match-play.md); partially supersedes ADR-0192's gameplay 4:3 clause and refines ADR-0189/0191 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0202](0202-play-uses-one-fixed-design-resolution.md) | Play uses one 1920×1080 design canvas with an 88px title bar, a directly adjoining 360px HUD, and a 1560×992 board pane; the browser uniformly contains and centers the whole composition, and resize or browser zoom changes only that outer scale rather than reflowing Play | superseded by [0259](0259-the-live-play-composition-is-the-authority-derived-views-conform.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0203](0203-ordinary-board-previews-match-the-play-pane.md) | Play, selected-level live previews, stored level thumbnails, and browser-baked authoring thumbnails use the Play pane's literal 195:124 aspect; compact derivatives are 390×248, chrome remains outside that drawable rectangle, and framing/backend revisions invalidate the retired 3:2 files | superseded by [0204](0204-all-board-viewing-panes-match-play.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0204](0204-all-board-viewing-panes-match-play.md) | Every rendered game-board pane—Play, editors, previews, replays, solver/Gym/Game Lab/Studio viewers, and thumbnails—uses Play's exact 195:124 aspect while source media and fixed-format export artifacts retain their own required dimensions | accepted; ratio moved to the canonical 4:3 board window by [0259](0259-the-live-play-composition-is-the-authority-derived-views-conform.md), with the main Level Editor viewport exception in [0278](0278-level-editor-board-fills-its-authoring-workspace.md); supersedes ADR-0203's ordinary-preview-only scope | 2026-07-29 |
| [0205](0205-navigation-loads-atomic-scenes-through-one-director.md) | Navigation is one scene lifecycle: lock and fade outgoing controls, retain the old background under graceful Loading copy, prepare a bounded first-viewport manifest invisibly, then fade the painted destination background and controls in together; startup alone reveals background, title, then menu in ordered minimum-beat stages | accepted; universal fade-to-background sequence partially superseded by [0462](0462-transition-choreography-is-derived-from-scene-ownership.md); supersedes ADR-0046/0049/0051 and refines ADR-0136 | 2026-07-27 |
| [0206](0206-scenes-declare-persistent-visual-hosts.md) | Scene manifests declare persistent visual-host identity: different-host navigation replaces the complete scene, while same-host navigation retains and locks shared chrome and gates only the destination region through painted acknowledgement and entrance | accepted; refines ADR-0205 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0207](0207-persistent-scene-hosts-form-a-nested-path.md) | Persistent scene hosts form a registered nested path; the director preserves the deepest shared host and gates only that host's named destination region, so Play navigation remains anchored while selected Play content changes | accepted; refines ADR-0206 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0208](0208-preserved-host-controls-remain-interactive.md) | During same-host loading, only the replaceable destination region becomes hidden and inert; preserved ancestor controls remain visible and interactive and may retarget the director to a newer destination generation | accepted; refines ADR-0205/0207 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0209](0209-routes-request-authored-scene-instances.md) | Routes resolve to immutable authored scene paths; named slots keep committed and pending instances separate, and route events may request but never directly reveal visible content | accepted; refines ADR-0205/0207 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0210](0210-empty-scene-slots-commit-without-loading.md) | Removing a retained host's authored child uses an empty-slot commit: exit, commit directly to current, and never show Loading or impose a loading minimum when nothing must be acquired | accepted; refines ADR-0205/0209 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0211](0211-navigational-drawing-requires-an-authored-scene-slot.md) | Every click/history action that replaces a navigable drawn region must resolve to an authored instance in a named slot; local state remains for interaction within the committed scene, never route-owned visible selection | accepted; refines ADR-0209/0210 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0212](0212-scene-transitioning-does-not-imply-loading-presentation.md) | Authored transitions declare `loading` or `transition-only`: resident destinations keep director ownership and painted entrance without Loading copy or an artificial minimum, while genuine acquisition retains both | accepted; refines ADR-0205/0211 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0213](0213-the-application-title-bar-persists-across-scenes.md) | The one application title bar is a persistent host above replaceable scenes: normal navigation never fades or disables its invariant frame and controls, route-owned contributions follow the committed path, and gameplay exit retains the exact battlefield composition beneath the homepage crossfade | accepted; refines ADR-0205/0206 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0214](0214-the-scene-director-owns-transition-target-lifetime.md) | The scene director owns one canonical transition target's DOM lifetime through exit, preparation, commit, and entrance; pending intent cannot unmount it, host-specific fade selectors are retired, navigational drawing is managed by default, and component-local controls such as gameplay HUD tabs remain immediate | accepted; refines ADR-0205/0211 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0215](0215-route-loading-status-lives-in-the-persistent-title-bar.md) | After startup, genuine acquisition reports Loading in the persistent title bar while full-scene replacement retains the complete outgoing scene beneath the hidden destination and crossfades only after destination paint; no blank intermediary or reconstructed background is permitted | accepted; supersedes ADR-0205 retained-background and centered route-Loading presentation; refines ADR-0212/0213 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0216](0216-run-lipsana-live-in-a-playfield-overlay-beneath-the-title-bar.md) | Acquired Run lipsana render as one frameless native-size icon strip at the upper-left beneath the invariant title bar, independent of Battle Controls; between-Battle screens reserve its band and large collections scroll horizontally | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0198's Battle-HUD and between-Battle tray placement | 2026-07-29 |
| [0217](0217-run-lipsanon-icons-use-immediate-styled-tooltips.md) | Run lipsanon icons reveal their name and complete effect immediately through the shared fixed-position tooltip on hover or keyboard focus; native browser titles are retired while the help cursor remains | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0216's native hover-title clause and refines ADR-0059 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0218](0218-new-ui-surface-paint-is-build-blocked.md) | Frontend build/check freeze all CSS and React-inline background, border, and box-shadow paint behind an exact reviewed baseline; new or changed raw surface paint fails, and the one tooltip owner must render through registered `InnerChromeBox` | accepted; enforces ADR-0032/0059 and refines ADR-0217 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0219](0219-run-piece-bundles-are-portrait-cards-with-a-live-gold-icon.md) | Opening hands and shop bundles are whole-card inner-chrome actions composed from canonical unit portraits and live duplicate badges; compact prices use an accepted native Run gold resource icon plus a live number, installed through a typed live-media and drawable record with no fallback art | superseded in part by [0225](0225-run-bundle-cards-show-every-board-unit.md); live gold and whole-card decisions remain accepted | 2026-07-29 |
| [0220](0220-run-victory-gold-scales-with-enemy-force-value.md) | Non-final Run victory gold equals one per enemy King plus half the standard value of every other fixed or setup-spawned enemy chess piece; the exact reward is stored on and displayed by the resulting shop | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0193's fixed one-gold reward | 2026-07-29 |
| [0221](0221-run-sells-use-a-dedicated-live-gold-clink.md) | Run Sell requests one dedicated `gold-sell` one-shot instead of the default click; typed SFX slots are decoded and auditioned from exact private candidate bytes in Studio, hash/revision/snapshot proof gates atomic acceptance, and the live SFX profile is updated only after acceptance | superseded by [0222](0222-run-sell-clink-uses-the-owner-supplied-coins-recording.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0222](0222-run-sell-clink-uses-the-owner-supplied-coins-recording.md) | Run Sell keeps ADR-0221's dedicated typed live-SFX and proof-gated installation flow, but its candidate is the final isolated strike from the owner-supplied coins recording, clipped to 1.16 seconds with exact source and transform provenance | accepted; supersedes ADR-0221 and refines ADR-0085/0089/0193 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0223](0223-run-bundle-purchases-use-an-owner-supplied-card-draw.md) | Purchasing a Run piece-bundle card requests one dedicated `card-purchase` cue instead of the generic click; its typed candidate is the final isolated gesture from the owner-supplied card recording, with exact source and transform provenance and the existing proof-gated SFX installation flow | superseded by [0224](0224-owner-supplied-sfx-open-as-full-source-trim-instruments.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0224](0224-owner-supplied-sfx-open-as-full-source-trim-instruments.md) | Owner-supplied SFX open as complete private waveform sources with owner-controlled start/end, selection audition, immutable derived-candidate save, exact provenance, and proof-gated approval; candidate editing replaces rather than injects above the unrelated assignment editor | accepted; dedicated purchase-cue runtime assignment partially superseded by [0322](0322-run-openings-use-two-pawns-eight-gold-and-card-native-purchase-feedback.md); supersedes ADR-0223 and refines ADR-0071/0085/0089/0222 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0225](0225-run-bundle-cards-show-every-board-unit.md) | Opening-draft and shop bundle cards show one canonical player-side board sprite per actual unit, including repeated units, and scale the formation into the existing card area as a compact grid when necessary | accepted; supersedes ADR-0219's portrait-and-quantity representation; inherited price placement and temporary card-shell assumptions partially superseded by [0275](0275-run-cards-use-trading-card-anatomy-and-single-digit-gold.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0226](0226-play-expands-before-ultrawide-wings.md) | Play keeps its 1920×1080 reference scale and fixed title/HUD metrics, expands only the live board track through a 2.1:1 browser-content aspect, then caps a centered 2268×1080 composition with equal shell-owned ultrawide wings; wider Play stages reveal lateral world around the canonical 195:124 opening safe area while thumbnails and non-Play viewers remain unchanged | superseded by [0259](0259-the-live-play-composition-is-the-authority-derived-views-conform.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0227](0227-run-units-receive-seeded-persistent-names-on-acquisition.md) | Every persistent Run unit receives a seeded, stored name when it joins the army; 5,184 combinations stay unique through the army cap, format-1 Runs upgrade deterministically, and deployment carries the name into the live unit viewer while editing and ranks remain separate decisions | superseded by [0228](0228-run-unit-names-are-role-specific-historical-identities.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0228](0228-run-unit-names-are-role-specific-historical-identities.md) | Run names are complete, sourced historical identities selected by chess role: recorded archers, attested knights, religious leaders, real castles, queens/regents, and kings/emperors; six seeded 64-entry pools replace recombined fantasy names and format-1/2 Runs migrate deterministically | accepted; supersedes [0227](0227-run-units-receive-seeded-persistent-names-on-acquisition.md) and refines ADR-0193 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0230](0230-run-shops-separate-buying-army-inspection-and-selling.md) | Run shops use separate Shop, detailed Army, and compact Sell Units main-pane destinations while the existing Controls panel owns navigation, Reset Shop, and Continue; immediate sales are restored from a persisted same-offer shop-entry snapshot, unit identities remain stable and numbered, and Army inspection is available without pausing every Run phase | accepted; refines ADR-0193/0059/0217 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0231](0231-strategikon-and-enchiridion-share-one-reference-workspace-language.md) | Enchiridion is a main-menu and Battle-hosted rules/lipsanon reference; Strategikon is a Controls-titlebar Battle workspace whose menu-language rail opens Enchiridion, the Martial Prosopography, and the Lipsanotheca without unmounting Battle or using the Controls column; lipsanon history is an owner-scoped idempotent event ledger and Play exposes the exact unresolved activity first as Continue | accepted; Continue Run direct-route clause superseded by [0232](0232-continue-run-selects-run-before-play.md), and labeled Strategikon action presentation superseded by [0250](0250-strategikon-book-aligns-to-the-controls-content-boundary.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0232](0232-continue-run-selects-run-before-play.md) | Continue Run selects the existing Run submenu, where a nested action is the only control that enters the active Run; other unresolved Battle Continue entries remain direct | accepted; nested Play label and authored-War summary presentation partially superseded by [0289](0289-run-preparation-is-control-first-and-ataraxia-uses-one-selector.md); supersedes ADR-0231's direct-route rule for Runs and retains its other decisions | 2026-07-29 |
| [0233](0233-strategikon-uses-a-divider-safe-book-control.md) | Strategikon opens from one registered square open-codex control fully contained by the Controls title band; visible text is replaced by state-specific accessible names and critical hover explanations | superseded by [0249](0249-strategikon-book-art-is-the-title-control.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0234](0234-derived-thumbnails-use-exact-render-dependency-fingerprints.md) | Derived level thumbnails are versioned from their exact resolved render plan, consumed source hashes, and availability behavior; global catalog revisions select coherent snapshots but never invalidate unrelated derivatives, while true read repairs coalesce behind one bounded process-wide limiter and visible stale pixels remain fail-closed | accepted; refines ADR-0085/0106/0136 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0235](0235-battle-restart-is-not-a-board-destructive-operation.md) | Restart and Retry Battle reset mutable match state in place while preserving the mounted board, HUD, compositors, resources, and painted readiness; match epochs never key or reconstruct the visual surface, and only real visual-dependency changes use the board replacement lifecycle | accepted; refines ADR-0136/0214 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0236](0236-run-opening-draft-fills-the-shell-workspace.md) | The Run opening draft fills the shell-owned playfield through the shared fill-only workspace primitive; its content padding stays inside the continuous surface, and the retired nested `outer-panel` frame/gutter is forbidden | superseded by [0237](0237-run-destinations-fill-the-shell-workspace.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0237](0237-run-destinations-fill-the-shell-workspace.md) | Every player-facing non-Battle Run destination fills the shell-owned playfield through one shared Run workspace; content gutters and lipsanon reservation stay inside the continuous surface, and top-level outer panels plus raw browser controls are forbidden | accepted; Opening draft destination inventory partially superseded by [0321](0321-run-opening-is-the-normal-shop-and-draft-is-retired.md); supersedes ADR-0236 and refines ADR-0144/0059/0230/0031 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0239](0239-run-army-rows-use-a-divided-portrait-bay-and-explicit-type-hierarchy.md) | Run Army ledger rows use a frameless canonical portrait bay separated by the shared inner-role vertical divider; ChromeDivider and Chrome Lab support both axes through one role-owned rail/joint system, while status/ability/value fields use explicit readable design-system type tokens instead of browser `<small>` defaults | accepted; per-row frame ownership superseded by [0241](0241-run-army-ledger-is-one-continuous-divided-inner-grid.md); extends ADR-0092, partially supersedes ADR-0063 horizontal-only scope, and refines ADR-0230 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0240](0240-run-self-inspection-owns-the-left-shell-workspace.md) | Run Army and Lipsana are sibling Self inspection destinations in Controls; either replaces the complete left Play workspace through the shared fill-only shell primitive, suppresses the covered lipsanon strip, and keeps the underlying phase mounted, hidden, inert, and live | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0216 lipsanon-strip visibility while inspecting and ADR-0230 Army presentation; refines ADR-0144 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0241](0241-run-army-ledger-is-one-continuous-divided-inner-grid.md) | The complete Run Army ledger is one canonical inner 9-slice with one set of exterior corners; unframed unit regions are separated into aligned portrait, details, and Value cells by repeated shared horizontal and vertical inner-role dividers, with the measured clip apron preserving joint paint and the drawn scrollbar | superseded by [0242](0242-divided-inner-grids-own-one-rail-topology.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0242](0242-divided-inner-grids-own-one-rail-topology.md) | A divided inner grid is one shared rail topology: consumers declare tracks and rows, managed rails suppress endpoint paint, one mask-selected tee or cross owns every node, the scrollbar is a derived final track, and Chrome Audit exposes the reusable composition | accepted; four-corner cross-material clause partially superseded by [0243](0243-grid-crossings-use-the-installed-divider-joint-atom.md); supersedes ADR-0241's consumer-composed placement and extends ADR-0063/0092/0093 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0243](0243-grid-crossings-use-the-installed-divider-joint-atom.md) | Every non-corner node in a divided chrome topology uses the installed role-owned divider-joint atom; four-way topology draws all four rails beneath one accepted joint instead of composing host-frame corners, with no new media slot or consumer paint | accepted; role-specific grid seating clause partially superseded by [0245](0245-topology-junction-atoms-center-on-the-node.md); partially supersedes ADR-0242's cross-material clause and extends ADR-0092 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0244](0244-run-self-inspection-views-are-deep-linkable.md) | Run self-inspection is directly addressable as `/run?view=army` or `/run?view=lipsana`; Controls keep that presentation-only query in sync while unrelated route state is preserved and the persistent Run is never mutated | accepted; extends ADR-0240 and ADR-0211 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0245](0245-topology-junction-atoms-center-on-the-node.md) | Every topology-owned junction atom centers geometrically on its normalized rail node; N/E/S/W masks describe rail connectivity while standalone divider endpoints alone retain directional seating offsets | accepted; topology mask rotation clause superseded by [0248](0248-topology-junction-ornaments-remain-upright.md); partially supersedes ADR-0243's role-specific grid seating clause and extends ADR-0242 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0246](0246-boundary-junctions-center-on-the-frame-rail.md) | Perimeter topology nodes center on the visible frame rail: interior nodes use the grid-line coordinate directly, while a boundary node moves from the content edge by exactly half the role rail's reach, never by the full reach to the frame outer edge | accepted; extends ADR-0245 and ADR-0242 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0247](0247-run-unit-profiles-use-persistent-tile-backed-board-scenes.md) | A clicked Run Army unit uses the canonical board renderer and real board sprite on a one-cell terrain scene, never an enlarged portrait; format-4 units receive a persistent inspection seed when acquired, deterministically selecting a walkable live terrain surface and optional grass cover without storing media pointers | accepted; refines ADR-0230 and extends ADR-0073/0225 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0248](0248-topology-junction-ornaments-remain-upright.md) | Topology-owned junctions reuse one upright unreflected rendering of the installed divider-joint atom; side masks describe rail connectivity only, while the complete rail graph beneath the ornament forms tees and crosses | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0245's mask-selected rotation/reflection clause and extends ADR-0242/0246 | 2026-07-30 |
| [0249](0249-strategikon-book-art-is-the-title-control.md) | The open-codex pixels are the frameless Strategikon control: the art matches Controls-title scale, mirrors its border inset, clears the divider, and retains state-specific hover and accessibility information | superseded by [0250](0250-strategikon-book-aligns-to-the-controls-content-boundary.md) | 2026-07-29 |
| [0250](0250-strategikon-book-aligns-to-the-controls-content-boundary.md) | The frameless Strategikon book's visible right edge aligns to the shared Controls content boundary, compensating for the asset's transparent canvas edge instead of deriving placement from title typography | accepted; supersedes ADR-0249 and refines ADR-0231 | 2026-07-29 |
| [0251](0251-lipsanon-references-use-an-artwork-first-reliquary.md) | Enchiridion and Strategikon lipsanon references browse native artwork through compact canonical asset swatches with immediate styled tooltips, while the selected lipsanon record sizes to its content instead of stretching through the pane | superseded by [0252](0252-lipsanon-references-switch-between-rows-and-a-grouped-reliquary.md) | 2026-07-30 |
| [0252](0252-lipsanon-references-switch-between-rows-and-a-grouped-reliquary.md) | Lipsanon references begin with corrected named rows and offer a top-of-column Rows/Grouped tab; Grouped owns one inner frame around an otherwise unframed native-icon grid, while both modes preserve the selected content-sized record and styled tooltips | superseded by [ADR-0253](0253-lipsanon-reference-selection-owns-one-description.md) | 2026-07-30 |
| [0253](0253-lipsanon-reference-selection-owns-one-description.md) | Rows and Grouped remain available, but lipsanon-reference entries are direct selection buttons with no tooltips because the selected record is the sole visible name/effect/history authority; the main-menu workspace and record also shrink within the visible destination span instead of clipping at the right edge | superseded by [ADR-0254](0254-enchiridion-content-owns-the-remaining-menu-canvas.md) | 2026-07-30 |
| [0254](0254-enchiridion-content-owns-the-remaining-menu-canvas.md) | The main-menu and Enchiridion rails retain their canonical widths and indentation, but Enchiridion content no longer answers to the invisible 572px action-column cap: it consumes the remaining visible canvas through the standard end gutter; Strategikon remains Battle-pane-sized and lipsanon-reference tooltips remain absent | accepted; supersedes ADR-0253 and refines ADR-0231/0059 | 2026-07-30 |
| [0255](0255-backend-and-frontend-validation-run-in-parallel.md) | Pull-request and post-merge workflows run backend smoke tests and frontend contracts once in separate parallel jobs; the existing required image/deployment job preserves its public check name and begins only after both lanes pass, while backend `npm test` retains only its real production-shell/board-render/trainer prerequisites | accepted; refines ADR-0094 | 2026-07-30 |
| [0256](0256-individual-lipsana-are-routable-from-the-main-menu-enchiridion.md) | Each lipsanon is addressable at `/enchiridion/lipsana/<lipsanon-id>`: main-menu lipsanon entries are NavButtons to those addresses while the scene manifest normalizes the family id to the resolved section, so lipsanon selection is an address-only update with no veil; unknown ids fall back to the default selection and the Battle-hosted Strategikon keeps ephemeral selection | accepted; bare/unknown-section fallback partially superseded by ADR-0411; refines ADR-0231/0254/0052 | 2026-07-30 |
| [0257](0257-play-lands-on-the-resumable-activity-or-the-neutral-hub-root.md) | The installed Play entry opens the bare selector root, which resumes the one in-progress activity through its existing Continue destination once content authority settles, or reveals a neutral hub with no preselected mode; malformed selector states canonicalize to the root instead of Skirmish | accepted; resume-forward clause partially superseded by [0260](0260-play-always-presents-the-picker-continue-is-an-offer.md), and neutral landing/canonicalization by [0294](0294-play-defaults-to-a-multi-mode-continue-surface.md); partially supersedes ADR-0074's canonicalize-to-Skirmish clause and refines ADR-0231/0232 | 2026-07-30 |
| [0258](0258-derived-read-manifests-are-memoized-by-revision.md) | Thumbnail URL manifests are memoized per authority keyed by document + catalog/renderer revisions; reads serve the memo (stale-while-revalidate within one document revision), derivation runs only after content mutations, the per-level pass yields, officials warm at boot, slow requests log, and the pod gets honest CPU/liveness numbers | accepted | 2026-07-30 |
| [0259](0259-the-live-play-composition-is-the-authority-derived-views-conform.md) | Play is composed in real viewport pixels with its accepted title bar, HUD rail, and largest-4:3 live board frame — the design-canvas transform is removed; every derived board view conforms to the canonical 4:3 board window, and thumbnails change only their raster height (288×216) with framing/renderer revisions bumped | accepted; main Level Editor clause partially superseded by [0278](0278-level-editor-board-fills-its-authoring-workspace.md); supersedes ADR-0202/0226, moves ADR-0203/0204's ratio to 4:3, restores ADR-0192/0201 live-frame behavior | 2026-07-30 |
| [0260](0260-play-always-presents-the-picker-continue-is-an-offer.md) | The bare Play selector root always reveals the picker: a resumable activity (active Run or persisted battle) is offered as the leading Continue rail entry plus a prominent hub Continue card, never an automatic redirect; composition holds only until the Run document settles | accepted; neutral-root and single-offer clauses superseded by [0294](0294-play-defaults-to-a-multi-mode-continue-surface.md), with the duplicate hub card first retired by [0293](0293-continue-is-one-agnostic-resume-entry.md); partially supersedes ADR-0257's resume-forward clause and refines ADR-0232 | 2026-07-30 |
| [0261](0261-lipsanon-addresses-unfurl-with-their-installed-icon-and-effect.md) | Exact lipsanon-reference addresses unfurl publicly with the canonical lipsanon name and complete effect plus the exact installed native 64×64 live icon; browser behavior, backend validation, routing, and social metadata share one DOM-free lipsanon registry, while unknown ids remain generic and targeted art never falls back | accepted; refines ADR-0256/0198/0060/0059 | 2026-07-30 |
| [0262](0262-bundle-cards-are-scene-vignettes-with-authored-names-and-a-codex.md) | Bundle cards are portrait (5:7) faces whose artwork is a deterministic full-bleed 3×3 battlefield vignette seeded only by the canonical composition id (every carrier of the same pieces is the same card) — units mustered with canonical installed sprites, seeded cover, optional live props; every deck composition carries a unique authored banner name, and the Enchiridion gains a Cards section addressing `/enchiridion/cards/<bundle-id>` with the same routed/ephemeral selection split as lipsana | accepted; Enchiridion selected-record clause superseded by [0364](0364-enchiridion-cards-is-a-terminal-gallery-with-no-fourth-column.md); extends ADR-0225/0247/0231 and refines ADR-0256 | 2026-07-30 |
| [0263](0263-card-scenes-are-an-owner-authored-studio-instrument.md) | Card Scenes join the Studio as a catalog category whose composing surface is the REAL Level Editor in a document-free card-scene mode (`/editor/level?cardScene=<id>` — full placement interface; Level-Artwork/Unit/Zone/Rules/Status/Recovery/Play-test disabled; units derived and locked), with an authored card viewing pane (drag + width-zoom board-space rectangle) that every card window, capture, and art plate renders exactly; overrides persist as one revisioned card_scene_documents row (ADR-0089 shape) holding `{board (unit-less 3×3 canonical code), frame, salt}`, with Load-generated/Delete-saved as the ADR-0057 baseline path and a reduced cardscene Viewer keeping only the export capture stage | superseded by ADR-0277; extends ADR-0262/0071 and refines ADR-0029/0058/0059/0089 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0264](0264-run-lipsanon-identities-carry-anti-story-residue.md) | Run lipsanon names and original flavor fragments carry the four historical anti-story pressure sources; eight display identities replace modern, generic, or overtly supernatural names while stable ids and mechanics remain unchanged, and those eight show Art not generated until replacement icons are accepted | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0193's lipsanon display identities and ADR-0198's installed-art authority for those renamed identities; PixelLab-only replacement clause partially superseded by [0332](0332-eight-run-lipsanon-icons-ship-the-approved-resized-pixels.md) | 2026-07-30 |
| [0265](0265-run-cards-keep-core-identities-while-units-carry-modifiers.md) | The 49 Run composition cards keep stable authored names and flavor while particular units carry stackable Disciplined, Positioned, and Plagued state; whole-gold pricing adds +3/+2 and uses Plagued discounts of Pawn 0, minor 1, Rook 2, Queen 3 instead of half price | accepted; core-to-instance timing partially superseded by [0271](0271-core-cards-become-affected-when-drawn.md), exact pre-purchase disclosure by [0272](0272-card-types-author-effects-and-may-conceal-unit-targets.md), whole-card Plagued pricing by [0311](0311-pestiferous-cards-reveal-one-plagued-unit-at-a-time.md), and player-facing state name by [0341](0341-cacochymic-replaces-plagued-as-the-pestiferous-unit-state-name.md); partially supersedes ADR-0193's one-entry-per-composition bundle-deck identity | 2026-07-31 |
| [0266](0266-ataraxia-names-optional-run-difficulty-after-real-history.md) | Optional Run difficulty is Ataraxia: the first Run has No Ataraxia, later Runs may opt in, and each condition uses a real sourced historical identity with literal mechanics but no prescribed interpretation; Ataraxia I is The Great Mortality | accepted; unlock/stacking deferral partially superseded by [0268](0268-ataraxia-unlocks-linearly-and-stacks-cumulatively.md), and No Ataraxia baseline identity by [0291](0291-ataraxia-zero-is-a-named-tier-with-visible-impact.md); extends ADR-0193 and ADR-0025 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0267](0267-pestiferous-cards-lose-units-and-persist-when-empty.md) | The Great Mortality permits Pestiferous cards whose units are Plagued; at most one seeded random unit per eligible card is permanently lost on Battle advancement, and the card remains as a dead-draw burden when empty until an explicit removal effect purges it | accepted; eligibility/frequency deferral partially superseded by [0269](0269-every-pestiferous-card-deteriorates-at-one-eighth-prevalence.md), all-units-Plagued/resolution-time targeting by [0311](0311-pestiferous-cards-reveal-one-plagued-unit-at-a-time.md), and player-facing state name by [0341](0341-cacochymic-replaces-plagued-as-the-pestiferous-unit-state-name.md); partially supersedes ADR-0193's exclusive permanent-unit-removal rule and extends ADR-0265/0266 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0268](0268-ataraxia-unlocks-linearly-and-stacks-cumulatively.md) | Ataraxia is one fixed cumulative ladder: the first Run has none, completing the highest available tier unlocks exactly the next tier, and selecting tier N applies every condition from tiers 1 through N | accepted; baseline label partially superseded by [0291](0291-ataraxia-zero-is-a-named-tier-with-visible-impact.md); partially supersedes ADR-0266's deferred unlock and stacking rules | 2026-07-31 |
| [0269](0269-every-pestiferous-card-deteriorates-at-one-eighth-prevalence.md) | Every owned nonempty Pestiferous card loses one seeded random unit on every Battle advancement whether drawn or not; The Great Mortality initially targets roughly one Pestiferous card per eight eligible card instances, with the exact marking algorithm deferred to deck construction | accepted; marking point partially superseded by [0271](0271-core-cards-become-affected-when-drawn.md), prevalence details by [0286](0286-ataraxia-i-is-a-persisted-run-tier-with-draw-time-pestiferous-instances.md), and resolution-time targeting by [0311](0311-pestiferous-cards-reveal-one-plagued-unit-at-a-time.md); partially supersedes ADR-0267's deferred eligibility and frequency rules | 2026-07-31 |
| [0270](0270-run-card-ledgers-adapt-density-and-preserve-flavor.md) | Run cards have no guessed row cap before live testing: denser ledgers use bounded spacing/icon/type density steps, exact unit properties remain visible, and every core card retains its flavor text at the bottom | accepted; exact pre-purchase disclosure partially superseded by [0272](0272-card-types-author-effects-and-may-conceal-unit-targets.md); resolves ADR-0265's deferred density policy and extends ADR-0071/0225 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0271](0271-core-cards-become-affected-when-drawn.md) | The deck remains exactly 49 core identities; a shop draw deterministically creates one persisted affected offer with unit modifiers and any Pestiferous roll, purchase promotes that exact instance, and passing discards it without adding variant copies or removing the underlying core from later shuffles | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0265's core-instance ambiguity and ADR-0269's deferred marking point; restores ADR-0193's exact one-entry-per-composition deck count | 2026-07-31 |
| [0272](0272-card-types-author-effects-and-may-conceal-unit-targets.md) | Card types are causal rule-bearing classifications rather than one uniform behavior axis: Pestiferous owns whole-card attrition, while Tactical authors a positive contained-unit enhancement and may persist a target concealed until purchase; external modifiers do not retroactively change card type | accepted; type-line terminology partially superseded by [0276](0276-run-type-lines-declare-primary-families-and-affected-qualifiers.md), automatic Tactical description text by [0305](0305-card-ability-properties-do-not-synthesize-description-text.md), the Tactical name/open-ended positive family by [0309](0309-concinnous-names-the-white-positioned-card-qualifier.md), and Pestiferous all-contained-unit state by [0311](0311-pestiferous-cards-reveal-one-plagued-unit-at-a-time.md); partially supersedes ADR-0265/0270's exact pre-purchase disclosure rule and extends ADR-0267/0271 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0273](0273-relational-unit-placement-may-have-diminishing-value.md) | A role-aware placement modifier remains unit-instance state even when its preference examines the formation; a modified Bishop simply prefers the opposite square color from another Bishop, with no invented compensating benefit and no promise that repeated modifiers have linear value | accepted; name and lipsanon-relationship deferrals partially superseded by [0274](0274-lipsana-grant-unit-owned-deployment-abilities.md), working name by [0343](0343-agminate-replaces-marshalled-as-the-formation-ability-name.md); extends ADR-0193/0265 | 2026-07-31 |
