| [0274](0274-lipsana-grant-unit-owned-deployment-abilities.md) | Seven placement lipsana now grant the shared unit-owned Positioned or Marshalled ability to their respective piece types; lipsanon text names only the grant, ability definitions own the existing piece-specific placement behavior, and duplicate sources do not stack | accepted; Marshalled name partially superseded by [0343](0343-agminate-replaces-marshalled-as-the-formation-ability-name.md), Rook Positioned behavior by [0395](0395-eutactic-bestows-only-front-or-back-row-arrangement.md), and cross-ability grants by [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md); partially supersedes ADR-0193's direct placement-effect ownership and ADR-0273's name/lipsanon deferrals | 2026-07-31 |
| [0275](0275-run-cards-use-trading-card-anatomy-and-single-digit-gold.md) | Run cards use a Magic-like title, art, type-line, and a Contents Box anatomy; purchase cost is always one live digit from 1 through 9 over one compact blank gold coin in the upper-right title bar | accepted; frame-candidate and type-line clauses partially superseded by [0276](0276-run-type-lines-declare-primary-families-and-affected-qualifiers.md), automatic lower-box ability explanation by [0305](0305-card-ability-properties-do-not-synthesize-description-text.md), the illustrative Tactical label by [0309](0309-concinnous-names-the-white-positioned-card-qualifier.md), and the one-through-nine limit by [0325](0325-concinnous-applies-to-every-non-pestiferous-offer.md); partially supersedes ADR-0225's inherited price placement and temporary card-shell assumptions; extends ADR-0270/0272 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0276](0276-run-type-lines-declare-primary-families-and-affected-qualifiers.md) | The third generated frame is the selected Run-card direction; its type line is never empty, declaring Units for ordinary bundles and Units — Pestiferous or Units — Tactical when affected, with the Contents Box retaining the unit ledger and bottom-anchored flavor | accepted; live-frame import/runtime-renderer deferral partially superseded by [0283](0283-run-card-face-is-one-shared-live-runtime-component.md), automatic literal behavior prose by [0305](0305-card-ability-properties-do-not-synthesize-description-text.md), the Tactical qualifier by [0309](0309-concinnous-names-the-white-positioned-card-qualifier.md), and visible qualifier suffixes by [0339](0339-run-card-properties-and-unit-states-use-paired-icons.md); partially supersedes ADR-0272's type-line terminology and ADR-0275's exploratory-candidate/type-line clauses | 2026-07-31 |
| [0277](0277-the-card-scene-authoring-instrument-is-removed.md) | The card scene authoring instrument (ADR-0263: Studio Card Scenes catalog, Level Editor card-scene mode with the viewing pane, override document pipeline and `/api/card-scenes/*`) is removed in favor of the flavor-first illustrated card art produced in the parallel card-redesign workstream; the generated vignette faces, names, and codex of ADR-0262 stand; applied migration 47 stays in immutable history byte-for-byte (checksum pinned) and append-only migration 48 drops its table | accepted; supersedes ADR-0263 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0278](0278-level-editor-board-fills-its-authoring-workspace.md) | The main Level Editor board uses its complete authoring-workspace allocation as the measured, clipped, and interactive viewport instead of nesting a fixed 4:3 ViewPane seat; ordinary previews and analysis viewers remain canonical 4:3 | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0204/0259 and restores ADR-0201's actual-owning-viewport rule for the Level Editor | 2026-07-31 |
| [0279](0279-main-menu-insets-govern-full-workspace-content.md) | The main menu's responsive column-start geometry governs the symmetric inline and block content perimeter for Strategikon and the Level Editor Events/Rules workspace, while their shell-owned fills remain edge-to-edge | superseded by [0295](0295-shell-workspace-bodies-dock-to-controls.md); refined ADR-0031/0144/0231/0254 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0280](0280-campaign-and-war-libraries-are-peer-editor-rail-destinations.md) | Wars is a sibling collection row beside Skirmish Profiles and Unassigned Levels in the one persistent Editor rail; Campaigns and all three collections are authored children of its `editor-content` slot, so each destination change retains the rail and uses the shared transition-only fade; multiple Wars keep one active selection with first-private/default fallback | accepted; refines ADR-0065/0193/0207/0211/0212 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0281](0281-unit-card-art-uses-dedicated-database-prompt-plans.md) | Every one of the 49 core Units cards owns a dedicated live-media prompt plan with a card-specific historical scene and eye-concealment method; human faces may face front/three-quarter/profile but eyes remain unreadable, the exact prompt is database provenance on the future image candidate, and a routable Studio Card Prompts surface reviews/copies ungenerated plans without reviving the retired board-scene editor | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0262's deterministic battlefield-vignette and live-sprite-overlay art direction; extends ADR-0085/0265/0277 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0282](0282-units-card-art-uses-a-pixellab-pixel-art-core-set.md) | The 49 core Units cards use one atomic set of native 400×280 PixelLab pixel-art illustrations: unit roles control composition, historical anchors remain secondary residue, the forced eye-concealment rule is retired, exact or explicitly reconstructed prompt provenance stays in live media, Studio Card Prompts is the exact-byte review surface, and every runtime card resolves the accepted semantic slot without a packaged fallback | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0281's Parish baseline, eye-concealment, v1 prompt-direction, and standalone-acceptance clauses | 2026-07-31 |
| [0283](0283-run-card-face-is-one-shared-live-runtime-component.md) | Card Layout, draft, shop, routable review, and Enchiridion use one `RunCardFace` with the owner-approved tuning, accepted live frame/art slots, title, live coin cost, Units type line, composition ledger, and bottom flavor; runtime interaction wraps the face instead of substituting the retired offer box | accepted; one-digit cost description partially superseded by [0325](0325-concinnous-applies-to-every-non-pestiferous-offer.md); completes and partially supersedes ADR-0276's pending live-frame/runtime-composition clause; extends ADR-0059/0085/0275/0282 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0284](0284-board-views-render-the-complete-authored-visual-scene.md) | Every board view renders the complete persisted visual scene beyond the playable rectangle while gameplay addresses, input, rules, and the canonical opening frame remain playable-owned; War continues to use the shared renderer rather than a mode-specific policy | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0096's gameplay/thumbnail visibility clause and refines ADR-0098/0137/0162/0189/0191 | 2026-07-31 |
| [0285](0285-run-card-type-lines-use-one-optically-centered-baseline.md) | Every ordinary and qualified Run-card type line keeps the original 5.3cqw scale and 1.35cqw horizontal offset while sharing a lower 0.65cqw vertical offset, with no per-label scaling, rotation, or placement branch | accepted; named Tactical example partially superseded by [0309](0309-concinnous-names-the-white-positioned-card-qualifier.md); partially supersedes ADR-0283's exact vertical-offset handoff while preserving its shared `RunCardFace` and type weight | 2026-08-01 |
| [0286](0286-ataraxia-i-is-a-persisted-run-tier-with-draw-time-pestiferous-instances.md) | Ataraxia I is a persisted Run tier unlocked by completing the baseline; each shop offer independently receives the seeded one-in-eight Pestiferous roll at draw time, the complete affected offer and purchased card membership persist, every nonempty owned Pestiferous card loses one seeded unit on committed Battle advancement, cross-Run unlocks merge monotonically through browser and account progression, Card Layout exposes the authoritative prevalence plus realized sample, and Pestiferous cards resolve a dedicated typed frame slot without changing the shared face anatomy | accepted; acquired-unit and resolution-time target clauses partially superseded by [0311](0311-pestiferous-cards-reveal-one-plagued-unit-at-a-time.md); partially supersedes ADR-0269's deferred prevalence algorithm/instrument, ADR-0271's deferred offer-persistence shape, and ADR-0283's single frame-slot clause; extends ADR-0266/0268/0271 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0287](0287-deployment-is-a-dedicated-side-specific-authoring-workspace.md) | Deployment is a peer Level Editor workspace: War player zones expose the implicit Run-army consumer without a fake event, enemy fixed anchors combine with an optional randomized roster and on-demand zones, zero roster needs no zone, unused typed zones survive disabling, and exact capacity/overlap validation gates saves | accepted; zone-cardinality and selector clauses partially superseded by [0288](0288-new-deployment-authoring-uses-one-flexible-zone-per-side.md); partially supersedes ADR-0050's all-sides/no-painted-units authoring model and refines ADR-0144/0193/0279 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0288](0288-new-deployment-authoring-uses-one-flexible-zone-per-side.md) | New deployment authoring uses one automatically bound flexible zone per side: its painted squares may be disconnected, no selector is shown, create is idempotent, disabling preserves the zone without using it, and legacy multi-zone arrays remain readable but canonicalize to one id on the next roster edit | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0050/0287's multi-zone authoring clauses and refines ADR-0144 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0289](0289-run-preparation-is-control-first-and-ataraxia-uses-one-selector.md) | Run preparation removes pitch and authored-War copy, distinguishes continuation from replacement, and presents installed Ataraxia tiers through one scrollable selector with locked tiers visible but disabled | accepted; inline current/new presentation and Continue Run launch label partially superseded by [0290](0290-run-preparation-follows-play-master-detail-navigation.md), and baseline identity/explanation by [0291](0291-ataraxia-zero-is-a-named-tier-with-visible-impact.md); partially supersedes ADR-0232's authored-War summary presentation; refines ADR-0193/0266/0286 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0290](0290-run-preparation-follows-play-master-detail-navigation.md) | Run preparation follows Play's Campaign Levels master-detail language: Continue Run and Start New Run are action-column selections; current Run facts plus Play or Ataraxia plus Start Run appear in the fixed right detail column | accepted; Continue label/address clauses partially superseded by [0293](0293-continue-is-one-agnostic-resume-entry.md), nonbaseline-only impact presentation by [0291](0291-ataraxia-zero-is-a-named-tier-with-visible-impact.md), and bottom action placement by [0475](0475-run-preparation-actions-follow-their-decision-content.md); partially supersedes ADR-0289's inline presentation and Continue Run launch label; refines ADR-0074/0232 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0291](0291-ataraxia-zero-is-a-named-tier-with-visible-impact.md) | Tier zero is Ataraxia 0 — The Untroubled Mind; every Ataraxia tier uses the same numbered label, subtitle, and visible literal-impact anatomy, while persisted tier 0 and progression remain unchanged | accepted; baseline impact copy partially superseded by [0390](0390-ataraxia-tooltip-is-a-compact-cumulative-rule-list.md); partially supersedes ADR-0266/0268's No Ataraxia label and ADR-0289/0290's baseline-presentation clauses | 2026-08-01 |
| [0292](0292-play-hub-continue-uses-the-complete-choice-frame.md) | The neutral Play hub's resumable activity uses the same registered full-frame choice row as Run preparation: its label and detail sit inside one NavButton whose complete frame is the hit target, with no nested Continue control | superseded by [0293](0293-continue-is-one-agnostic-resume-entry.md) | 2026-08-01 |
| [0293](0293-continue-is-one-agnostic-resume-entry.md) | Play exposes one activity-agnostic Continue rail entry for the most recent unfinished Run, Campaign, or Skirmish; the neutral hub has no duplicate Continue card, and Run continuation addresses the already-selected Current Run detail while ordinary Run remains neutral | superseded by [0294](0294-play-defaults-to-a-multi-mode-continue-surface.md) | 2026-08-01 |
| [0294](0294-play-defaults-to-a-multi-mode-continue-surface.md) | Play defaults to a descriptor-free Continue rail destination whose fixed Campaign/Skirmish/Run/Levels rows expose honest per-mode availability; the most recent resumable mode is selected into a fourth-column detail with one final Play action, while ordinary Run preparation remains separate | accepted; supersedes ADR-0293, partially supersedes ADR-0257/0260, and refines ADR-0074/0232/0290 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0295](0295-shell-workspace-bodies-dock-to-controls.md) | Every shell replacement composes one shared edge-attached body whose frame and drawn scroll rail reach the Controls boundary; host-family block/start insets remain internal, the Controls-facing inset is always zero, and only the Battle field owns scenic gutter | superseded by [0296](0296-shell-surfaces-own-their-invariants.md); supersedes ADR-0279 and extends ADR-0144/0237/0240/0059 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0296](0296-shell-surfaces-own-their-invariants.md) | The application shell constructs its own Controls rail, Controls-attached workspace body, and retained/covered viewport state; consumers supply workflow content and slots but cannot omit or independently redeclare those invariants | superseded by [0297](0297-shell-workspaces-own-attached-bodies-and-inset-content-lanes.md); supersedes ADR-0295 and extends ADR-0059/0144/0237/0240 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0297](0297-shell-workspaces-own-attached-bodies-and-inset-content-lanes.md) | Every shell workspace constructs both a Controls-attached body and one inner content container: ordinary content automatically mirrors the host's start inset at the Controls-facing edge, while the semantic edge-attached variant keeps primary frames and drawn scroll owners docked without caller-authored padding values | accepted; supersedes ADR-0296 and extends ADR-0031/0093/0144/0237 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0298](0298-fence-posts-interleave-with-incident-rails-by-depth.md) | Fence posts occupy the half-depth seam between farther and nearer incident rail bands, so a rail crosses its far post and remains behind its near post; every board renderer, review surface, thumbnail, and pre-drawn occlusion path uses the shared depth rule | accepted; refines the board rendering contract and retires the unconditional foreground-cap policy | 2026-08-01 |
| [0299](0299-fence-review-choosers-exclude-retained-history.md) | Fence review choosers expose only actionable backend candidate or current accepted kits, never retained legacy bridges or archives; selection is exact, the dated 2026-07-10 route is retired, and unavailable review ids return to the ordinary fence editor | accepted; refines the runtime asset contract and migration policy | 2026-08-01 |
| [0300](0300-only-the-brand-mark-navigates-home.md) | Only the visible Chess Tactics shield navigates to the main menu; the title, screen name, transition status, and remaining title-bar surface are inert, with rendered geometry enforcing the shield-sized target | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0023/0052 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0301](0301-levels-own-an-authored-camera-coverage-boundary.md) | Every Level resolves one persisted or deterministic-default rectangular camera coverage boundary; the live viewport derives zoom-out and pan constraints from it, the Board page can show/edit/snap it with balanced, proportional, or fixed playable-bounds padding, scenery never expands it, and AI boards intersect it with accepted pixels | accepted; Board-page authoring surface partially superseded by ADR-0302; partially supersedes ADR-0189/0190's accepted-art-only/no-level-relative-limit clauses and refines ADR-0191/0278/0284 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0302](0302-camera-authoring-is-a-dedicated-level-editor-page.md) | Camera is a dedicated, routable Level Editor page in the main layer dropdown; selecting it frames and exposes the persistent boundary, direct handles, snap presets, and size readout, while Board retains only board-owned controls | accepted; automatic writer-edit/no-mode-control clause partially superseded by ADR-0303; partially supersedes ADR-0301's Board-page camera-instrument clause and refines ADR-0278 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0303](0303-camera-page-preserves-explicit-view-and-edit-modes.md) | The dedicated Camera page preserves explicit View boundary and Edit boundary modes; writers default to Edit, the full box moves directly, eight handles resize, Snap returns to Edit, and read-only sessions visibly disable Edit without removing it | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0302's automatic writer-edit/no-second-mode-control clause | 2026-08-01 |
| [0304](0304-level-editor-documents-are-live-shared-working-copies.md) | Every owner Level Editor page targets one live shared working copy; stale snapshots merge structurally, page presence never blocks editing or creates recovery, takeover/start-editing controls are retired, and retained history is collapsed and lazy by default | accepted; supersedes ADR-0143/0152/0153/0154/0157/0177, partially supersedes ADR-0139, and refines ADR-0140/0160 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0305](0305-card-ability-properties-do-not-synthesize-description-text.md) | Detecting a Run card type, unit ability, or modifier does not synthesize description prose on the card face; affected properties may still select frame, qualifier, and direct state markers, the shared face retains its generic optional rules slot, and the future tooltip/reference system remains deferred | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0272/0275/0276's automatic ability-description clauses and extends ADR-0270/0283 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0306](0306-browser-authentication-has-one-session-owner.md) | Browser authentication has one client session owner: bootstrap owns the sole identity probe/retry/state machine, all screens observe the same checking/unavailable/authenticated/anonymous snapshot, unavailable is never anonymous, and a source guard rejects direct or caller-owned parallel auth paths | accepted; extends ADR-0059/0138 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0307](0307-every-replaceable-region-is-a-director-owned-scene-slot.md) | Every meaningful drawn-region replacement is a closed, director-owned authored scene slot regardless of whether URL or persisted state requested it; sources project stable identities plus immutable snapshots, readiness cannot reveal scenes, and Run phase/workspace changes use the one persistent shell and shared lifecycle with Battle activation gated on commit | accepted; one-visual-class treatment partially superseded by [0462](0462-transition-choreography-is-derived-from-scene-ownership.md); extends ADR-0059/0205/0209/0211/0214/0237/0297 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0308](0308-local-development-layers-have-one-recovery-owner.md) | Caddy owns routing, devctl owns named-environment identity plus persistent top-level process containment/recovery, and Vite alone owns its backend child; failed component health degrades routing but cannot authorize devctl to terminate a living ready-era Vite process | accepted; extends ADR-0197/0199 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0309](0309-concinnous-names-the-white-positioned-card-qualifier.md) | Concinnous is the adjective and white affected-card qualifier for a Units card that causes exactly one persisted target to become Positioned upon acquisition; it replaces the Tactical umbrella, uses the shared card face and type tuning, teaches its harmonious-arrangement meaning in the Enchiridion, and migrates the accepted white frame pixels to a truthful semantic slot | accepted; Tactical-retirement clause partially superseded by [0329](0329-concinnous-and-tactical-use-distinct-frames-and-one-shared-coin.md), visible Concinnous suffix by [0339](0339-run-card-properties-and-unit-states-use-paired-icons.md); partially supersedes ADR-0272/0275/0276/0285 and extends ADR-0305 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0310](0310-concinnous-offers-use-a-seeded-one-in-eight-eligible-roll.md) | Each otherwise ordinary eligible shop offer receives a seeded one-in-eight Concinnous roll; the stored uniformly selected unit becomes Positioned on purchase for two extra gold, Pestiferous takes precedence, and the nine-gold ceiling determines eligibility | accepted; nine-gold eligibility ceiling superseded by [0325](0325-concinnous-applies-to-every-non-pestiferous-offer.md); extends ADR-0271/0286/0309 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0311](0311-pestiferous-cards-reveal-one-plagued-unit-at-a-time.md) | Each nonempty Pestiferous card publicly marks exactly one Plagued unit in its offer and owned state; only that unit receives the tier discount and dies on the next victorious-Battle advancement, after which one remaining unit is immediately marked, while removal paths retarget and empty cards persist | accepted; state/card responsibility partially superseded by [0397](0397-cacochymic-dies-at-combat-end-while-pestiferous-retargets.md) and player-facing state name by [0341](0341-cacochymic-replaces-plagued-as-the-pestiferous-unit-state-name.md); partially supersedes ADR-0265/0267/0269/0272/0286's all-units-Plagued and resolution-time targeting clauses | 2026-08-01 |
| [0312](0312-plagued-targets-use-a-live-icon-not-a-written-label.md) | The marked unit on a Pestiferous card uses a dedicated live-media status icon and never prints Plagued beside the sprite; accessibility still names the state, and a small neutral DOM diamond temporarily reserves the final icon socket until the generated asset is accepted | accepted; accessibility/explanatory terminology partially superseded by [0341](0341-cacochymic-replaces-plagued-as-the-pestiferous-unit-state-name.md); refines ADR-0311's direct marker presentation and extends ADR-0085 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0313](0313-enchiridion-filters-cards-and-previews-affected-types.md) | Enchiridion Cards filters the core deck by exact gold and contained unit type, while a peer Card Types section compares four affected-type slots through the shared Run card face and The Volunteer art; Pestiferous and Concinnous use accepted behavior, and Type III/IV remain explicit reference-only placeholders until parallel decisions replace them | accepted; filter inventory partially superseded by ADR-0510, Type III placeholder by [0329](0329-concinnous-and-tactical-use-distinct-frames-and-one-shared-coin.md), Type IV by [0339](0339-run-card-properties-and-unit-states-use-paired-icons.md); extends ADR-0231/0272/0276/0283 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0314](0314-run-card-presentations-promote-atomically.md) | RunCardFace keeps the last complete card visible while the next content/frame/art/unit layer settles its actual image consumers, promotes the entire card after paint opportunities, and cancels superseded pending generations so rapid selection never pairs one card's text with another card's art | accepted; extends ADR-0136/0283 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0315](0315-card-types-uses-enchiridion-master-detail-columns.md) | Card Types completes Enchiridion's four-column composition: affected-type names are selectable rows in column three and exactly one Volunteer-based canonical card face is the selected detail in column four, with provisional status kept on pending names and atomic face promotion preserved | accepted; Type IV name partially superseded by [0339](0339-run-card-properties-and-unit-states-use-paired-icons.md); refines ADR-0254/0313/0314 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0316](0316-plagued-icon-candidates-are-reviewed-in-context.md) | Plagued uses a dedicated native 64×64 PixelLab candidate family under one live UI-kit slot; every private option is reviewed in the real Enchiridion icon seat and at native size before any owner-selected candidate is installed | superseded by [0317](0317-plagued-iconography-depicts-the-condition-not-a-chess-piece.md) | 2026-08-01 |
| [0317](0317-plagued-iconography-depicts-the-condition-not-a-chess-piece.md) | Plagued iconography communicates sickness, poison, decay, weakness, and struggle through deliberately varied condition-first motifs, never a pawn or other chess-piece silhouette; rejected families are archived and excluded from review | accepted; icon-family name partially superseded by [0341](0341-cacochymic-replaces-plagued-as-the-pestiferous-unit-state-name.md); supersedes ADR-0316 and refines ADR-0085/0198/0313 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0318](0318-plagued-and-pestiferous-use-separate-owner-selected-icons.md) | PixelLab Option 03 is the typed Plagued Unit Ability icon and Option 01 is the separate typed Pestiferous card-property icon; both are reviewed together in their real Enchiridion seats before installation, and unselected candidates are archived | accepted; Unit Ability name partially superseded by [0341](0341-cacochymic-replaces-plagued-as-the-pestiferous-unit-state-name.md); refines ADR-0198/0313/0317 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0319](0319-run-openings-spend-nine-gold-on-one-of-three-seeded-cards.md) | A Run starts with 9 gold and three persisted standard Units cards whose distinct values are sampled uniformly from 1–9 before one core composition is sampled at each value; buying exactly one adds its owned card and units while unspent gold carries forward, and Card Layout exposes seeded opening samples | accepted; one-purchase cap partially superseded by [0323](0323-run-shops-allow-every-affordable-card-purchase.md), budget/range partially superseded by [0322](0322-run-openings-use-two-pawns-eight-gold-and-card-native-purchase-feedback.md), opening-specific transaction by [0321](0321-run-opening-is-the-normal-shop-and-draft-is-retired.md), retained-three-Pawns clause by [0320](0320-run-openings-begin-with-only-the-permanent-king.md); partially supersedes ADR-0193 and extends ADR-0271/0275/0283 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0320](0320-run-openings-begin-with-only-the-permanent-king.md) | A fresh Run's pre-purchase party contains only the permanent King; the bought opening card supplies every non-King unit, Pawn numbering begins at one, uncommitted legacy openings lose only their three source-starting Pawns, and committed rosters remain unchanged | accepted; King-only party partially superseded by [0322](0322-run-openings-use-two-pawns-eight-gold-and-card-native-purchase-feedback.md), draft-phase normalization by [0321](0321-run-opening-is-the-normal-shop-and-draft-is-retired.md); partially supersedes ADR-0193/0319's three free starting Pawns | 2026-08-01 |
| [0321](0321-run-opening-is-the-normal-shop-and-draft-is-retired.md) | A fresh Run begins in the normal Shop with kind `opening`; buying stays in Shop with purchased/Reset/Sell behavior, explicit Continue enters Battle 0, and the separate draft phase, Muster screen, draft fields, action, mode, and compatibility path are deleted | accepted; opening Sectio superseded by ADR-0494; mandatory opening purchase partially superseded by [0347](0347-opening-shop-purchases-are-optional.md), one-purchase transaction and format-8 shape by [0323](0323-run-shops-allow-every-affordable-card-purchase.md), format-8 vocabulary, starting balance, and purchase feedback by [0322](0322-run-openings-use-two-pawns-eight-gold-and-card-native-purchase-feedback.md); partially supersedes ADR-0193/0237/0319/0320 and extends ADR-0230/0319/0320 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0322](0322-run-openings-use-two-pawns-eight-gold-and-card-native-purchase-feedback.md) | A fresh Run starts in Shop with the King, two Pawns, 8 gold, and three distinct-valued affordable cards; purchases show a framed textual Purchased state without the generic active outline, use the Sell gold cue, and replace the former gameplay noun throughout current model, UI, persistence, tests, and docs | accepted; opening offers superseded by ADR-0494 while the starter army and gold remain; one-purchase cap and singular format-9 purchase field partially superseded by [0323](0323-run-shops-allow-every-affordable-card-purchase.md); `card-purchase` retirement partially superseded by [0372](0372-a-card-sounds-like-a-card-when-it-is-handled.md); partially supersedes ADR-0224/0319/0320/0321 and extends ADR-0059/0230/0283 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0323](0323-run-shops-allow-every-affordable-card-purchase.md) | Opening and post-Battle Shops allow every dealt card to be bought once while gold remains; format 10 persists all bought offer ids, each bought card stays visibly Purchased, other affordable cards stay enabled, Reset clears the visit, and opening Continue requires at least one purchase | superseded by [0551](0551-a-sectio-admits-one-card.md); mandatory opening purchase partially superseded by [0347](0347-opening-shop-purchases-are-optional.md); partially supersedes ADR-0319/0321/0322 and extends ADR-0230/0283 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0324](0324-run-card-frames-declare-native-content-boxes.md) | Every meaningfully distinct Run-card frame declares SHA-bound title, cost, art, type, and contents rectangles in native 1060×1484 coordinates; the shared face projects those boxes through one formula, Card Layout exposes their overlay and handoff, unmatched hashes use Standard, and geometry promotes atomically with the frame | accepted; steel-geometry assignment partially superseded by [0339](0339-run-card-properties-and-unit-states-use-paired-icons.md); extends ADR-0275/0283/0285/0309/0314 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0325](0325-concinnous-applies-to-every-non-pestiferous-offer.md) | Every non-Pestiferous offer receives the seeded one-in-eight Concinnous roll regardless of value; Positioned still adds two gold, so ordinary eight- and nine-gold cards visibly cost 10 and 11 in the shared live coin | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0275/0283/0310 and extends ADR-0309 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0326](0326-tactical-cards-grant-discipline-at-one-half-eligible-prevalence.md) | Tactical initially granted Discipline on one half of price-eligible cards while preserving the nine-gold ceiling | superseded by [0327](0327-tactical-cards-roll-one-in-eight-and-may-cost-twelve.md) | 2026-08-01 |
| [0327](0327-tactical-cards-roll-one-in-eight-and-may-cost-twelve.md) | Every post-Battle shop-card draw has a literal seeded one-in-eight Tactical chance; Discipline adds three gold, every core is eligible, prices may reach twelve, and Tactical resolves before Pestiferous | accepted; target timing partially superseded by [0328](0328-tactical-targets-are-chosen-at-acquisition-and-use-the-discipline-icon.md); supersedes ADR-0326 and part of ADR-0275 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0328](0328-tactical-targets-are-chosen-at-acquisition-and-use-the-discipline-icon.md) | Tactical chooses and persists its exact Disciplined unit at acquisition; multi-unit offers conceal the outcome, while one-unit offers use the shared shield icon because the target is forced | accepted; generic shield clause partially superseded by [0339](0339-run-card-properties-and-unit-states-use-paired-icons.md); supersedes ADR-0327's offer-time target and extends ADR-0305 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0329](0329-concinnous-and-tactical-use-distinct-frames-and-one-shared-coin.md) | Forged-steel Concinnous grants Positioned, blue-water Tactical grants Discipline, every frame uses one accepted live gold-coin source with a live integer, and Tactical replaces the Enchiridion Type III placeholder | accepted; visible suffix and forged-steel assignment clauses partially superseded by [0339](0339-run-card-properties-and-unit-states-use-paired-icons.md); partially supersedes ADR-0309/0313 and extends ADR-0283/0324/0328 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0330](0330-run-card-type-lines-use-the-lower-optical-baseline.md) | Every ordinary and qualified Run-card type line shares the lower 1.2cqw vertical offset while preserving its common size and horizontal offset | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0285 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0331](0331-run-lipsanon-replacements-use-owner-selected-material-motifs.md) | The eight renamed Run lipsana use the owner-selected material motifs proven by their calibration candidates; production still requires native 64x64 PixelLab regeneration and atomic review with the other eight unchanged members of the sixteen-icon family | superseded by [0332](0332-eight-run-lipsanon-icons-ship-the-approved-resized-pixels.md) | 2026-08-01 |
| [0332](0332-eight-run-lipsanon-icons-ship-the-approved-resized-pixels.md) | Eight exact owner-approved resized Run lipsanon PNG hashes may ship through a closed truthful-provenance exception; every other raster remains under the native-1x rule and the sixteen-icon family still promotes atomically | accepted; supersedes ADR-0331 and partially supersedes ADR-0076/0198/0264 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0333](0333-live-card-faces-derive-their-density-step-from-card-load.md) | The shared face derives each card's Contents Box density step from its own cell load using the four reviewed study tunings as the accepted ladder, stepping denser only when extras would overflow the card's actual Contents Box, then growing flavor text into remaining room (capped, clip-checked, never affecting the step); explicit tunings remain Studio-only experiment overrides | accepted; implements ADR-0270's accepted density adaptation on every live host and extends ADR-0283 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0334](0334-current-run-stays-visible-disabled-without-an-active-run.md) | Run preparation keeps the Current Run row mounted first with the shared disabled treatment and a No active Run description when no Run exists, matching Continue's empty rows, locked Campaign levels, and ADR-0289's visible-but-disabled tiers; active-Run presentation, the adoption-conflict card, and no-Run address canonicalization are unchanged | accepted; refines ADR-0289/0290 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0335](0335-the-strategikon-is-a-run-wide-reference-not-a-battle-only-workspace.md) | The Strategikon opens from the Controls title band in every Run phase, not only Battle: RunScreen contributes the same book control and mounts the same director-owned workspace over Deployment/Shop/Victory, only an absent Run repairs a `/run/strategikon/*` address, and the Controls Army/Lipsana/Sell verbs always address the Run root so they stay live under the open reference | accepted; refines ADR-0231/0250 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0336](0336-strategikon-ships-the-owner-approved-command-archive-background.md) | Strategikon ships the exact owner-approved PixelLab command-archive hash through one closed cover-scaling exception, a typed live-media proof, and the required app-ui drawable role; normal routes resolve the accepted immutable URL and the private candidate path is retired | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0076 for this exact hash/presentation and refines ADR-0085/0231/0297 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0337](0337-level-editor-brush-ships-the-exact-approved-option-01-pixels.md) | The Level Editor Brush replaces the Pencil shortcut with the exact owner-approved Option 01 64px bytes in the actual registered 20px glyph box; a closed SHA-bound live-media exception records truthful contain scaling and rejects every regenerated or padded approximation | accepted; refines ADR-0026/0076/0082/0085 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0338](0338-a-crafted-run-is-composed-from-real-transitions-not-authored-state.md) | A crafted Run is composed from the game's own transitions and deck lookups rather than authored state: overrides apply in the phase where each is legal, the opening Shop takes none, `/run?craft=…` is development-only and refuses a bad spec on screen without writing, and the model internals it composes with are exported rather than copied | accepted; opening-Shop craft clause partially superseded by ADR-0494; refines ADR-0193 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0339](0339-run-card-properties-and-unit-states-use-paired-icons.md) | Unit cards retain **Units** at left and use the right type-strip seat for a distinct card-property icon paired with a separate granted-state icon: Pestiferous/Plagued, Concinnous/Positioned, Tactical/Discipline, and Hieratic/Marshalled; names move to tooltip/accessibility, white remains Concinnous, Hieratic owns the selected steel-armor frame, and its Run mechanics remain deferred | accepted; pair names partially superseded by [0341](0341-cacochymic-replaces-plagued-as-the-pestiferous-unit-state-name.md) and [0343](0343-agminate-replaces-marshalled-as-the-formation-ability-name.md); partially supersedes ADR-0276/0309/0313/0315/0324/0328/0329 and extends ADR-0198/0305/0318/0330 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0340](0340-run-card-icon-fitting-is-an-owner-operated-studio-instrument.md) | Card Icon Fitting is a click-reachable Studio Viewer instrument that independently selects exact property/state candidates, tunes per-property placement plus one shared unit-state placement with per-control resets, and saves only a non-publishing design-portfolio draft until explicit owner approval | accepted; fitting labels partially superseded by [0341](0341-cacochymic-replaces-plagued-as-the-pestiferous-unit-state-name.md) and [0343](0343-agminate-replaces-marshalled-as-the-formation-ability-name.md); focused Viewer rail refined by [0342](0342-studio-viewers-are-entered-through-catalog-and-own-only-local-controls.md); fifth pair added by [0412](0412-praecipuus-and-primogeniture-join-card-icon-fitting.md); extends ADR-0057/0071/0283/0339 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0341](0341-cacochymic-replaces-plagued-as-the-pestiferous-unit-state-name.md) | Cacochymic replaces Plagued as the canonical player-facing unit state bestowed by Pestiferous; the selected poison pixels and saved fitting remain intact, while non-presentational storage identifiers migrate only with the deferred atomic paired-icon production cutover | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0265/0267/0269/0272/0286/0311/0312/0317/0318/0339/0340 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0342](0342-studio-viewers-are-entered-through-catalog-and-own-only-local-controls.md) | Catalog is Studio's sole cross-kind directory; a Viewer is entered from Catalog or a deep link, keeps `viewerKind` only as route/state identity, and exposes shared preview plus local controls without a global kind/category selector; Lab is title-bar navigation to the Level Editor, not an in-place Studio mode | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0029 and refines ADR-0058/0340 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0343](0343-agminate-replaces-marshalled-as-the-formation-ability-name.md) | Agminate replaces Marshalled as the canonical player-facing role-aware formation ability; behavior, exact selected icon pixels, and saved fitting remain unchanged, while the pre-cutover persisted value and media slot stay non-presentational until coordinated migration | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0273/0274/0339/0340 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0344](0344-opening-shop-cards-roll-qualifiers-at-every-core-value.md) | Opening Shop offers roll Tactical/Concinnous — and Pestiferous under Ataraxia I — through the same draw, precedence and pricing as every later Shop, in their own roll index space, at every core value even when the surcharge prices the card past the starting eight gold; the sole suppression repairs the deal in which nothing is affordable by dropping the cheapest offer's qualifier, and the server requires one affordable opening offer instead of pinning them standard, with no active-Run format bump | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0321/0327 and extends ADR-0286/0322/0323/0329 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0345](0345-hieratic-draws-one-in-eight-and-grants-agminate-at-acquisition.md) | Hieratic enters play as the fourth drawn qualifier: it resolves after Tactical, Pestiferous and Concinnous at the same seeded one-in-eight chance, adds Agminate's three gold, and grants Agminate to exactly one contained unit drawn at acquisition under its own seed label; active Run format 12 adds the card type while the ability persists under the existing `marshalled` identifier | accepted; supersedes ADR-0339's deferred Hieratic mechanics; extends ADR-0327/0328/0329/0343/0344 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0346](0346-run-deployment-is-a-battlefield-state-with-conditional-input.md) | Run Deployment uses the full battlefield with a phase-specific Controls workflow; Discipline places named units on highlighted legal squares, opponent pieces remain unresolved, meaningful Muster/Compass choices update the same board, and no-choice deployments commit directly from Shop Continue while the persisted phase and director-owned Battle activation boundary remain intact | accepted; conditional entry and flat choice order partially superseded by [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md), provisional-formation timing by [0348](0348-discipline-resolves-before-automatic-deployment.md); partially supersedes ADR-0237's Deployment-workspace clause and refines ADR-0193/0259/0307 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0347](0347-opening-shop-purchases-are-optional.md) | Opening Shop card purchases are optional exactly as in post-Battle Shops; Continue is enabled on entry, an unresolved Loot choice is the only Shop exit gate, and continuing with zero purchases retains the starting King, two Pawns, eight gold, and no cards before advancing to Battle 0 | superseded by ADR-0494; partially supersedes ADR-0321/0323's mandatory opening-purchase clause and refines ADR-0346 | 2026-08-01 |
| [0348](0348-discipline-resolves-before-automatic-deployment.md) | Discipline is visibly and mechanically first: while any required exact placement remains, Deployment shows only committed Disciplined Run units; committed Disciplined units stay fixed, and the deterministic remaining friendly formation appears only after the final Discipline placement | accepted; all-Adlected-first grouping partially superseded by [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md); partially supersedes ADR-0346's provisional-formation timing | 2026-08-02 |
| [0349](0349-the-final-deployment-choice-commits-and-camera-authority-follows-the-active-scene.md) | The final required Discipline/Muster/Compass choice commits directly to Battle with no Begin Battle confirmation; Deployment and Battle share one Run-Battle camera identity, while only the director-activated scene may publish minimum zoom, zoom, pan, or opening-camera state during their overlap | accepted; choice boundary partially superseded by [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md), Compass preview/choice press by ADR-0403, and immediate final-placement timing by ADR-0352; partially supersedes ADR-0346's explicit confirmation and ADR-0348's completed-formation pause; refines ADR-0307 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0350](0350-run-deployment-promotes-the-mounted-battlefield-in-place.md) | Deployment and its Battle are one mounted battlefield activity sharing the director key, readiness signature, session store, board compositor, and camera; the final placed position is promoted before paint, already-visible units never replay arrival, and combat behavior is gated by Run phase rather than a second scene activation | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0346/0348/0349's separate-scene/incoming-presentation clauses and refines ADR-0307; per-unit arrival clause partially superseded by ADR-0351 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0351](0351-unit-arrival-choreography-follows-newly-visible-unit-identity.md) | One retained battlefield tracks presented unit ids: Disciplined placements and the remaining formation animate when first introduced, while already-visible units stay seated and the board/camera remain mounted | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0350's blanket continuation-arrival suppression; overlapping-wave allowance partially superseded by ADR-0352 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0352](0352-final-discipline-arrival-precedes-the-automatic-deployment-wave.md) | The final Disciplined unit completes its compositor-owned arrival while the Run remains in Deployment; settlement automatically promotes the same mounted battlefield, then unresolved units enter as a separate automatic wave | accepted; separate automatic wave partially superseded by [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md); partially supersedes ADR-0349's immediate phase timing and ADR-0351's allowance for overlapping final-manual and automatic arrivals | 2026-08-02 |
| [0353](0353-battlefield-view-state-is-instance-owned-and-camera-ready-before-reveal.md) | Every mounted battlefield owns an instance-local view store; its actual-viewport canonical camera is prepared and included in readiness before reveal, while activation gates only input, clocks, AI, and arrivals; Deployment and Battle retain one such store | accepted; supersedes ADR-0349's activated-scene-only shared-camera publication rule and refines ADR-0136/0189/0205/0307/0350 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0354](0354-a-run-link-crafts-the-state-it-opens.md) | A crafted Run is handed over as `/run/craft/<id>`, a link that CRAFTS the state it opens: pressing it again restores that state from whatever the Run has since become, so it is both the way there and the restart button after a bug is found; the id is the fingerprint of the spec's canonical text and the spec lives server-side in `run_craft_links` (migration 50, deliberately not a readiness requirement), crafting moves to admin-gated endpoints so the link works in a built app, and the readable `?craft=` grammar stays a way to write a spec that is minted into its permanent id rather than a second kind of link | accepted; refines ADR-0338 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0355](0355-a-rail-of-sections-is-a-registry-entry.md) | Every rail of sections inside a retained shell — the main menu and its destinations, Settings, the Enchiridion, the Campaign Editor, the Play selector, and the Strategikon — is one entry in a sectioned-shell registry that derives scene identity, the instance chain, the retained region and the content slot; the Strategikon becomes one entry mounted under both the Battle and Run ancestries, so its rail transitions instead of swapping, and a registry-walk test plus a live gate fail closed for rails added later | accepted; state-driven Run overlap clause partially superseded by [0462](0462-transition-choreography-is-derived-from-scene-ownership.md); refines ADR-0207/0307 and applies ADR-0059 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0356](0356-continue-resumes-in-place-instead-of-re-listing-the-rail.md) | Continue's action column is the resume surface itself and presents exactly one activity — the most recently updated resumable one, as its title, facts, and a single Play action, with no fourth column, no mode inventory and no second offer; another unfinished activity is reached through its own rail destination, any other Continue address canonicalizes onto the shown one, and an empty Continue says so once instead of listing four disabled modes the neighbouring rail already offers | accepted; resume-action label partially superseded by [0474](0474-continue-resume-action-is-labeled-continue.md); partially supersedes ADR-0294's fixed four-mode inventory and fourth-column detail; refines ADR-0074/0334 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0357](0357-a-unit-awaiting-its-entrance-is-staged-off-the-board.md) | A unit's entrance is staged when it is admitted to the board and released when the battlefield is activated, so a staged unit is painted off the board rather than at its seat; a battlefield reports `pending`/`active` rather than an on/off arrival flag, `pending` being the state it prepares and is REVEALED in, which stops a cold battle from showing its whole army seated, removing it, and re-entering it | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0353's activation-gated arrival clause and refines ADR-0045/0351 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0358](0358-a-premove-is-judged-against-permanent-board-law.md) | A premove is judged against the board's PERMANENT law, not the live position: `rules.speculativeMoves` offers every square the unit's geometry reaches through bounds, terrain/elevation/water, edge fences and obstacles (rocks never move or die, so they block like terrain), and ignores everything the opponent's next move can change — blockers (rays slide straight through movable pieces), the destination's occupant, pawn-capture occupancy (both diagonals always offered, covering a capture that hasn't happened and en passant), king safety, and castling's clear-path/check conditions. Speculative recapture stops being a carve-out and falls out of the rule. Exact legality is unmoved at FIRE time: the store's drain still re-validates the head through `legalMoves` and drops the whole chain when reality didn't honour the prediction | accepted | 2026-08-02 |
| [0359](0359-run-card-text-is-centered-in-per-frame-boxes.md) | Every Run card frame owns its five boxes in native frame pixels, addressed by the slot it is served from; a text box is the whole drawn opening measured off that frame's own pixels on all four edges, and text placement is derived — centred on its ink inside its box, inset from the opening's real edges by one shared value — so no card, frame or type line carries an offset of its own | accepted; extends ADR-0057/0071/0283/0329 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0360](0360-two-run-card-frames-ship-normalised-to-the-shared-card-box.md) | Concinnous and Hieratic paint a different card SHAPE from the other three inside the same 5:7 element, so they ship resampled to the shared 1009x1402 painted box; regeneration was tried first and rejected on sight, and the resampled bytes carry a `run-card-frame-normalised-production-exception-v1` evidence schema that truthfully records native1x=false and is pinned to these two slots, these exact hashes, the native canvas and one recorded transform | accepted; extends ADR-0076/0332/0359 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0361](0361-an-installed-structure-draws-its-authored-halves.md) | A structure drawable's authored `back`/`front` halves and its eight-way source turntable are two separate media lanes: the installed lane (props, doodads, seats, renderer, canvas plan, thumbnails) always draws the authored halves, and a `<direction>-*` row never displaces them; the source-artwork lane reads directional rows and keeps its `south` → authored fallback, while a source-only landmark with no authored halves falls back to its south facing for that case alone — so installing a turntable for an existing prop is purely additive and cannot reseat or resize anything already placed | accepted; refines ADR-0061/0150/0173 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0362](0362-wall-materials-own-a-typed-projection-and-review-instrument.md) | Wall materials are their own typed live-media family: `wallMaterialSlot`/`wallMaterialMediaIssue` pin the ADR-0086 `128x336` frame (and square picker thumbs) and dispatch BEFORE the board-tile rules, so a wall never falls through to the `96x180` tile projection that made every wall candidate unacceptable; `wall-material-canonical-board-proof-v1` requires the candidate mounted on the real barrier renderer at canonical 1x from `/studio`, with each candidate pinned to its own slot entry in a batch proof; Studio > Walls > Inspect mounts every waiting candidate and carries Record owner review + Accept, `/studio/wall-candidates` canonicalizes into it, and selection is geometry-driven rather than keyed to one upload label | accepted; enforces ADR-0086 at acceptance; extends ADR-0058/0085 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0363](0363-the-ataraxia-ladder-is-an-enchiridion-reference-section.md) | Ataraxia becomes the seventh Enchiridion section on both hosts: it enumerates the installed tiers from `ATARAXIA_TIERS`/`ATARAXIA_BY_TIER` rather than restating their copy, and a row takes the rung itself into the mark seat every other reference section gives a glyph, leaving the heading as the descriptive name alone and no icon in any row; the rung mark is an owner-selected carved-stone numeral forged `0`–`X` in ONE pass so a designed tier never waits on art, read by live-media PREFIX with the typed numeral as the fallback render path; standing (Completed / Unlocked / Locked with the completion that opens it) comes from the same `RunProgression` the preparation selector reads, and the selector loses both its hand-written tier list and its hard-coded unlock sentence  The rung marks are the first `ui-kit` slots to leave that domain bridge-only default: they register a typed completeness validator and a domain owner proof whose sanctioned review surface is the Ataraxia rows themselves, rather than recording a Studio surface the reviewer never opened | accepted; refines ADR-0231/0266/0268/0291/0355 and applies ADR-0011/0013/0014 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0364](0364-enchiridion-cards-is-a-terminal-gallery-with-no-fourth-column.md) | Enchiridion Cards deliberately mounts no fourth column: after the two canonical rail predecessors, its terminal third column consumes the remaining canvas and shows the real cards as a left-to-right-row, top-to-bottom gallery inside one wooden KitScroll; filters stay pinned, descriptor rows/detail duplication are retired, and card addresses scroll to and mark the gallery face | accepted; filter inventory partially superseded by ADR-0510, `<card-id>` address token and gallery marking by [0373](0373-a-card-is-addressed-by-the-name-on-its-banner.md); partially supersedes ADR-0262's Enchiridion selected-record presentation and refines ADR-0030/0254/0313/0314 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0365](0365-props-stand-on-the-authored-surface-not-the-playable-grid.md) | A prop is bounded by authored GROUND, not the playable rectangle: it places, moves and erases anywhere on the authored surface — playable board plus scenic apron — under the same terrain-family, footprint-occupancy, ghost-preview and Move-tool rules, refused past the scenic rectangle because there is no tile to stand on, and required to be wholly playable or wholly scenic so a straddling footprint can never draw over a playable cell the projection gives no collider; gameplay isolation stays where ADR-0098 put it, in the export projection, so an off-board prop round-trips losslessly in boardCode and renders but never enters `layers.props`, stamps no collider, and leaves `validateLevel` and the backend's playable-bounds rejection untouched; Doodads stay playable-only and Scene Art stays the free-pixel channel | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0176's Props placement boundary; partially restores ADR-0098's off-board prop authoring; refines ADR-0096 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0366](0366-a-run-names-its-phase-as-route-and-its-repeatable-ideas-as-icons.md) | The title bar's screen-name line is a route a screen may name its own position in: `routeSlot` renders one bounded portal after the screen name and the Run screen fills it with its phase, so the line reads `Run › Shop` and the progress chip stops spending a line on it; Conflict and Battle each gain a typed `run-progress-icon` role at `ui/kit/icons/run/<idea>.png` while Ataraxia reuses ADR-0363's carved rung numeral as its one mark through a shared resolver rather than a second symbol for the same idea, the Run bar keeps exactly ONE framed chip (War name over the authored Battle name) while everything else becomes an unboxed measure — an icon and its number — with Ataraxia written as symbol plus tier and the word dropped, each mark's stroke is BAKED into the artwork by a deterministic 2px outer-stroke pass rather than faked with a CSS shadow, and the same bake TRIMS every mark to its occupied pixels and pads it to their bounding square so one seat size draws them all and the only spacing left is the row's gap — which makes the `run-progress-icon` and `run-resource-icon` families accept any square 16-64px raster whose runtime frame equals its own side, takes the shared gold coin through the same pass, and gives every measure a shared framed Tooltip instead of a native title — which fixes two general primitive bugs its first title-bar consumer exposed: a portalled popup now carries the chrome-family scope so it is not unframed floating text outside a screen's <main>, and a title-bar trigger anchors its popup below the whole bar rather than behind it, a seat with no icon decision yet is reserved rather than fail-closed, and selection is one owner act at `/studio?runProgressIconReview=1` that reviews, accepts and binds the `app-ui` role in that order | accepted; orientation-copy-only clause partially superseded by ADR-0409; refines ADR-0042/0104/0316/0318 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0367](0367-a-piece-type-can-be-broken-off-the-deployment-pool.md) | A War level's deployment geometry says per square which piece types may stand there: the Player Deployment zone carries `excludedPieceTypes`, and a broken-off type gets its own singular zone (`player-pawn-spawn`, `player-king-spawn`) which may overlap it freely — a dedicated square outside the general zone takes its one type, a shared square takes anything; a pawn is broken off because it is column-bound, a King because an author wants it in a named keep rather than wherever the roll lands it, and the model is per-type so a third is a data change; automatic placement becomes a free-for-all in seeded random order EXCEPT the King, which takes its square first because the Run always fields it and a shared keep would otherwise be honored on a coin flip (which also restores Agminate, the ability that reads the King's square); a unit that finds its squares taken is held back as a reservist, Discipline still reaches every square, and `W3_PLAYER_KING_SQUARE` blocks Save when nothing accepts the King; deployment zones are singular by enforcement at every read, folding duplicates into one per type without discarding a painted square | accepted; Pawn-only geometry and non-King ordering partially superseded by [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md), affinity-dependent ordering by [0381](0381-affinity-dependent-agminate-units-deploy-after-the-random-formation.md); refines ADR-0287/0346/0348, partially supersedes ADR-0288 | 2026-08-02 |
| [0368](0368-conflicts-open-with-bona-vacantia-instead-of-closing-with-loot.md) | Run lipsana move from the END of a Conflict to its HEAD: `bona-vacantia` is a Run phase carrying its own offers on `RunDocument.vacantia`, shown BEFORE the shop that leads into the Conflicts first Battle, so the lipsanon is inherited and then spent around rather than handed out as a reward; whether a Conflict opens with one is derived from the authored `loot` flags (a Conflict opens with a lipsanon when a loot Battle remains at or after its first index), so the official ten-Battle War lands on Battles 1/4/7 and leaves the final approach lipsanon-free without hardcoding a Battle number; the loot lipsanon is REMOVED from the shop rather than duplicated, because six reveal events would burn eighteen of an effective nineteen-lipsanon pool; `RUN_FORMAT_VERSION` 13 discards in-progress Runs, and the opening Shops pinned gold widens to 80 plus `RUN_LIPSANON_IMMEDIATE_GOLD` for the lipsana held, one table read by both the model and the server | accepted; opening handoff partially superseded by ADR-0494 and immediate-commit/choice presentation by ADR-0382; refines ADR-0193/0264/0346 and applies ADR-0057/0076/0085 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0369](0369-one-cold-load-ladder-builds-background-then-chrome-then-scene.md) | The persistent shell stops being a second, parallel loading mechanism: its art becomes a startup PRECONDITION (chrome composition decodes every image its own CSS references, and the bar's marks are decoded before App is imported), and one cold-load ladder — `background` -> `chrome` -> `scene` — runs for EVERY route instead of only the main menu, with the final rung handing off to the ordinary painted contract and entrance; `manifest.critical` is enforced (a declared id that never registers fails the scene) and therefore corrected to the truth, with `homepage-background`/`title-bar` removed everywhere as shell rather than scene and the Level Editor registering the `document`/`board-compositors`/`visible-editor-chrome` authorities it already computes; the bar wears the COMMITTED scene's identity rather than the browser's intent, and `reveal-pending` is inert as well as invisible | accepted; supersedes ADR-0205's cold-start arrangement; refines ADR-0042 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0370](0370-a-tooltip-defines-the-mechanics-it-names.md) | A tooltip carries the definitions of the Run mechanics its own body names, installing the ability-definition system ADR-0305 deferred: `RUN_GLOSSARY` holds one entry per card property and unit state whose definition IS the authored `RUN_CARD_TYPE_REFERENCE` effect or the `runAbilityGeneralDescription` the per-unit tip falls back to, so nothing is restated and nothing can drift; the shared `Tooltip`/`InfoTip` primitive resolves every call site with no opt-in, marks the named term with `<mark class="tooltip-keyword">` in the defining pane's own title color, and STACKS the definitions as further framed panes in the tip's column rather than nesting a second hoverable tip — the pop stays `pointer-events: none`, so a definition arrives on the hover or focus that raised the tip with no target to travel to; a term the tip is already about is dropped, definitions are never themselves scanned (one level, never a chain), a fourth term is left unmarked rather than marked without a pane, matching is whole-word and case sensitive against authored spellings including inflections, the measured column hangs above the trigger when hanging below would leave the viewport, and every pane is named in the trigger's `aria-describedby` | accepted; Ataraxia title-bar exception partially superseded by [0390](0390-ataraxia-tooltip-is-a-compact-cumulative-rule-list.md); supersedes ADR-0305's deferral; extends ADR-0217/0339 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0371](0371-the-chartulary-is-the-held-half-of-the-cards-reference.md) | The Strategikon gains a third Run register, the Chartulary — Held Cards, at `/play\|/run/strategikon/chartulary`: it is the Enchiridion Cards gallery itself (one shared `CardGalleryFilters`, the same gold-value coin groups, the same `RunCard` faces) and nothing more — a gallery item is the face alone, with no annotation box, unit roster or acquisition caption, because ADR-0364 already retired exactly that kind of row on the grounds that a card IS its own record, and the whole feature is WHICH cards are in the gallery; `RunCard` accepts a `cardType` so an owned card keeps the property it was bought with while the purchase-time captions stay offer-only, `acquiredAfterBattleIndex` is deliberately not printed as a Battle number (the opening Shop and the Shop after Battle 1 both record `0`), the section reads the Run and never writes one, and the rail's unit/card registers take the Enchiridion's Units and Cards marks instead of sharing one between adjacent tabs; two general link additions had to land first, because before them no link could show a populated Chartulary or land on it — the crafter's `cards=` field buys the cards a Run already holds for real in the opening Shop and carries them through every Battle before the target (gold restored, staged offers withdrawn, mutually exclusive with `army`), and `?to=<address>` lets a craft link land inside a Run workspace instead of one click short of it | accepted; opening acquisition timing partially superseded by ADR-0494; refines ADR-0231/0335/0364 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0372](0372-a-card-sounds-like-a-card-when-it-is-handled.md) | Handling a card sounds like a card: selecting a face in the Cards gallery or a property row in Card Types requests the installed `card-purchase` set through `data-ui-sfx` instead of the generic interface click, while buying in the Shop keeps the `gold-sell` transaction cue — reviving ADR-0322's retired runtime assignment on the shared `ReferenceTrigger` call sites, so every host of those sections (Enchiridion, Strategikon, Chartulary) inherits the cue with no per-page wiring | accepted; partially supersedes [0322](0322-run-openings-use-two-pawns-eight-gold-and-card-native-purchase-feedback.md) and refines ADR-0224/0364/0371 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0373](0373-a-card-is-addressed-by-the-name-on-its-banner.md) | A card is addressed by the name printed on its banner rather than by the model's piece-initial id — `/enchiridion/cards/country-parish`, with `runCardSlug`/`RUN_CARD_ID_BY_SLUG` owning one reversible transform and the id staying the key for art slots, craft, and persistence — and the addressed face loses its hand-rolled `.is-addressed` outline entirely: the gallery still scrolls to it and still marks it for assistive technology, but paints nothing over the art, leaving keyboard focus to the one shared `button:focus-visible` ring | accepted; partially supersedes [0262](0262-bundle-cards-are-scene-vignettes-with-authored-names-and-a-codex.md)/[0364](0364-enchiridion-cards-is-a-terminal-gallery-with-no-fourth-column.md) and refines ADR-0052/0256 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0374](0374-legatine-and-eutactic-retire-the-last-plain-run-vocabulary.md) | The Run's last plain-English keywords are retired and every layer that stored them moves with them: the qualifier **Tactical** becomes **Legatine** (of a legate, a commander's deputy entrusted with a detached force), the state it grants becomes **Adlected** (enrolled by direct appointment rather than by the usual process), and Concinnous's **Positioned** becomes **Eutactic** (well-ordered, drawn up in good array), making the four canonical pairs Pestiferous/Cacochymic, Concinnous/Eutactic, Legatine/Adlected and Hieratic/Agminate with no behavior change of any kind; a stored value and its player-facing name are now ONE word — `RunAbility` is `adlected|eutactic|agminate`, the modifier is `cacochymic`, the card type is `legatine`, the fields named after the retired words move with them, and `RUN_FORMAT_VERSION` 14 discards in-progress Runs rather than translating them, retiring the two-vocabulary drift ADR-0341/0343 opened instead of widening it; `runAbilityDisplayName` stops falling back to capitalizing a stored value so an unnamed state is a type error rather than a retired word on screen, and ADR-0370's keyword glossary teaches the new terms; the seven live-media slots are re-pointed onto byte-identical successors (same sha256, no regeneration, no resampling — the ADR-0309 move repeated), the originals retired only after each successor is accepted and verified sha256-equal, and the `app-ui` drawable roles rebound in the same pass because the catalog fails closed on a role whose slot is retired — completing ADR-0339's deferred paired-icon cutover; two general gaps in the media contract had to be closed to do it: `POST /api/admin/media-slots/<slot>/metadata` implements the patch the server already named in `media_slot_metadata_requires_patch` but never provided, and the atomic card-type row-texture group re-accepts all four members when one moves, with the departing member stood down to standalone so retiring it does not demand retiring the group; the two seed labels keep their exact wording because they are RNG inputs and rewording one would deal different cards at every existing seed; the craft grammar and the Studio card-variant address still accept the retired words so previously minted links keep resolving | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0309/0329/0339 and ADR-0274's deferral; extends ADR-0341/0343 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0375](0375-the-profile-owns-what-an-interface-cue-sounds-like.md) | A control declares an interface CUE (`activate`/`card`/`gold`) and the DB-owned SFX profile decides what it sounds like: `interfaceAssignments` is exact-keyed per cue with `null` for deliberate silence, schema version 2, migration 51 carrying today's audible behaviour forward, and the SFX Studio gaining assignment rows with preview and per-row reset — so what a surface sounds like is an owner edit by ear in the running app rather than a commit; ADR-0372's committed assignment and its asserted Cards/Lipsana asymmetry (never an owner decision) are withdrawn | accepted; partially supersedes [0089](0089-sfx-runtime-profile-is-db-authoritative.md)/[0372](0372-a-card-sounds-like-a-card-when-it-is-handled.md) and refines ADR-0057/0071 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0376](0376-held-relics-are-lipsana.md) | The held-relic vocabulary becomes one Greek root end to end: a **Lipsanon**, the **Lipsana**, in the **Lipsanotheca** that ADR-0231 already named — *leipsanon* (relic) + *thēkē* (case), so the container stops being named in one language and its contents in another, and its root *leipō* ("to be left behind") says in Greek what the **Bona Vacantia** screen that hands one over says in Latin; this is a full migration under docs/migration-policy.md rather than a copy change, so the retired spelling survives nowhere — `RunDocument.relics` → `lipsana` at `RUN_FORMAT_VERSION` 15, the identifier surface (`RunRelicId`, `RUN_RELICS`, `hasRelic`), the craft grammar's `relics=` → `lipsana=`, `/enchiridion/relics` → `/enchiridion/lipsana`, and migration 52 re-pointing the production catalog (`ui/run/relics/*` → `ui/run/lipsana/*` with its `run-relic-icon`/`run-relic-mat` components and roles) plus `run_relic_stat_events` with the index and check constraint a table rename does not carry; there is NO compatibility read, so documents below format 15 are refused rather than upgraded, which deliberately makes the format 1/2/3/5 upgrade paths unreachable and leaves in-progress Runs unsupported, while minted craft-link specs are rewritten in place so links already held keep resolving because the stored data moved, not because anything reads the old key; migration 45 stays byte-for-byte canonical per ADR-0174, so the relation repair for `lipsanon_stat_events` replays `[45, 52]` | accepted; extends ADR-0231/0335; follows ADR-0374's vocabulary retirement | 2026-08-04 |
| [0377](0377-a-won-battle-is-reported-on-its-own-screen.md) | A won Run Battle ends on a screen of its own instead of a card over the board it just finished: the new `aftermath` phase reports what the Battle paid and cost — gold won (and the lipsanon's share of it), turns taken, wall-clock elapsed, and the units that fell — and hands off to the Shop or Bona Vacantia on Continue, retiring the `Victory + <gold>` line that announced the reward inside the Shop; the reward is REPORTED here and BANKED on Continue, so the screen cannot promise a number the Run then fails to pay, and the three measures that nothing kept are captured at the transition because the turn count and clock unmount with the board and `battleRuntime` (which knows who fell) is torn down when the Shop opens — elapsed time is measured from a `startedAtMs` recorded by `beginBattle` and re-recorded by `restartBattle`, so a reload does not erase it and a retry is a fresh Battle; the FINAL Battle keeps no aftermath because the War's own victory screen is its report and it grants no spendable reward (ADR-0220), and the title bar separates the two endings as `Run › Victory` and `Run › War Won`; `craft=aftermath` makes the state linkable per ADR-0338, with `turns`/`seconds`/`fallen` writing the result a placed-not-played Battle cannot produce — through `observeRunUnitDeath` and a backdated start, so the report is still written by the transitions that write a played one; format 16 (on top of ADR-0376's 15) discards in-progress Runs, and the screen borrows the War victory workspace's background until art of its own is generated | superseded by [0452](0452-a-won-run-battle-pauses-on-its-visible-board-before-rewards.md); persistence and accounting retained | 2026-08-03 |
| [0378](0378-run-saves-name-their-version.md) | The persisted Run schema marker is named **RunSaveVersion** rather than the unexplained generic “format”: `runSaveVersion`, `RunSaveVersion`, and `CURRENT_RUN_SAVE_VERSION` replace `formatVersion`/`RUN_FORMAT_VERSION`; RunSaveVersion 17 accepts only its exact current save shape, older in-progress Runs are unsupported rather than upgraded or re-stamped, the client and server share one current value, and current-shape repair remains separate from historical migration | superseded by ADR-0379 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0379](0379-lossless-run-save-changes-migrate.md) | A RunSaveVersion change with a total deterministic transform migrates in-progress Runs rather than discarding them: migration 54 losslessly rewrites account Run version 16 from `formatVersion` to `runSaveVersion` 17 and advances its CAS revision, browser storage performs and immediately persists the same bounded transform, current normalization and validation remain exact-version-only, and an incompatible future change must record why the old state cannot be represented truthfully before requiring a restart | superseded by ADR-0380 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0380](0380-run-save-versions-always-migrate.md) | Every RunSaveVersion that reaches players has an explicit account and browser migration to its successor; active-development removals map stored references to typed tombstones or neutral replacements (a retired card becomes **Removed card** in the deck) rather than dropping the content or invalidating the whole Run, runtime logic still consumes only the current shape, and rejection is reserved for corrupt, unknown, or pre-policy data outside the declared migration history | accepted; supersedes ADR-0379 and refines ADR-0174/migration policy | 2026-08-03 |
| [0381](0381-affinity-dependent-agminate-units-deploy-after-the-random-formation.md) | Agminate Pawns prefer a same-row square immediately alongside another Pawn, and affinity-dependent Agminate Pawns and Bishops resolve after the ordinary seeded formation so the same-type units they inspect are already placed; the King remains first, relative shuffled order is preserved inside both non-King groups, and every other placement rule remains unchanged | accepted; phase grouping partially superseded by [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md); partially supersedes ADR-0367's random automatic-placement order and refines ADR-0273/0274 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0382](0382-bona-vacantia-keeps-a-targeted-take-provisional-until-the-shop.md) | Bona Vacantia's mat asks only which lipsanon to take: an ordinary choice commits on landing and reveals the Shop, while a unit-targeted choice lands provisionally in the held strip and replaces the mat with the exact Martial Prosopography ledger/profile; the profile confirms both ids atomically and no pending acquisition enters `RunDocument` | superseded by ADR-0383; partially superseded ADR-0368's immediate-commit and choice-presentation clause; refined ADR-0231/0240 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0383](0383-run-viewport-dom-is-a-scene-contribution.md) | A Run feature cannot mount a viewport and then privately decide which screen it contains: the manifest projects a discriminated workspace object (including Bona target, lipsanon, and unit profile identity), and `RunSceneViewport` is the sole capability that emits the Run viewport landmark/frame/layer from a required typed scene contribution; mat → target, Army ledger → unit profile, target ledger → unit profile, and every back action therefore navigate authored scene identities through the director, while build checks reject raw viewport DOM and portal bypasses, manifest tests prove every replacement changes the workspace instance, and the live transition gate proves the real DOM executes the lifecycle | accepted; supersedes ADR-0382; refines ADR-0059/0205/0307/0355 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0384](0384-bona-vacantia-leaves-its-room-caption-corner-empty.md) | Bona Vacantia leaves its upper-left room-caption corner deliberately empty: the workspace paints no heading, instruction, or placeholder over the authored room, the persistent title bar is the sole textual location label, and any future artistic room depiction belongs to the authored scene-art system rather than feature prose | accepted; refines ADR-0366/0368/0383 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0385](0385-scene-crossing-visuals-use-the-directors-continuity-layer.md) | A visual that physically crosses scene ownership uses one inert director-owned continuity layer through the closed `SceneContinuityPortal` capability and a typed `shared-element` contribution identity: the outgoing scene keeps the landed paint outside both fades until it is retired, the incoming canonical owner reveals beneath it at the same coordinate, and the carry cannot receive input, own a viewport, navigate, or carry application state; Bona Vacantia opts both targeted and ordinary lipsanon takes into this handoff, and the live gate rejects any frame with no visible owner | accepted; outgoing-component lifetime rule partially superseded by [0463](0463-continuity-handoffs-settle-with-the-director.md); refines ADR-0205/0206/0215/0307/0383 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0386](0386-shops-offer-read-only-intelligence-on-the-upcoming-battle.md) | Every Shop adds a deep-linkable **View Battle** destination that shows the next canonical Level through the shared pannable read-only board and Level ledger; fixed units are visible, setup-event opponent rosters are disclosed without inventing their unresolved squares, the Run army still waits for Deployment, and reconnaissance never mutates or activates the Battle | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0230's destination inventory and ADR-0346's opponent-information deferral; refines ADR-0193/0237/0383 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0387](0387-bought-cards-travel-into-a-title-reachable-chartulary.md) | The Controls title exposes the Strategikon's four direct section marks beside its right-aligned book, with the full rail and title shortcuts reading one destination inventory; buying sends the measured canonical card face through the director-owned continuity layer along one shrinking straight transfer into the real Chartulary shortcut, commits on landing (or immediately when geometry is unavailable), removes the held card from the Shop, announces its destination accessibly, and keeps Reset as the replay instrument; the persistent Purchased box is retired, and the one functional transfer remains visible under Windows' reduced-motion report without adding an arc, bounce, spin, or decorative loop | accepted; commit-on-landing and inertness partially superseded by ADR-0431; partially supersedes ADR-0322/0323 and refines ADR-0043/0250/0335/0371/0385 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0388](0388-remaining-shop-cards-settle-into-their-new-seats.md) | When a transferred Shop card lands and leaves the deal, the shared row FLIPs every surviving offer from its measured old rectangle into its measured newly centred rectangle across all three-to-two and two-to-one purchase positions, using live motion tokens with no guessed seats or duplicate JS timing; Shop content and phase controls remain inert through that straight settle, and the functional continuity cue stays visible under Windows' reduced-motion report rather than becoming the jolt it exists to remove | superseded by [0481](0481-sectio-offers-reveal-the-face-down-pile-beneath-them.md); inertness and moving-target prohibition partially superseded by ADR-0431; refines ADR-0043/0071/0387 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0389](0389-the-title-route-names-the-visible-strategikon-address.md) | The gameplay title route names the complete visible workspace ancestry: a Run retains its phase then appends Strategikon and its exact section (`Run › Shop › Strategikon › Chartulary`), Enchiridion appends its routed reference as a further segment, every Run phase and the ordinary play host inherit the rule, and title plus rails read the same exported section-label inventories and canonical address parser rather than parallel suffix logic | accepted; orientation-only clause partially superseded by ADR-0409; refines ADR-0231/0335/0366/0387 | 2026-08-03 |
