| [0390](0390-ataraxia-tooltip-is-a-compact-cumulative-rule-list.md) | The Run title-bar Ataraxia tooltip is a compact cumulative list of every active tier, one canonical carved numeral beside the model-owned effect; tier zero's effect is shortened at its source to **Standard rules.**, and this list is the one closed exception to automatic mechanic-definition panes so future tiers spend the popup's space on their own conditions | accepted; selected-tier heading and 24px numeral seat partially superseded by [0391](0391-ataraxia-tooltip-names-the-ladder-once.md); partially supersedes ADR-0291's baseline impact copy and ADR-0370's no-opt-out rule for this tooltip; refines ADR-0268/0363/0366 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0391](0391-ataraxia-tooltip-names-the-ladder-once.md) | The compact cumulative tooltip names **Ataraxia** once in its heading and leaves tier identity to the modifier rows; each row's canonical numeral shrinks to a 14px inline index beside its effect, and the accessible label follows the same non-repeating anatomy | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0390's selected-tier heading and 24px numeral seat; refines ADR-0363/0366 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0392](0392-sectio-is-the-run-disposal-and-acquisition-phase.md) | **Sectio** replaces Shop as the one canonical word for the Run's post-catastrophe disposal and acquisition phase: the player acquires expensive heterogeneous lots and inherits both their strength and their liabilities; the phase, state property, unit source, craft grammar, scene/DOM/media identities and title route move end to end, RunSaveVersion 18 plus migration 55 losslessly migrate active Runs and stored craft links, selected media bytes move unchanged, and deterministic seed labels plus the separate proper name Merchant's Shopkey retain their exact strings | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0230/0321/0366/0386/0387/0388/0389; extends ADR-0374/0380 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0393](0393-adlectio-and-alienatio-are-the-movements-within-sectio.md) | **Adlectio** names admitting an offered card's expensive heterogeneous people into the Run, while **Alienatio** names relinquishing an existing unit for value: card and unit actions, the Alienatio route/scene/workspace, stored operation collections, acquired-unit provenance, craft/model functions, tests, DOM/CSS and review-media identities use those roots; the still-unshipped RunSaveVersion 18 and migration 55 move version 17 directly to the final shape, while Merchant's Shopkey's property purchase remains a purchase | accepted; Alienatio clauses superseded by ADR-0511; partially supersedes ADR-0230/0387/0388/0392; extends ADR-0374/0392 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0394](0394-run-battle-undo-rewinds-one-player-decision-for-one-gold.md) | An active single-player Run Battle may spend one gold to Undo the latest player decision: the checkpoint before that player move restores the move, deterministic enemy reply, adjudication, clock increment and move-owned Run casualty/Reservist/Mercenary Boat effects together; the cost comes from the pre-move economy, one reload-durable browser match checkpoint replaces the previous one with no Redo, and the mounted battlefield survives unchanged | accepted; terminal defeat affordance partially superseded by ADR-0428 and terminal victory affordance partially superseded by ADR-0452; refines ADR-0193/0235/0358 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0395](0395-eutactic-bestows-only-front-or-back-row-arrangement.md) | Eutactic bestows only row arrangement: Pawns prefer the front row, while the King, Bishops, and Rooks prefer the back row; an Eutactic Rook has no horizontal preference within that row, leaving piece-specific stations and formations to Agminate | accepted; two-outcome row map partially superseded by ADR-0396; partially supersedes ADR-0193/0274's Rook outer-square preference and refines ADR-0374 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0396](0396-eutactic-and-agminate-compose-as-best-fit-row-and-station.md) | Eutactic and Agminate compose instead of competing: Eutactic chooses the closest available best-fit row from a complete six-piece map (Pawn front; Knight/Bishop one behind front; Rook/Queen/King back), then Agminate chooses a complete six-piece station within it (Pawn/Queen middle, Knight one-square-inset ring, King edge, existing Rook formation and Bishop color); Agminate alone reads the whole eligible geometry, and seeded randomness breaks only equal fits | accepted; partially superseded by ADR-0406's one ordinary-unit ability limit and Pawn station; partially supersedes ADR-0395's two-outcome row map and refines ADR-0273/0274/0345/0367 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0397](0397-cacochymic-dies-at-combat-end-while-pestiferous-retargets.md) | Cacochymic has one unit effect — the unit dies when combat ends — while Pestiferous owns the target lifecycle, immediately marking another remaining contained unit whenever its current Cacochymic unit dies; the marked unit's piece tier may still reduce the Pestiferous offer price, but pricing is not part of the Cacochymic ability description | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0311's attribution of death and pricing to the marked unit state and refines ADR-0341 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0398](0398-run-deployment-has-an-owner-operated-studio-lab.md) | Studio gains a click-reachable Deployment Lab that varies rows (including gaps and 3+ depths), seed, layout roll, Pawn/King dedicated regions, obstacles, roster, every per-unit ability combination, and Adlected placements; it calls the canonical Run deployment and projection paths, renders through the canonical board, and reads a canonical transient per-unit trace rather than reconstructing the algorithm, with every input URL-addressable | superseded by ADR-0399's real player-flow launcher; refines ADR-0058/0071/0367/0396 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0399](0399-deployment-lab-launches-the-real-player-flow.md) | Deployment Lab retains its canonical result/trace and adds a Player flow launcher that builds a validator-complete active Run from the current lab case, clears debug choices, includes deployment lipsanon triggers and a fixed hidden opponent force, replaces the disposable active Run, and navigates to the actual RunScreen so real Controls, arrivals, final-choice commit, mounted-board promotion, and live Battle—not a Studio imitation—own the test | accepted; supersedes ADR-0398's debug-only boundary and refines ADR-0346/0349/0350/0352 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0400](0400-deployment-playtests-carry-a-visible-return-to-the-lab.md) | A Deployment Lab player-flow launch carries the exact configured Studio URL as the Run's validated `returnTo`, and the persistent typed title-bar action names it **Back to Deployment Lab** through both Deployment and Battle (including reload), restoring the same case without relying on browser history | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0399's browser-Back-only return and refines ADR-0071/0104 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0401](0401-deployment-lab-generates-an-editable-seeded-crew.md) | Deployment Lab's explicit **Generate** action uses the case seed to deal six-to-ten ordinary editable roster entries—exactly one King, seeded purchasable piece types and order, and an independent uniform three-bit Adlected/Eutactic/Agminate mask per unit—then clears only index-addressed manual squares; later manual edits continue to round-trip in the URL | accepted; three-bit ability mask and loose starting army partially superseded by [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md), owner-set seed interaction by ADR-0402; refines ADR-0071/0399 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0402](0402-deployment-lab-generate-chooses-the-seed.md) | Deployment Lab's Crew generator exposes one **Generate** action with no seed-setting prerequisite: each click chooses a fresh 0–99,999 seed (advancing on an exact repeat), deals ADR-0401's crew, and uses that same persisted seed for canonical placement, while the resulting roster remains ordinary manually editable URL data | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0401's owner-set generation seed and refines ADR-0071 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0403](0403-surveyors-compass-chooses-between-ability-resolved-formations.md) | Deployment choices resolve in dependency order—Muster Roll fixes the roster, Adlected fixes exact squares, Eutactic then Agminate resolve two complete automatic candidates, and Surveyor's Compass adds no bias but lets the player view either candidate on the full battlefield before **Deploy this formation** persists the choice; upstream edits invalidate Compass, the final Adlected arrival settles before previews appear, and identical layouts collapse as no choice | superseded by [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md); partially supersedes ADR-0349's combined preview/choice press and refines ADR-0193/0346/0348/0352/0396 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0404](0404-muster-roll-leaves-the-lipsanon-offer-pool.md) | Muster Roll leaves the canonical seeded lipsanon offer pool until its role at the card-to-deployment-pool boundary is decided; its registered identity and current behavior remain for already-held saves and explicit playtests, so the deferral changes neither RunSaveVersion nor the database | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0193's all-registered offer pool and refines ADR-0368/0380 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0405](0405-surveyors-compass-leaves-the-lipsanon-offer-pool.md) | Surveyor's Compass joins Muster Roll outside the canonical seeded lipsanon offer pool until phased Deployment and its information locks are settled; its registered identity and current behavior remain for existing references and explicit playtests, leaving eighteen offerable lipsana with no RunSaveVersion or database migration | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0193's Compass availability and refines ADR-0380/0403/0404 | 2026-08-03 |
| [0406](0406-klerosis-deals-cards-before-one-unit-at-a-time-deployment.md) | Klerosis deals a Battle-limited set of held cards into a divisible unit pool; His Grace always leads and Primogeniture places its King first, then one hidden seeded Farrago queue owns capacity and one-unit-at-a-time placement, with Deploy all/Step through presentation, queue-local Adlection, single ordinary-unit abilities, persistent exact information, starter Chartulary cards, and one general Pawn-capable player zone under RunSaveVersion 19 and migration 56 | accepted; card-owned order, hidden stack, and Primogeniture partially superseded by [0419](0419-deployment-draws-a-hidden-card-stack-in-play-order.md), shared starter frame by [0413](0413-royal-purple-belongs-to-praecipuus-not-starter-status.md); supersedes ADR-0403, partially supersedes ADR-0346/0348/0349/0352/0367/0381/0398/0401, and refines ADR-0193/0274/0350/0395/0396/0399/0400/0404/0405 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0407](0407-expunctio-removes-one-card-per-sectio.md) | Expunctio removes at most one held card and all of its remaining attached units per Sectio for the card's full printed value plus those units' standard value; Alienatio discounts that fee without ever funding it completely, His Grace is unavailable, Reset restores the complete transaction, and RunSaveVersion 20 plus migration 57 persist the operation and its Pestiferous-loss snapshot | accepted; individual-unit disposal and discount clauses superseded by ADR-0511; partially supersedes ADR-0393's two-movement inventory and refines ADR-0230/0380/0392/0406 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0408](0408-the-enchiridion-always-names-its-visible-section.md) | The Enchiridion always appends its canonical visible section to the persistent title route on the main-menu, Battle, and Run hosts; deep item addresses stay oriented to their owning section, and title and rails share one label inventory | accepted; explicitly empty root scope partially superseded by ADR-0411; refines ADR-0256/0366/0389 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0409](0409-the-title-route-is-a-clickable-breadcrumb.md) | The persistent title route is a clickable breadcrumb: its base screen name and every canonical descendant are frameless NavButtons, ancestors return to their parsed canonical addresses, relevant search identity is preserved, and these intrinsic leading controls remain separate from the trailing typed action lane | accepted; Units-root ancestor destinations partially superseded by ADR-0411; partially supersedes ADR-0366/0389 and refines ADR-0052/0408 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0410](0410-address-derived-title-routes-belong-to-the-app-shell.md) | Address-derived title breadcrumbs belong to the persistent App shell rather than replaceable scene activation: standalone Enchiridion and ordinary Play Strategikon segments render directly from `titleBarConfig`, while only live document state such as the Run phase uses the route portal, and both paths consume the same canonical parsers and `TitleRoute` primitive | accepted; refines ADR-0369/0389/0409 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0411](0411-reference-ancestors-own-empty-routed-roots.md) | Reference ancestors own distinct empty routes: `/enchiridion`, `/play\|/run/strategikon`, and their nested `/enchiridion` roots retain their rails while exposing no child, breadcrumb ancestors land on those exact roots, Units appears only when explicitly addressed, and scene identity models each root as an empty content slot without placeholder copy | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0256/0408/0409 and refines ADR-0231/0410 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0412](0412-praecipuus-and-primogeniture-join-card-icon-fitting.md) | Card Icon Fitting gains Praecipuus → Primogeniture as a fifth exact-candidate pair on canonical His Grace with the selected Codex illustration, a direct `iconPair` address, shared unit-state fitting, and lossless version-2 normalization of existing four-pair drafts; review remains non-publishing and requires no database migration | accepted; Primogeniture fitting pair partially superseded by [0419](0419-deployment-draws-a-hidden-card-stack-in-play-order.md); refines ADR-0340/0406 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0413](0413-royal-purple-belongs-to-praecipuus-not-starter-status.md) | Royal purple identifies Praecipuus rather than starter membership: His Grace keeps the selected Codex royal frame while unqualified Front Lines uses the ordinary Standard Units frame everywhere, retaining its starter name and art with no gameplay, persistence, or database change | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0406's shared starter frame and refines ADR-0283/0412 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0414](0414-selected-starter-card-media-becomes-dedicated-runtime-identity.md) | The exact owner-selected Codex starter-card illustrations, Praecipuus frame, Praecipuus icon, and Primogeniture icon cut over to dedicated live runtime slots/roles; the saved fit becomes committed geometry, four SHA-bound resized derivatives receive one closed truthful exception, and Praecipuus joins Card Types without a schema or Run-save migration | accepted; installed Primogeniture role partially superseded by [0419](0419-deployment-draws-a-hidden-card-stack-in-play-order.md); partially supersedes ADR-0406's borrowed visual identities and refines ADR-0085/0340/0412/0413 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0415](0415-every-run-page-is-assembled-by-one-closed-form.md) | `RunScreen` constructs one branded `RunForm`, every phase enters it through `form.add(runActivity(...))`, and the form permanently owns the Run shell, title, Controls surface, Strategikon, lipsana strip and workspace swap while bounded activities provide only controls, viewport and overlay content; Deployment/Battle preserve their mounted battlefield inside the same form, direct shell imports are closed, and the old early-return/duplicate-Strategikon bypass is deleted | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0335's split ownership and refines ADR-0059/0307/0350/0355 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0416](0416-klerosis-is-a-dedicated-pre-battle-screen.md) | Klerosis is a dedicated full Run workspace before the battlefield: cards deal in exact persisted order into an unbounded wrapping field above the resolved combat roster, Confirm alone crosses the persisted information boundary, and only then does the canonical battlefield mount and ask Deploy all or Step through; confirmed Deployment and Battle remain one continuous scene, while RunForm structurally preserves the Strategikon and Run chrome | superseded by [0419](0419-deployment-draws-a-hidden-card-stack-in-play-order.md); partially supersedes ADR-0346/0350/0406 and refines ADR-0307/0386/0415 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0417](0417-klerosis-deals-from-the-chartulary.md) | Klerosis deals canonical card faces from the real measured Chartulary title shortcut, reversing its established Adlectio endpoint through the director-owned continuity layer: cards minimize inside the mark, travel centre-to-centre in exact deal order, grow into their wrapping seats, and keep Confirm unavailable until the deal lands; missing geometry falls back to an immediate resolved reveal | accepted; face-up separate-workspace composition partially superseded by [0419](0419-deployment-draws-a-hidden-card-stack-in-play-order.md); refines ADR-0387/0406/0416 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0418](0418-klerosis-presents-the-deal-without-summary-copy-or-rosters.md) | Klerosis presents its phase label, dealt card faces, and Confirm without “Your deployment deal,” explanatory prose, or a duplicate Deploying/Unavailable roster; capacity truth remains persisted and operational, while the cards and their Chartulary-to-seat movement are the ordinary player-facing explanation | accepted; separate Confirm workspace partially superseded by [0419](0419-deployment-draws-a-hidden-card-stack-in-play-order.md); partially supersedes ADR-0406/0416 and refines ADR-0417 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0419](0419-deployment-draws-a-hidden-card-stack-in-play-order.md) | Deployment mounts the empty battlefield, draws the exact deal face down from the Chartulary into a numbered Controls stack, then reveals and resolves cards in persisted deal order and each card's persisted left-to-right unit-seat order; Deploy all and Step through share that sequence, cards discard only after their final unit settles, Praecipuus alone puts His Grace and its King first, Primogeniture retires, and one universal live-media card back is reviewed in Card Layout | superseded by ADR-0422; supersedes ADR-0416 and parts of ADR-0417/0418, partially supersedes ADR-0406/0412/0414, and refines ADR-0283/0350/0387/0415 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0420](0420-the-fivefold-gambit-codex-is-the-default-run-card-back.md) | The exact native 1060×1484 Codex rendering of **The Fivefold Gambit** becomes the accepted universal Run-card back at `ui/run/card-back/standard.png`: five occult powers surround a chessboard contest, the approved review hash promotes byte-for-byte without resampling, and other study candidates remain non-runtime review history; this establishes the current default without precluding a later player-selectable card-back system | accepted; completes ADR-0419's separate owner selection and refines ADR-0085/0283 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0421](0421-a-preparing-scene-has-no-permission-to-perform.md) | A preparing scene may render, decode and measure but has no permission to perform: each SceneBoundary owns an inactive SceneActivity authority, entered choreography runs only after director commit, imperative Web Animations require its motion capability, descendant CSS motion is held during preparation, and deactivation cancels the scoped work; Deployment's deal/reveal/discard and Sectio's FLIP move behind that boundary, with a static guard closing raw `Element.animate` as a feature-level escape hatch | accepted; refines ADR-0205/0307/0415/0419 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0422](0422-deployment-deals-a-visible-deck-before-transport-begins.md) | Deployment opens on the empty battlefield with the complete held deck face down at center and a Deal action; the persisted deal sends combat cards deliberately one at a time to a prominent upper-left Controls pile and the counted remainder to the Chartulary, a shared device-local Deal automatically setting may initiate the same action, and paused/play/next/full-deploy transport replaces the old two-mode choice without changing card order, information locks, required Adlected pauses, settlement, discard, or battlefield promotion; RunSaveVersion and account storage migrate losslessly to the new boundary | accepted; Full deploy pace partially superseded by [0435](0435-full-deploy-is-one-placement-wave-and-one-discard.md), pre-Deal transport availability by [0436](0436-deployment-transport-may-own-deal-and-attention-is-serial.md); supersedes ADR-0419's automatic source, pile position, and mode boundary; refines ADR-0307/0350/0415/0421 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0423](0423-run-battle-retry-costs-five-gold.md) | Every in-Battle Restart and terminal Retry of the current Run Battle atomically spends five gold through one model transition, is unavailable below that balance, states the canonical live-gold cost, and preserves the already-mounted battlefield; non-Run retries remain free and no save or database shape changes | superseded by [0424](0424-run-battle-retry-costs-three-gold.md); refines ADR-0193/0235/0394 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0424](0424-run-battle-retry-costs-three-gold.md) | Every in-Battle Restart and terminal Retry of the current Run Battle atomically spends three gold through the existing paid reset transition, is unavailable below that balance, and states the canonical live-gold cost without changing the in-place visual lifecycle, non-Run retries, or persistence shape | accepted; supersedes ADR-0423 and refines ADR-0193/0235/0394 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0425](0425-run-battle-restart-unlocks-after-the-first-turn.md) | Paid Restart is disabled while an active Run Battle remains on its first turn and unlocks after the first complete turn, while terminal Retry remains available after a first-turn defeat, draw, or resignation so the Run cannot be stranded; affordability remains independently authoritative | accepted; resignation clause partially superseded by [0426](0426-run-battles-do-not-offer-resign.md); refines ADR-0235/0394/0424 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0426](0426-run-battles-do-not-offer-resign.md) | Run Battles do not expose Resign: it only manufactured a Defeat whose meaningful action was the already-available paid Retry, while Undo owns correcting a decision and Abandon Run owns ending the persistent Run; resignation remains unchanged outside Run | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0425's first-turn resignation clause and refines ADR-0193/0235/0394/0424 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0427](0427-deployment-cards-retain-their-authored-seat-geometry.md) | An active Deployment card retains its complete authored ledger, density, unit scale, and stack positions while played, sold, or lost occurrences become visibly empty seats with occupied counts; the canonical face projection and atomic media signature own the mask, and existing nullable seats plus unit cursor require no persistence migration | accepted; supersedes ADR-0419's visible-face condensation allowance and refines ADR-0283/0422 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0428](0428-run-defeat-offers-retry-and-exits-without-blocking-controls.md) | A defeated Run Battle removes Undo, retains paid Retry, and adds explicit New Run and Main Menu exits; its non-modal result shade stays inside the battlefield viewport so the persistent Controls panel remains available for post-Battle inspection | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0394's terminal-defeat Undo affordance and refines ADR-0235/0290/0300/0415/0424/0425 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0429](0429-level-format-versions-always-migrate.md) | Every durable Level format has an explicit forward migration; Level 2 and migration 61 complete Pawn-zone retirement across every storage boundary, advance embedded Runs to RunSaveVersion 23, reconstruct saved working-copy baselines from retained revision evidence, and keep conflict Discard reachable | accepted; baseline evidence completeness partially superseded by ADR-0430; refines ADR-0174/0304/0380/0406 and the migration policy | 2026-08-04 |
| [0430](0430-pruned-saved-revisions-retain-baseline-evidence.md) | When the exact saved revision has been pruned, migration 62 may restore only the newest retained non-null baseline hash carrying the same saved-revision identity; the historical hash remains a deliberate conflict until explicit fenced Discard, and missing evidence still fails readiness closed | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0429's exact-revision-only completeness and refines ADR-0174/0304 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0431](0431-sectio-transactions-never-wait-for-presentation.md) | Adlectio commits immediately against the latest Run and launches an independent presentation-only transfer; concurrent flights coexist, moving survivors stay clickable, interrupted FLIP reflows continue from current pixels, and no Sectio viewport, card, or phase control becomes inert or disabled because animation is running | accepted; survivor-FLIP clauses superseded by [0481](0481-sectio-offers-reveal-the-face-down-pile-beneath-them.md); partially supersedes ADR-0387/0388 and refines ADR-0043/0421 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0432](0432-aliene-is-the-alienatio-action-verb.md) | **Alienatio** remains the Run operation noun while archaic legal English **Aliene** becomes its unit-action command and **Aliened** its completed-state word, completing the grammatical action pairs Adlectio/Adlect, Alienatio/Aliene, and Expunctio/Expunct without changing persisted Run vocabulary | superseded by ADR-0511 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0433](0433-leaf-chrome-uses-oak-over-structural-teal-fields.md) | Cool teal/blue stone establishes structural fields that host subordinate units, while the installed oak surface identifies terminal leaf controls and status plates; the first Run rollout covers dropdown triggers, operation and Controls actions, Army-profile actions, and the Run identity plate with data-owned texture phase | accepted; refines ADR-0034/0063/0102 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0434](0434-scene-art-uses-its-ground-contact-for-shared-depth.md) | Free-pixel Scene Art derives a render-time ground contact from its installed directional sprite anchor and enters the canonical continuous structure depth bands, so nearer walls and other scene objects occlude it correctly without persisted board coordinates, z values, or manual collection reordering | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0147's no-depth/always-overlay clauses and refines ADR-0176 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0435](0435-full-deploy-is-one-placement-wave-and-one-discard.md) | Full deploy commits every remaining automatic unit across every remaining card in one model transition and one compositor arrival wave, then shuffles the complete remaining pile into discard once; Play and Next retain the observable unit/card sequence, while required Adlected input still pauses the batch | accepted; serial attention and measured discard refined by [0436](0436-deployment-transport-may-own-deal-and-attention-is-serial.md); supersedes ADR-0419's per-card Deploy-all sequence, partially supersedes ADR-0422's fastest-pace interpretation, and refines ADR-0406/0350 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0436](0436-deployment-transport-may-own-deal-and-attention-is-serial.md) | Play, Next, and Full deploy may initiate Deal with their intent preserved; a paused deal leaves every card face down, Full deploy presents one exclusive unit-arrival beat followed only after settlement by measured pile-to-Chartulary flights, and Play/Next retain serial reveal, arrival, and discard beats | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0422's unavailable pre-Deal transport and refines ADR-0421/0435 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0442](0442-expunctio-is-a-card-first-gallery.md) | Expunctio is a two-column card-first gallery whose tiles grow from the complete canonical face and scroll through the shared drawn rail; narrow layouts reduce columns without collapsing the card, and His Grace globally keeps its gold socket while omitting the misleading zero numeral and zero-price accessible copy | accepted; refines ADR-0030/0283/0407 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0443](0443-athetize-is-the-card-action-within-expunctio.md) | Expunctio remains the canonical movement, workspace, fee, and persisted transaction, while **Athetize** is the deliberately unfamiliar card-level verb and **Athetized this visit** its completed state; literal screen copy continues to teach the complete consequence without changing Run or database storage | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0432's Expunct command and refines ADR-0309/0341/0407/0432/0442 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0444](0444-expunctio-card-tiles-remain-opaque-in-every-state.md) | Expunctio card tiles retain full opacity when unavailable, unaffordable, spent, or completed; the canonical card and shared inner chrome remain solid while status copy and the disabled action control communicate transaction state | accepted; refines ADR-0218/0283/0442 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0445](0445-card-companions-align-to-painted-frame-keylines.md) | Card-adjacent copy and controls align to measured painted frame keylines rather than the transparent 5:7 canvas: canonical frame geometry owns responsive paint bounds, the heading cap-height meets the top rail, and the action rail meets the lower rail | accepted; heading-anchor clause superseded by [0446](0446-expunctio-tiles-use-shell-surface-and-oak-actions.md); refines ADR-0027/0283/0442 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0446](0446-expunctio-tiles-use-shell-surface-and-oak-actions.md) | Expunctio tiles stop repeating the card's printed title, keep their inner frame over the installed outer-role surface shared by the title bar and Controls panel, and render their canonical action frame over the registered oak surface | accepted; refines ADR-0059/0218/0433/0442/0445 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0447](0447-expunctio-scroll-end-shares-the-terminal-tile-keyline.md) | At the end of the Expunctio gallery, the terminal tile frame and the shared drawn scrollbar terminate on one bottom keyline; the scroll content therefore has no trailing block-end inset, while its opening inset and canonical always-present rail remain unchanged | superseded by [ADR-0448](0448-expunctio-scrollbar-keys-to-the-terminal-frame-rail.md) | 2026-08-05 |
| [0448](0448-expunctio-scrollbar-keys-to-the-terminal-frame-rail.md) | Expunctio keys the drawn scrollbar to the terminal tile's straight frame rail while its corner atom continues through the measured clip apron; `KitScroll` sizes, positions, and drags its thumb against the rendered rail's actual height | accepted; supersedes ADR-0447; refines ADR-0030/0093/0442 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0449](0449-deployment-position-rerolls-cost-one-before-battle-and-five-after.md) | Reroll deployment costs one gold at every Deployment boundary and five gold after promotion into Battle; either price atomically restarts the complete placement sequence with a new position seed while preserving the combat deal, and the post-placement path re-promotes one fresh browser match inside the mounted battlefield | accepted; refines ADR-0193/0350/0394/0422/0424 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0450](0450-live-units-leave-mounted-boards-through-registered-departure-tracks.md) | A still-live unit leaves a mounted board only through a reason-mapped track in one closed physical departure registry; Deployment reroll defaults to a compositor-owned home-edge withdrawal, freezes conflicting Deployment/Battle authority, and commits its atomic gold/reset transition only after every visible unit clears the board | accepted; refines ADR-0350/0351/0449 | 2026-08-04 |
| [0451](0451-untimed-levels-display-elapsed-battle-time.md) | An untimed Level keeps the central Battle-clock chip but replaces the static infinity with a monotonic `m:ss` elapsed-time readout: it starts only when the painted Battle activates, includes both sides' live turns, freezes at the terminal/departure boundary, resets for a new attempt, and resumes from a banked device-local duration without gaining any flag-fall rule | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0053's no-chip untimed display clause and retires the later undocumented infinity placeholder; refines ADR-0307 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0452](0452-a-won-run-battle-pauses-on-its-visible-board-before-rewards.md) | A won Run Battle pauses on the fully visible, inspectable final board with a fading bottom **Victory** and prominent **Rewards >** action; terminal-victory Undo, the shade/card recap, duplicated aftermath Conflict/Battle copy, and non-Sectio's no-op Run views group are removed, while the persisted aftermath report and reward-banking transition remain unchanged | accepted; supersedes ADR-0377's presentation decision, partially supersedes ADR-0394's terminal-victory Undo, and refines ADR-0415 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0453](0453-a-terminal-run-battle-is-a-craftable-review-landing.md) | `craft=battle-victory` composes an ordinary valid persisted Battle and carries its terminal player-win presentation only in the admin craft response; the client keys that transient instruction to the fresh Run/Battle, waits for the exact board to paint, applies the existing one-shot victory action, and clears it, so the minted craft link repeatedly lands directly on the visible-board Victory surface without changing RunSaveVersion or persisted match state | accepted; immediate-clear timing partially superseded by [0460](0460-a-crafted-terminal-marker-lasts-through-its-victory-surface.md); refines ADR-0071/0338/0354/0452 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0454](0454-run-phases-retire-before-their-successors-reveal.md) | A complete Run phase retires before its successor reveals: the outgoing scene remains an exact committed snapshot throughout its fade, the prepared incoming scene stays invisible until the same boundary completes, and only same-phase workspace changes retain their narrower crossfade; presentation-object churn cannot restart or otherwise mutate a retiring Battle | accepted; presentation sequencing partially superseded by [0458](0458-aftermath-uses-an-out-in-dissolve.md), [0461](0461-aftermath-crossfades-directly-without-a-homepage-floor.md), and [0462](0462-transition-choreography-is-derived-from-scene-ownership.md); refines ADR-0205/0307/0452 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0455](0455-aftermath-retains-a-reversible-terminal-board-review.md) | The aftermath report offers Back to its exact won board while the Run remains durably in aftermath: a matching terminal player-win board snapshot is retained locally through that report, the review's Rewards action returns to the same captured report without recomputation, Continue or abandonment retires the snapshot, transitions in either direction replace the complete scene, and sparse non-Sectio Controls are static layouts rather than false scroll panes | accepted; refines ADR-0030/0452/0454 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0456](0456-aftermath-report-owns-the-optical-center.md) | The aftermath information box is the screen's dominant focal point and owns the viewport's 45%-from-top optical center; Victory is its display heading and Back/Continue follow below, but neither changes the box's anchor by making the combined stack the centered object | accepted; refines ADR-0452/0455 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0457](0457-aftermath-back-uses-a-mandatory-scene-handoff.md) | A won Run Battle hands its exact terminal match directly to the aftermath scene before best-effort browser persistence, so the report reached through Rewards always presents Back; exact-identity disk storage remains the reload fallback, and neither path may substitute another Run, Battle, or Level | accepted; refines ADR-0455 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0458](0458-aftermath-uses-an-out-in-dissolve.md) | The won-board/aftermath review boundary uses one measured out-in dissolve: the exact outgoing scene fades fully over the shared 350ms token before the complete prepared successor fades in over the same token, while ordinary Run phases retain their atomic no-blank crossfade and Victory itself uses the shared opacity-only entrance | superseded by [0461](0461-aftermath-crossfades-directly-without-a-homepage-floor.md); partially superseded ADR-0454 and refined ADR-0043/0205/0452/0455 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0459](0459-battle-scenes-reveal-once-and-terminal-review-units-stay-settled.md) | The scene director solely owns a navigated Battle's opacity reveal; ordinary units remain staged until activation and then arrive, while aftermath Back paints its exact terminal units already settled at their seats instead of replaying their entrance; generated and predrawn boards share the same contract | accepted; refines ADR-0205/0357/0455 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0460](0460-a-crafted-terminal-marker-lasts-through-its-victory-surface.md) | A crafted terminal marker remains keyed to its exact Battle for the lifetime of the board-visible Victory surface and clears when Rewards retires that surface, so every render stays terminal, units remain settled, and Victory participates only in the director-owned scene reveal | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0453's immediate-clear timing and refines ADR-0459 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0461](0461-aftermath-crossfades-directly-without-a-homepage-floor.md) | The won-board/aftermath boundary crossfades directly between complete prepared scenes with no transparent midpoint, while the homepage backdrop mounts only for a current or pending homepage scene instead of serving as a universal visual floor | accepted; supersedes ADR-0458, partially supersedes ADR-0454's retire-before-reveal presentation, and refines ADR-0043/0205/0452/0455 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0462](0462-transition-choreography-is-derived-from-scene-ownership.md) | The scene graph derives choreography from presentation ownership: complete scene-owner changes crossfade directly between prepared scenes with no background midpoint, while a selection change may pass through its retained owner's real deselected region without fading that scene's surrounding chrome | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0205/0307/0355/0454 and refines ADR-0206/0207/0461 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0463](0463-continuity-handoffs-settle-with-the-director.md) | A scene-crossing visual is owned by the nearest semantic scene owner that spans its complete handoff and releases when the director settles the destination, whether that transition retires a complete scene or replaces a selection inside a retained owner; Bona Vacantia therefore lifts its carried lipsanon from the mat selection to the Run phase owner, while the closed continuity portal follows director phase/generation instead of a component unmount or guessed timer | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0385's outgoing-component lifetime rule and refines ADR-0462 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0464](0464-forests-are-saved-rerunnable-generator-instances.md) | Forests are named saved rerunnable generator instances: grid area, weighted explicit Scene Art entries, knobs, orientation, and seed persist; selection never generates; explicit Generate/Regenerate replaces only instance-owned output; the retired transient swatch recipe is deleted; absent optional board state means no Forests and needs no Level or database migration | accepted; seed-interaction clause superseded by ADR-0468 and one-recipe-per-Forest clause superseded by ADR-0470; refines ADR-0059/0071/0129 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0465](0465-forest-art-presets-seed-explicit-editable-recipes.md) | The Forest art chooser offers All Trees, Lush Woodland, and Rocky Grove as catalog-aware recipe starters; each replaces only the selected Forest's contents with concrete editable weighted entries, does not change knobs or generate, and is never persisted as a hidden mode | accepted; chooser-placement clause superseded by ADR-0467 and contents-only preset scope superseded by ADR-0470; refines ADR-0071/0464 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0466](0466-town-building-presets-seed-editable-section-recipes.md) | The Town building chooser offers Village Homes, Mill Village, and Castle Borough as catalog-aware section-recipe starters through the shared generator preset control; each expands to concrete editable weighted entries, changes no other section or Town setting, and never generates | accepted; chooser placement superseded by ADR-0467 and section-recipe-only scope superseded by ADR-0469; refines ADR-0059/0464/0465 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0467](0467-generator-recipe-presets-remain-visible.md) | Forest presets remain visible in selected Forest Contents and Town presets in every expanded section, before their explicit rows; expandable art/building grids choose individual sources only, so recipe actions are never hidden behind two near-identical picker states | accepted; Town section-preset placement clause superseded by ADR-0469 and remaining per-Section placement superseded by ADR-0471; supersedes ADR-0465/0466 chooser-placement clauses | 2026-08-05 |
| [0468](0468-placement-generators-randomize-unless-fixed-seed-is-enabled.md) | Forest and Town Generate choose and persist a fresh non-repeating seed by default; the shared Fixed seed toggle is the sole opt-in to visible seed controls and exact replay, and absent optional flags migrate existing instances to automatic mode | accepted; supersedes ADR-0464 seed-interaction clause and refines ADR-0059/0071 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0469](0469-town-presets-compose-plans-and-editable-districts.md) | Whole-Town presets expand before Plan into a global street Plan plus one or more explicit editable Districts and Town settings; each District has independently replaceable building, share, scale, and frontage presets; generation retains one continuous Plan and assigns plots by District share and Blend | superseded by ADR-0470; supersedes ADR-0466 section-recipe-only scope and ADR-0467 Town placement clause; refines ADR-0059/0071/0464/0468 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0470](0470-placement-generator-sections-compose-mixed-and-distinct-approaches.md) | Town and Forest retain one author-drawn outer patch while complete Section-local approaches declare mixed or distinct relationships; one shared seeded composer owns every internal territory, enabling separate, combined, and hybrid layouts without user-managed subareas; presets restore complete Section approaches and Generate remains the sole materialization boundary | accepted; preset ownership and application scope partially superseded by ADR-0471; supersedes ADR-0464 one-recipe Forest clause, ADR-0465 contents-only preset scope, and ADR-0469 global Town Plan/District composition; refines ADR-0059/0071/0468 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0471](0471-placement-presets-replace-container-section-collections.md) | Town and Forest presets live above and replace the container's complete explicit Section collection; simple presets may produce one Section while richer presets produce mixed/distinct multi-Section layouts, every result remains editable, preset identity is not persisted, and only Generate materializes output | accepted; supersedes ADR-0470 Section-local preset scope and ADR-0467 per-Section placement; refines ADR-0059/0071/0468 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0472](0472-forest-generation-bounds-ground-contact-not-sprite-frames.md) | Forest generation keeps each projected root/base footprint inside the selected grid or generated distinct territory while permitting canopy and all other elevated sprite pixels to overhang; one shared inverse-projection primitive owns exact Town and Forest ellipse-bound tests | accepted; frame-derived sizing partially superseded by ADR-0473; refines ADR-0464/0470 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0473](0473-source-art-ground-contact-is-measured-from-base-alpha.md) | Every source-art facing owns base-alpha-measured contact anchor and footprint geometry; candidate metadata and installation preserve it, current accepted natural art is deterministically backfilled, Forest containment uses it, and elevated sprite pixels remain free to overhang | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0472 frame-derived sizing; refines ADR-0085/0173/0464 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0474](0474-continue-resume-action-is-labeled-continue.md) | The final action on Play's Continue surface is labeled Continue, matching the destination and explicitly telling the player that the saved activity will resume; ordinary launch actions elsewhere remain Play | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0356's Play-action label and refines ADR-0294 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0475](0475-run-preparation-actions-follow-their-decision-content.md) | Run preparation keeps its fixed right detail column but places Play or Start Run immediately after the facts or Ataraxia flow they complete; the destructive replacement disclosure stays between selection and action, while Campaign and level previews keep their established placement | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0290's bottom action placement and refines ADR-0289/0291 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0476](0476-generation-references-freeze-the-autosaved-working-copy.md) | A Generation Reference freezes the exact acknowledged Level Editor working-copy revision with immutable pixels and semantic provenance; Save and Publish remain separate canonical/player-visibility boundaries and are never prerequisites for artwork handoff | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0166's canonical-saved-Level capture requirement and refines ADR-0304 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0477](0477-board-art-pipeline-owns-ai-result-ingress.md) | Generation References owns only immutable input capture/copy, while Board Art Pipeline visibly owns **Add AI artwork**: selecting the producing reference and pasting or choosing a valid PNG creates the hidden reference-bound waiting attempt and stages exact bytes for **Use this board**; invalid ingress creates no empty attempt | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0166's Generation-References-owned attempt start and returned-image ingress; refines ADR-0071/0168/0476 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0478](0478-ai-artwork-intake-is-source-agnostic.md) | Generation References remain an optional model-input library, while Board Art Pipeline accepts any valid AI-artwork PNG and binds its exact raw bytes only to the current working-copy processing context; new intake records no producing-reference relation or external-model claim | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0166's mandatory reference binding and ADR-0477's producing-reference selector; refines ADR-0168/0476 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0479](0479-first-time-grid-fitting-starts-from-the-game-grid.md) | First-time raw-artwork fitting immediately shows the Level's centered canonical game grid, and coarse mode can scale the whole grid uniformly around its center without changing its proportions | accepted; refines ADR-0113/0135/0178 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0480](0480-using-a-fitted-grid-saves-and-selects-its-board.md) | **Use fitted board** persists the displayed grid through its immutable corrected raster and selects that result on the working copy in one action; exact-source browser recovery prevents a failed or interrupted create from silently reseeding, while unchanged raw selection explicitly ignores fitting | accepted; refines ADR-0158/0178/0479 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0481](0481-sectio-offers-reveal-the-face-down-pile-beneath-them.md) | Every Sectio offer is the face of a canonical pile over the accepted universal card back; Adlectio sends only that face to the Chartulary and reveals the non-interactive back in its unchanged original seat, so surviving offers stay fixed and the retired FLIP implies no replacement offer or hidden gameplay stock | accepted; hidden-stock clause partially superseded by ADR-0494; supersedes ADR-0388, partially supersedes ADR-0431's survivor-FLIP clauses, and refines ADR-0059/0387/0420 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0482](0482-expunctio-owns-card-aware-alienatio.md) | Expunctio becomes the one card-aware disposal workspace: each canonical held-card face identifies and marks one attached unit at a time through a keyboard-and-touch cycle control, shows its exact Alienatio return, and offers immediate Aliene beside the card-level Athetize choice; the redundant standalone Alienatio destination, route, workspace, filters, and review-screen identity are retired while Prosopography remains the detailed unit ledger and the transaction/persistence model remains unchanged | superseded by ADR-0511 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0483](0483-expunctio-unit-selection-is-explicit.md) | Every Expunctio card begins with no selected unit; the player deliberately selects an exact attached unit by activating its labelled figure or by using Previous/Next, which enter the stable card-seat cycle from either end; selection changes neither position nor scale and uses a stationary high-contrast magenta edge and dark-violet glow distinct from the card paint, while the picker exposes no return and no Aliene action until that presentation-only choice exists | superseded by ADR-0511 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0484](0484-expunctio-unit-pointers-follow-sprite-alpha.md) | Expunctio pointer hover and click follow the installed unit sprite's actual alpha pixels rather than its rectangular semantic-button seat: the renderer reuses its cached source-pixel measurement as a visible-pixel mask, transparent pixels are inert, pointer cursor and glow appear only on visible pixels, and coordinate-free keyboard activation plus the Previous/Next controls remain complete | superseded by ADR-0485; refines ADR-0427/0483 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0485](0485-expunctio-unit-pointer-targets-include-the-visible-outline.md) | Expunctio pointer targets follow the installed unit silhouette plus a small scale-relative halo matching the visible outline instead of requiring an exact opaque source pixel; when neighboring halos overlap, the nearest visible sprite pixel wins, while transparent seat corners remain inert and keyboard/cycle activation remains unchanged | accepted; supersedes ADR-0484 and refines ADR-0427/0483 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0486](0486-run-disposal-prices-use-directional-gold-marks.md) | Expunctio visually distinguishes opposite gold movements with owner-selected native 64×64 PixelLab marks: green-plus gain for Alienatio return and red-minus loss for the Expunctio fee/Paid state; a shared live-asset primitive resolves direction-specific drawables, retains visible labels and directional accessible names, and leaves neutral compact gold amounts unchanged | accepted; refines ADR-0026/0076/0219/0407/0482 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0487](0487-expunctio-selection-swaps-content-within-persistent-seats.md) | Expunctio is a persistent physical panel: before selection it already reserves the exact 64px Alienatio-return seat, 20px trait band, picker, actions, fee, and copy geometry; selecting or cycling a unit swaps contents and the stationary card highlight in place without moving, scaling, wrapping, or animating any control | superseded by ADR-0511 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0488](0488-expunctio-unit-selection-uses-one-blue-mark.md) | Expunctio pointer hover previews one stationary blue silhouette mark, and direct figure activation plus Previous/Next retain that identical mark for the one shared selected-unit state; no input path receives a separate magenta, violet, moved, scaled, or enlarged treatment | superseded by ADR-0511 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0489](0489-alienatio-fades-the-departure-and-flips-the-next-card-frame.md) | A valid Alienatio commits immediately, fades the sold figure over its captured old pixels, composes the exact compact post-sale card frame, and FLIPs each stable surviving unit from its current visual rectangle into its committed seat without disabling input; selection alone remains stationary | superseded by ADR-0508 and ADR-0511 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0490](0490-run-card-units-reveal-a-larger-named-reading.md) | Every occupied same-role unit stack on the canonical Run card face immediately reveals the same player-side sprite at a larger stable size plus its chess role in live prose through one shared fixed tooltip; crossing repeated units does not replace or move the popup, Sectio keeps the complete card as its one action, and unacquired offers never fabricate persistent personal names | accepted; refines ADR-0217/0218/0225/0283 | 2026-08-05 |
| [0491](0491-camera-boundary-can-adopt-the-current-editor-view.md) | Camera authors may zoom and pan the unconstrained editor canvas, then Set from view to persist that exact measured world rectangle through the canonical boundary path; the opening frame is retained, Edit remains active, and the full-interior move surface never paints over the artwork | accepted; refines ADR-0301/0302/0303 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0492](0492-run-cards-are-small-authored-formations-without-abilities.md) | Run cards become a bounded nineteen-card set of authored one-to-three-unit formations; His Grace holds the complete starter triangle, each played card commits its formation atomically with deterministic fallback, and card qualifiers, unit abilities, targeting, ability relics, rarity, and Ataraxia tiers above zero leave the active ruleset | superseded by [0493](0493-generated-run-formations-fall-sideways-and-own-rarity.md) | 2026-08-06 |
| [0493](0493-generated-run-formations-fall-sideways-and-own-rarity.md) | Run cards become a 714-card generated connected formation core plus seven retained authored exceptions; each exact shape prints on a cropped player-view isometric board, formations fall from the right into a two-row band, composition art may repeat during prototyping, and tier-first 75/20/5 rarity uses Standard, white, and steel frames with every opposite-color Bishop pair Rare | accepted; catalog counts and Queen + Pawn exclusion partially superseded by ADR-0502, deal/opening clauses partially superseded by ADR-0494; supersedes ADR-0492's bounded deck, random translation, Standard-only frame, and tabled rarity | 2026-08-06 |
| [0494](0494-runs-begin-in-battle-and-sectio-deals-a-derived-rarity-pile.md) | Runs have no opening Sectio: opening Bona Vacantia leads directly to Battle 1 and the first offers arrive after that win; each seed derives a hidden 180-card pile with exact 135/36/9 rarity quotas, per-rarity unseen-before-recycle queues, natural streaks, three-card consumption or four with Quartermaster's Ledger, and one persisted cursor under RunSaveVersion 26 plus migration 64 | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0321/0322/0338/0347/0368/0371/0481/0493 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0495](0495-rarity-colors-the-standard-frame-metalwork.md) | Rarity is independent from frame type; the Standard family keeps its wood identity while Common uses dark steel, Uncommon colors all existing metalwork light blue, and Rare colors it antique gold; future frame types require a complete rarity triplet | superseded by ADR-0496; partially superseded ADR-0493's white/steel frame mapping and refined ADR-0085/0283 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0496](0496-rarity-colors-the-complete-standard-frame.md) | Rarity remains independent from frame type, while the Standard family's Uncommon and Rare cards use complete light-blue and gold structural frames instead of preserving Common's brown wood; live content openings, geometry, and future frame-family triplets remain shared | superseded by ADR-0497; superseded ADR-0495 and refined ADR-0085/0283/0493 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0497](0497-rarity-colors-the-standard-artwork-frame.md) | Rarity remains independent from frame type; every Standard card keeps the original outer body while Uncommon colors only the illustration bezel light blue and Rare colors it gold, preserving shared anatomy and geometry | accepted; supersedes ADR-0496 and refines ADR-0085/0283/0493 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0498](0498-level-editor-moves-the-playable-grid-within-the-authored-scene.md) | The Level Editor moves the playable grid one tile North, East, South, or West inside existing scenic terrain by transferring opposite extents and atomically rebasing the complete authored scene; canonical zero-based gameplay coordinates remain unchanged, inherited entering terrain is materialized, gameplay-only placements outside the new projection are removed and reported, and accepted pre-drawn registrations remain locked | accepted; refines ADR-0098/0131/0365 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0499](0499-generation-references-may-bake-the-playable-grid.md) | Each immutable Generation Reference explicitly chooses a clean image or the canonical playable grid baked into its exact preview and PNG; clean remains the default, scenic terrain stays ungridded, and operation/provenance records the choice while historical references remain clean | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0166/0476's overlay-free requirement and refines ADR-0109 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0500](0500-scene-art-select-is-local-alpha-aware-and-stack-cycling.md) | Scene Art Select draws no global candidates: hover follows exact rendered alpha and depth, repeated local clicks cycle the unchanged painted overlap stack, and a compact current-object readout replaces the unbounded instance dropdown | accepted; supersedes ADR-0149, partially supersedes ADR-0148's growing Selected dropdown, and refines ADR-0059/0434/0464 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0501](0501-play-surfaces-load-with-the-board-grid-enabled.md) | Every newly mounted play battlefield shows the canonical board-grid overlay by default; Settings > Gameplay owns the device-local Board grid preference, while the existing in-battle Grid and Clear all controls remain temporary overrides for that mounted battlefield | accepted; refines ADR-0059/0353 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0502](0502-queen-and-pawn-receive-the-complete-connected-set.md) | Queen + Pawn is the one ten-material generated roster and receives all six connected two-cell arrangements; the core becomes 720 cards plus six authored exceptions, all six Queen + Pawn cards are Rare, and RunSaveVersion 27 resets only the derived hidden cursor against the expanded catalog | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0493's nine-material exclusion and catalog counts | 2026-08-06 |
| [0503](0503-pawns-arrive-before-their-promotion-choice.md) | A player Pawn visibly completes its move onto the promotion cell before replacement controls appear; manual moves and premoves share that post-arrival boundary, while the arrival remains client-local presentation and the complete chosen move still commits atomically or through the ordered multiplayer relay | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0077's queue-time premove choice and refines ADR-0072; premove clause partially superseded by ADR-0541 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0504](0504-promotion-choices-stay-with-the-arrived-pawn.md) | The post-arrival promotion question is one blocking inner-chrome callout anchored beside the highlighted Pawn, opens toward the board middle, cancels camera zoom for stable readable controls, and leaves no duplicate choice card in the Controls rail | accepted; supersedes ADR-0503's Controls-rail placement and refines ADR-0059/0102 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0505](0505-promotion-opens-without-a-preselected-looking-choice.md) | The arrived-Pawn picker opens with no replacement button programmatically focused, so Queen, Rook, Bishop, and Knight begin with equal visual weight while deliberate keyboard navigation still receives the normal focus treatment | accepted; supersedes ADR-0504's first-choice autofocus clause | 2026-08-06 |
| [0506](0506-card-gold-groups-use-the-open-rail-divider.md) | Cards and the Chartulary group every live gold-value section under one shared text-free open-rail ornament; the existing live coin sits in its cradle, while exact PixelLab bytes use a SHA- and geometry-bound three-slice scaling exception from live storage | accepted; refines ADR-0002/0059/0076/0085/0364/0371 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0507](0507-card-gold-divider-fitting-is-a-studio-viewer.md) | Card gold-divider fitting is a click-reachable typed Studio Viewer that renders the real shared divider at canonical widths, resets from the runtime's Git-owned geometry, and saves only that fixed file through a named-dev loopback admin operation; player-facing galleries expose no tuning UI | accepted; refines ADR-0057/0058/0071/0506 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0508](0508-alienatio-leaves-the-authored-formation-seat-vacant.md) | Alienatio keeps the sold figure's in-place continuity fade but leaves its authored formation cell vacant; every survivor retains its indexed cell, scale, and identity, and the obsolete compact-face projection and FLIP reflow are retired across Expunctio and Deployment | superseded by ADR-0511; supersedes ADR-0489's compact post-sale frame and survivor FLIP; refines ADR-0283/0427/0487 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0509](0509-run-formations-summon-off-board-before-sliding.md) | A Run formation first reuses the staggered unit summon at its fully off-board entry seats; only after the final unit has landed does the complete rigid shape slide toward decreasing board x into its persisted destination | accepted; refines ADR-0045 and ADR-0493 | 2026-08-07 |
| [0510](0510-enchiridion-cards-filters-rarity-on-structural-teal.md) | Enchiridion Cards and the held-card Chartulary add an independent Common/Uncommon/Rare filter beside Gold and Contains; the shared pinned row is one structural teal field whose three separately phased closed dropdowns use the canonical oak leaf surface while popup bodies remain structural | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0313/0364's two-filter inventory and refines ADR-0371/0433/0493 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0511](0511-held-cards-are-immutable-formations.md) | Held cards remain intact formations: Alienatio/Aliene, Fair Scales, The Paid Crossing, promotion cash-out, unit selection/reflow, and the gain mark retire; Expunctio only athetizes a whole eligible card, while RunSaveVersion 28 and migration 66 reset an open Sectio to entry and retire the complete installed-content graph | accepted; supersedes ADR-0432/0482/0483/0487/0488/0489/0508 and partially supersedes ADR-0332/0393/0407/0485/0486 | 2026-08-06 |
| [0512](0512-run-creation-selects-automatic-or-arranged-formations.md) | Every Run explicitly selects Arrange formations or Automatic formations at preparation; arranged Deployment reveals the complete dealt hand, permits any-order quarter-turn placement and free replacement of rigid cards, admits capacity as a whole-card prefix, requires His Grace's King before Begin Battle, and blocks deliberately unplaced formations, while version-28 Runs migrate to Automatic under RunSaveVersion 29 and migration 68 | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0493's mandatory automatic settling and ADR-0509's universal choreography; refines ADR-0290/0422/0493 | 2026-08-07 |
| [0513](0513-campaign-play-entry-is-dormant.md) | Campaign remains implemented and directly routable but its ordinary Play rail, Continue resume entry, and reserved scroll region are hidden behind one Git-owned switch; the visible Play menu collapses to Continue, Skirmish, Run, and Levels | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0074/0356 and refines ADR-0059 | 2026-08-07 |
| [0514](0514-play-opens-run-without-a-mode-rail.md) | Run is the sole player-facing mode: Play paints and canonicalizes directly to Run preparation, the redundant source rail disappears, and Run action/detail columns shift one full seat left while Campaign, Skirmish, Levels, Continue, and their direct routes remain implemented for development | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0074/0356/0513 and refines ADR-0059/0290 | 2026-08-07 |
| [0515](0515-player-arranges-rotation-canonical-cards-from-a-complete-shuffle.md) | Player arrangement is the sole Deployment rule; translated quarter-turn-equivalent formations collapse to a 272-card live offer catalog while reflections remain distinct, and Sectio temporarily deals complete random catalog shuffles without rarity quotas under RunSaveVersion 30 and migration 69 | accepted; supersedes ADR-0512, partially supersedes ADR-0493/0494/0509, and refines ADR-0422 | 2026-08-07 |
| [0516](0516-the-run-opens-with-a-formation-card-grant-on-a-band-deep-enough-to-turn.md) | A deployment band is as deep as its level authored it and a seat's row is a board row rather than a lane index, so quarter turns are limited by geometry instead of a two-row clamp; the Run's opening Bona Vacantia grants one of three free formation cards worth 4 to 6 instead of a lipsanon, under RunSaveVersion 31 | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0368's opening lipsanon and refines ADR-0515 | 2026-08-07 |
| [0516](0516-the-playable-grid-is-placed-over-ai-artwork-by-hand.md) | The playable grid's SIZE and PLACEMENT over AI artwork are owner decisions rather than artwork properties: in `ai` mode the Board page authors **Width**, **Height** and a four-way **Move grid over artwork** pad while every baked-environment family — terrain, Subterrain, paths, props, fences, walls, wall art, Placed Art — stays locked and suppressed because those pixels genuinely are the plate's, and those declared playable-window operations commit through a path exempt from the baked-art signature guard, since rebasing or pruning coordinates is the mechanical consequence of what the owner asked for rather than an edit contradicting the plate. Sliding moves the PICTURE, not the scene: the Level records an owner-authored `predrawnPlateOffset` in projected board pixels and one shared render seam (`predrawnRenderSurface`) folds it into the surface's world bounds for the editor plate, gameplay, both thumbnail renderers and the occlusion depth map, so no scenic apron has to exist first and no unit, prop or zone tile is dropped by a slide — while the selection's own `worldBounds` are never rewritten, because they are part of the artifact's identity that every lineage check compares, and **Reset** returns the offset to the artwork's own registration. `predrawnGridDetached` records deliberate detachment as a STATE, not a defect: it skips only the environment-geometry comparison — the single question the owner has just answered — while artifact resolution, lineage, stage completeness, archive status and the schema-v3 move-highlight profile's binding all still fail closed, an uncalibrated profile cell falls back to the full-cell highlight instead of invalidating the selection, and setting a newly generated artifact clears both the detachment and the offset. A grow seeds every new square with the terrain of its nearest authored neighbour | accepted | 2026-08-07 |
| [0517](0517-a-player-en-passant-pays-a-five-gold-bounty.md) | A player en passant capture in a Run Battle pays five gold at the moment it commits, per capture and player-only; the capture event carries an `enPassant` flag so surrounding layers can recognize it, board law and the save shape are unchanged, and Undo reverses the bounty while Restart does not | accepted; refines ADR-0193/0220/0394/0424 | 2026-08-07 |
| [0518](0518-the-board-assembles-obstacles-before-armies.md) | Placed ROCKS take part in the board's entrance, landing on the same fall curve as the units and starting before them, so the reveal order becomes ground → obstacles → armies running from the far corner toward the player — ONE continuous beat, not three, because on a rock-heavy board the first units are already arriving while the last rocks are still falling, and that overlap is what makes the start read as a position being built rather than a stage being dressed. ADR-0045 drew the opposite line ("neutral rocks are scenery, not deploying units → no drop") while choreographing an army's DEPLOYMENT; a rock is not scenery here, it is the obstacle that shapes the position. One motion, not a second one: rocks call the same `arrivalOffset()` on the `drop` track, so ADR-0045 §A/§B still govern the physics and the unit choreography is untouched — same base, same wave gap, same per-unit stagger. Obstacles order by DEPTH (`x + y`, the order their ops already paint in) rather than by home edge, because order teaches mine-vs-theirs and an obstacle has no side to teach, and `STRUCTURE_ARRIVAL_BASE_MS` (130ms) frees the first rock well before `ARRIVAL_BASE_MS` (400ms) frees the first unit. `structureArrives()` is the single predicate and admits `kind === 'rock'` only — a house falling out of the sky is a different claim about the world, and widening the set is a one-line change there. `BoardDrawOp.structure` (`{key, kind, x, y}`) stamps prop identity onto every op an anchor emits so a flat-contact prop's two clipped depth halves take the same offset and cannot shear apart mid-fall; it is identity metadata that changes no pixels, so thumbnails, bakes and every still consumer are byte-unaffected. Props run the units' own `unitArrivalPlan()` lifecycle keyed by anchor cell, so a battlefield prepared behind a scene transition never reveals a rock at a seat it has not arrived in, a settled review owns no prop plans, and a retained battlefield does not re-drop standing rocks. No new art (an arriving rock is the existing sprite under a transform), no authored field, no content migration and no editor surface — dropping is a property of the KIND resolved from `propDef().kind`, so every rock already placed gets the entrance without being re-saved | accepted; refines ADR-0045's scenery-does-not-deploy clause | 2026-08-07 |
| [0519](0519-an-expired-sign-in-pauses-the-level-editor.md) | An expired sign-in under an open Level Editor document pauses cloud autosave instead of failing or closing it: the 401 is classified by the shared session owner, the board stays mounted and keeps writing browser recovery under the document's owner, the same account signing back in reconnects and syncs the edits made while signed out, and a browser branch mount declines to adopt is offered for restore/export/discard | accepted; refines ADR-0304/0306 | 2026-08-07 |
| [0520](0520-card-art-is-keyed-to-the-formation-and-may-come-from-either-generator.md) | Units-card art is keyed to (footprint, roster) rather than roster alone, giving 94 illustrations across 272 cards; the identity set is derived from the live deck, either PixelLab or Codex may produce a family with resampling recorded rather than claimed native, and each family is promoted on its own proof instead of one atomic 49-card group | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0282's roster keying, single generator, and atomic group; extends ADR-0516 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0521](0521-an-unread-artwork-version-list-is-not-a-verdict.md) | A pre-drawn level's artwork version list failing to READ is a retrying transport state (`unreachable`), never a finding about the artwork: a 401 is classified by the shared session owner, recovery rides ADR-0519's online/focus/visibility/20s signals plus the session snapshot, the failure announces itself once outside the Level Artwork panel, and the plate stays hidden but temporary rather than blanked for the life of the page | accepted; refines ADR-0306/0519 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0522](0522-run-card-rows-are-sized-from-their-lane-not-a-fixed-track.md) | Bona Vacantia and Sectio card rows measure their lane and print a tuned share of the largest 5:7 face that fits it in both axes, instead of an auto-fit ladder that never saw the lane; the share, px ceiling, and gutter are Git-owned and tuned in a Pages-shaped Studio Card Size dressing room that iframes the real Run route and auditions through injected custom properties | accepted; refines ADR-0029/0057/0058/0507/0516 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0523](0523-card-rarity-is-a-material-band-adjusted-by-footprint.md) | Card rarity is a material band (Common through 4, Uncommon 5-6, Rare above) with the five awkward four-cell footprints dropped one tier and opposite-colour Bishop pairs excepted; a pile is an exact 16/3/1 quota rather than a per-card roll, and the Sectios after Battles 1 and 2 cap card cost at six so early gold converts into cards instead of banking, under RunSaveVersion 32 and migration 71 | accepted; supersedes ADR-0515's rarity-free pile and partially supersedes ADR-0493/0494 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0524](0524-the-player-chooses-which-back-their-run-deals.md) | The Run card back is a player setting: `runCardBack` in Settings → Gameplay offers six opaque native 1060×1484 backs chosen by sight, The King's Position ships as the default, the card back becomes a `ui/run/card-back/<id>.png` slot family whose `runtime.variant` must name its own slot, `standard.png` stays as the availability-critical fallback and is not re-pointed, and an unresolvable stored choice falls back to it rather than throwing | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0420's single universal back and Fivefold Gambit default | 2026-08-08 |
| [0525](0525-a-run-change-inside-a-live-battle-cannot-be-silent.md) | The Run's committed-board transform returns `{ game, notices }` and every store commit folds those notices into the Battle log, so a Run change reaching a live Battle cannot be silent; the paying/spawning model functions hand back the notice welded to the document, a gold notice also seats a rising `+5` marker over its cell and sounds the owner-authored `gold` cue, and the Reservist arrival is narrated for the first time | accepted; refines ADR-0517/0193/0047/0089 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0526](0526-a-formation-is-carried-on-the-cursor.md) | An arranged-Deployment formation is carried on the cursor: every square takes the pointer, the pointed square resolves to a seating that covers it, the formation hangs from a grip seat at its centre of mass rather than its empty bounding-box corner, the highlight is the footprint over a band the level owns, and the pointer hides beneath it; a secondary press that releases without panning turns it clockwise and Q/E turn it both ways, each spinning it inside the box it stands in rather than walking that box across the band, and each walking only the turns it can actually hold, while the secondary drag remains pan-only; the hand shows one card at full panel width with its steppers beneath it, turning is two direction buttons rather than four angles, and every such control wears its shortcut key (W/S, Q/E) in the shared cap, a seated formation is taken back by clicking it, the whole dealt hand is read in a Chartulary that opens narrowed to this Battle, the Controls scroll on the house KitScroll rail, and a pinned line counts the admitted hand onto the board and turns to a green check when the last is seated | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0515's anchor placement and refines ADR-0128 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0527](0527-a-royal-fork-pays-one-gold.md) | A player move that lands a royal fork -- one piece attacking the enemy King and, from the same square, a Rook or Queen -- pays one gold at the moment it commits, per fork and player-only; the bar is read off `PIECE_VALUE.rook`, a discovered check is two pieces' work and pays nothing, defence of the victim is not asked, board law and the save shape are unchanged, and Undo reverses the bounty | accepted; refines ADR-0193/0517/0525 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0528](0528-an-installed-board-plate-is-a-selection-not-an-absence.md) | An installed board plate — a hand-placed painting registered by four dragged corners — is a settled, drawable selection with its own `plate` kind rather than a `missing` one; it asks the server nothing because it has no lineage to prove, drawability is asked as `predrawnSelectionIsDrawable` instead of comparing against `valid` at each gate, one `predrawnSelectionSeed` answers for mount/load/commit/undo/redo, and AI artwork mode is selectable for a plate. Fortress Gate, the last one, was then migrated by solving its own projective map and resampling onto its saved viewing pane, leaving 0 plates canonical; decoding one is kept so restoring an earlier revision brings its artwork back | accepted; refines ADR-0521 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0529](0529-a-battle-row-is-a-level-row.md) | A War Battle draws the same `EditorLevelRow` a campaign level does — board thumbnail, goal line, and carved edit / reorder / delete — instead of a hand-rolled parallel with a bare digit and typed arrows; both official campaigns were published empty so all 30 of their levels fall into Unassigned levels, the Editor rail stops advertising campaign rows entirely (listing only the campaign the address names, a display rule that leaves every campaign verb and route intact), and the Skirmish profiles level class with its Editor collection, Play panel, `/play/select/skirmish` address and `skirmish` mode entry are retired, with those levels surviving as ordinary standalone ones | accepted; refines ADR-0059/0091/0280, partially supersedes ADR-0074/0514 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0530](0530-his-grace-keeps-royal-purple-and-a-priceless-coin-is-struck-with-a-crown.md) | His Grace takes the owner-selected royal-purple Praecipuus frame again — ADR-0492's Standard-only rule had taken it away as a side effect of retiring card properties, not as a decision about this card — and Praecipuus owes no rarity triplet because nothing but the starter ever resolves it; a coin carrying no price is struck with a crown in the numeral's own seat instead of printing blank, from one transparent native 64×64 `ui/run/card-prototypes/cost-crown-v1.png` seated in a square `.75` of the drawn coin so the raster's own transparent margin is the layout, and smoothly resampled rather than `image-rendering: pixelated` because the mark draws at about a sixth of native and nearest-neighbour at that ratio decimates it into a smear, read identically by the card face, both gallery band dividers and the gold filter chips, resolving optionally so an unaccepted mark prints the old blank coin and stays outside the face's readiness protocol, its share of the coin tuned by a `mark.fill` slider in Studio > Card Gold Divider beside the coin's own seat, with Reset/Save/Copy, and every uploaded mark mounted together there on the real band and a real His Grace face as selectable catalog cards rather than one review address per candidate | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0492's Standard-frame rule, restores ADR-0413/0414, refines ADR-0495/0506 | 2026-08-08 |
| [0531](0531-a-craft-link-keeps-its-address-and-presents-the-run.md) | A `/run/craft/<id>` link keeps its own address instead of being spent on arrival, so the browser's reload button re-crafts as many times as it is pressed; the Run screen PRESENTS the Run address the link names through `presentedRunAddress`, which the scene graph, the title bar and the screen all read, and an address repair defers to a craft in flight or refused | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0354's spent-on-arrival address | 2026-08-08 |
| [0532](0532-a-bishop-costs-a-rarity-band-because-the-pair-is-assembled-by-hand.md) | A card carrying any Bishop moves up one rarity tier after material and footprint have set the band, because free placement (ADR-0526) lets the player choose the square every Bishop lands on, so any two Bishops they own become the opposite-colour pair whichever cards those arrived on; card-local `(x + y) % 2` parity is deleted rather than widened, no Bishop card is Common, every two-Bishop card is Rare, the Bishop's band and the awkward footprint's cancel, and the six-gold opening ceiling stops emptying the Rare tier so the capped pile keeps the full 16/3/1 quota. Bishop cards fall from 8.5 to 1.6 per 20-card pile and Common narrows from 47 identities to 29; rarity is derived, so no RunSaveVersion bump | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0523's Bishop-pair exemption and emptied-tier claim | 2026-08-08 |
| [0533](0533-a-seated-formation-is-a-plan-until-battle.md) | A Run Deployment formation the player has seated is PLANNED, not deployed: it is drawn at the same `PLANNED_UNIT_OPACITY` the carried formation is drawn at, is held out of the compositor's arrival ledger via `SkirmishBoardSurfaceState.plannedPieceIds`, and therefore spends no entrance when it is placed, turned, or taken back; Begin Battle promotes the plan into the live match on the same mounted board, where ADR-0351's identity ledger sees the whole army as newly visible and gives it ADR-0045's staggered drop, voiced by a `voiceDeployRollCall` now separated from `preserveBoardPresentation`'s silence | accepted; partially supersedes ADR-0351's placement-is-an-entry-beat rule for arranged Deployment formations | 2026-08-08 |
